📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 移植后 1、3 和 5 年,HBV 阳性和 HBV 阴性肾脏受者之间的同种异体移植存活风险比没有统计学差异,汇总估计值分别为 1 [0.98–1.01]、1.01 [0.98–1.04] 和 1.00 [0.91–1.09]。两组患者的生存率也相当。HBV 传播 的发生率较低,为 4.94%,特别是在使用预防措施以及受者在移植前进行免疫时。HBV 感染供体的肾脏与 HBV 阴性供体的肾脏具有相似的患者和同种异体移植存活率,可用于扩大供体库,特别是在适当预防的免疫 KTR 中。
  • 抗病毒药物 核苷(酸)类似物治疗可改善 HBV 相关 HCC 的长期生存率。与未经治疗的患者相比,接受治疗的患者具有更高的 3 年和 5 年总生存 (OS) 率。然而,抗病毒药物 疗法目前在美国还不是 HCC 的 治疗 标准,其在临床实践中的使用情况仍然报道不足。
  • 与连续治疗相比,有限疗程的NA治疗可能增加HBsAg丢失的概率,尽管证据确定性较低。研究发现,停药与HBsAg丢失的风险比为5.02(95% CI:0.47-53.47),相当于每1000名患者约有53例额外的HBsAg丢失。然而,有限疗程显著增加了病毒学复发(RR 23.09)和再次治疗(RR 21.94)的风险,同时降低了持续停药后的病毒学应答率(RR 0.46)。未报告肝功能失代偿或严重不良事件。
  • 该平台在所有目标上均显示出可比的 log 10 VL 和可接受的精度。方差分析显示 HBV 和 HCV 没有显着差异,而 HIV 的 VL 值有显着差异。一致性分析确定了平台比较中的恒定偏差、比例偏差和变异性。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • At 1, 3, and 5 years post-transplant, the risk ratio for allograft survival was not statistically different between recipients of HBV positive and HBV negative kidneys, with pooled estimates of 1 [0.98–1.01], 1.01 [0.98–1.04], and 1.00 [0.91–1.09] respectively.
  • Antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogues is associated with improved long-term survival in HBV-related HCC. Treated patients had higher 3- and 5-year overall survival (OS) rates compared to untreated patients.
  • Finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss compared to continuous therapy, although the evidence is of low certainty. [10.1111/jvh.70243].
  • The platforms showed comparable log 10 VL and acceptable precision across all targets. ANOVA showed no significant differences for HBV and HCV, while HIV showed a significant difference in VL values.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • FDA于2026年11月5日批准贝匹罗韦森(Hibsago)作为首个功能性治愈慢性乙型肝炎的药物,基于3期临床试验结果。
  • FDA批准贝匹罗韦森用于治疗慢性乙型肝炎,适用于无肝硬化且接受核苷类似物治疗的患者。
  • 3期试验显示,19%的患者在治疗后达到功能性治愈,而安慰剂组为0%。
  • 药物通过每周注射给药,可与现有治疗结合,部分患者可停药。
  • On November 5, 2026, the FDA approved bepirovirsen (Hibsago) as the first drug offering a 'functional cure' for chronic hepatitis B, based on phase 3 trial results.
  • The FDA approved bepirovirsen for the treatment of chronic hepatitis B in adults without cirrhosis who are on nucleoside analog therapy.
  • Phase 3 trials showed a 19% functional cure rate in patients with HBsAg levels at or below 3,000 IU/mL, compared to 0% in the placebo group.
  • The drug is administered via weekly injections and can be used alongside existing treatments, allowing some patients to discontinue pills.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Liver international : official journal of the International Association for the Study of the Liver  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/liv.70918  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 46, 11, e70918
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

GST-HG131在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中的安全性、耐受性和功能性治愈活性:一项随机、双盲IIa期研究。

Safety, Tolerability and Antiviral Activity of GST-HG131 in Chronic Hepatitis B: A Randomized, Double-Blind Phase IIa Study.
作者： Yuming Guo, Zhe Xu, Yuanyuan Li, John Mao, Tianxiang Zhang, Fanping Meng, Junliang Fu, Dong Ji, Yingjie Ji, Yongqian Cheng
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然是全球主要的健康负担。由于 HBsAg 清除率低、病毒复制模板(共价闭合环状 DNA (共价闭合环状DNA))的持续存在以及终生 治疗 的需要,目前的疗法很少实现 功能性治愈。GST-HG131 是一种一流的口服生物可利用的 PAPD5/7 小分子抑制剂,PAPD5/7 是参与 HBV mRNA 稳定和 HBsAg 产生的关键 宿主 因子。我们在一项随机、双盲、安慰剂对照 IIa 期研究中评估了 GST-HG131 在接受核苷(酸)类似物(NUC)治疗的 CHB 患者中的安全性、耐受性和 抗病毒药物 活性。方法:这项双盲、随机、安慰剂对照 IIa 期研究评估了 GST-HG131 在 45 名慢性乙型肝炎患者中的疗效和安全性。队列研究 1 和 2(n = 10 名;8 名活性药物,2 名安慰剂)分别接受 30 mg 或 60 mg BID,持续 28 天。队列研究 3(n = 25;20 名活性药物,5 名安慰剂)接受 30 mg BID,为期 12 周。所有参与者均接受核苷(酸)类似物单药治疗超过 6 个月,HBsAg 介于 100 至 1500 IU/mL 之间,血清 ALT 低于 1×ULN。结果:第 28 天时,30 mg BID 28 天组的 HBsAg 减少量为 -0.65 log10 IU/mL,60 mg BID 28 天组的 HBsAg 减少量为 -0.53 log10 IU/mL。与安慰剂相比,30 mg BID 12 周组在 治疗 结束时显示出更大的降低 -0.89 log10 IU/mL(HBsAg 降低 50%,最大降低 1.64 log10 IU/mL。值得注意的是,12 周组中有 76.5% 的患者达到 HBsAg 低于 100 IU/mL。HBsAg 反弹在 治疗 停药后观察到,GST-HG131 的耐受性良好,所有 队列研究 均未出现剂量相关的毒性或新出现的安全性问题。治疗,支持 GST-HG131 的进一步临床开发,作为 CHB 的 功能性治愈 策略的组成部分。临床试验注册号:NCT06263959。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)仍然是全球主要的健康负担。由于HBsAg清除率低、病毒复制模板——共价闭合环状DNA(共价闭合环状DNA)的持续存在以及需要终身治疗,现有疗法很少能实现功能性治愈。
设计与对象
这项双盲、随机、安慰剂对照的IIa期研究评估了GST-HG131在45名慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中的疗效和安全性。所有参与者均接受了超过6个月的核苷(酸)类似物单药治疗,HBsAg水平在100至1500 IU/mL之间,且血清ALT低于1× ULN。
核心方法
组1和组2(各10名患者;8名接受治疗,2名接受安慰剂)分别接受了30 mg或60 mg每日两次(BID)治疗28天。组3(25名患者;20名接受治疗,5名接受安慰剂)接受了30 mg BID治疗12周。
主要结果
在第28天时,30 mg每日两次28天组的HBsAg水平降低了-0.65 log10 IU/mL,而60 mg每日两次28天组的HBsAg水平降低了-0.53 log10 IU/mL。在治疗结束时,12周组的HBsAg水平比安慰剂组减少了更多的-0.89 log10 IU/mL。值得注意的是,12周组中有76.5%的患者实现了HBsAg低于100 IU/mL。
解释与创新
在12周的治疗后观察到最大的功能性治愈活性,支持进一步开展GST-HG131在慢性乙型肝炎功能性治愈策略中的临床开发。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,GST-HG131可能是一种有前景的候选药物,有助于改善慢性乙型肝炎患者中HBsAg的清除,从而可能为功能性治愈策略做出贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究样本量较小且治疗时间较短,这可能影响其结果的普遍性。由于研究设计和有限的随访时间,证据强度中等。

Safety, Tolerability and Antiviral Activity of GST-HG131 in Chronic Hepatitis B: A Randomized, Double-Blind Phase IIa Study.

Authors: Yuming Guo, Zhe Xu, Yuanyuan Li, John Mao, Tianxiang Zhang, Fanping Meng, Junliang Fu, Dong Ji, Yingjie Ji, Yongqian Cheng
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a major global health burden. Current therapies rarely achieve a functional cure due to low rates of HBsAg clearance, the persistence of the viral replication template-covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA)-and the need for lifelong treatment. GST-HG131 is a first-in-class, orally bioavailable small-molecule inhibitor of PAPD5/7, key host factors involved in HBV mRNA stabilization and HBsAg production. We evaluated the safety, tolerability and antiviral activity of GST-HG131 in patients with CHB receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue (NUC) therapy in a randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled Phase IIa study. METHODS: This double-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled Phase IIa study evaluated the efficacy and safety of GST-HG131 in 45 CHB patients. Cohorts 1 and 2 (n = 10 each; 8 active, 2 placebo) received 30 mg or 60 mg BID for 28 days, respectively. Cohort 3 (n = 25; 20 active, five placebo) received 30 mg BID for 12 weeks. All participants were on nucleos(t)ide analog monotherapy for more than 6 months, with HBsAg between 100 and 1500 IU/mL, and serum ALT below 1× ULN. RESULTS: At Day 28, HBsAg reductions were -0.65 log10 IU/mL in the 30 mg BID 28-day group and -0.53 log10 IU/mL in the 60 mg BID 28-day group. The 30 mg BID 12-week group showed a greater reduction of -0.89 log10 IU/mL at the end of treatment versus placebo (p 50% reduction in HBsAg with a maximum decrease of 1.64 log10 IU/mL. Notably, 76.5% of patients in the 12-week group achieved HBsAg below 100 IU/mL. Rebounds in HBsAg were observed following treatment discontinuation. GST-HG131 was well tolerated, with no dose-related toxicity or emerging safety concerns across all cohorts. CONCLUSION: GST-HG131, a first-in-class oral PAPD5/7 inhibitor, demonstrated favourable safety and significant HBsAg reduction in patients with CHB. The greatest antiviral activity was observed after 12 weeks of treatment, supporting further clinical development of GST-HG131 as a component of functional cure strategies for CHB. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov identifier: NCT06263959.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a major global health burden. Current therapies rarely achieve a functional cure due to low rates of HBsAg clearance, the persistence of the viral replication template-covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA)-and the need for lifelong treatment.
Design and population
This double-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled Phase IIa study evaluated the efficacy and safety of GST-HG131 in 45 CHB patients. All participants were on nucleos(t)ide analog monotherapy for more than 6 months, with HBsAg between 100 and 1500 IU/mL, and serum ALT below 1× ULN.
Core methods
Cohorts 1 and 2 (n = 10 each; 8 active, 2 placebo) received 30 mg or 60 mg BID for 28 days, respectively. Cohort 3 (n = 25; 20 active, five placebo) received 30 mg BID for 12 weeks.
Main results
At Day 28, HBsAg reductions were -0.65 log10 IU/mL in the 30 mg BID 28-day group and -0.53 log10 IU/mL in the 60 mg BID 28-day group. The 30 mg BID 12-week group showed a greater reduction of -0.89 log10 IU/mL at the end of treatment versus placebo. Notably, 76.5% of patients in the 12-week group achieved HBsAg below 100 IU/mL.
Interpretation and novelty
The greatest antiviral activity was observed after 12 weeks of treatment, supporting further clinical development of GST-HG131 as a component of functional cure strategies for CHB.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that GST-HG131 may be a promising candidate for improving HBsAg clearance in patients with chronic hepatitis B, potentially contributing to functional cure strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a small sample size and short treatment duration, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the study design and limited follow-up.

慢性乙型肝炎 中 PD-1 抑制剂 Cetrelimab 的随机、双盲、安慰剂对照研究。

A Randomized, Double-Blind, Placebo-Controlled Study of the PD-1 Inhibitor Cetrelimab in Chronic Hepatitis B.
作者： Tarik Asselah, Thomas N Kakuda, Sarah Kulke, Evelyn Walenta, Robin J Halter, Jay Ariyawansa, Simon Verheijden, Oliver Lenz, Zuleima Aguilar, Isabelle Lonjon-Domanec
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 诱导的 T 细胞耗竭可以使用程序性死亡受体 1 (PD-1) 抑制剂来逆转,例如西曲利单抗(一种针对 PD-1 的研究性全人单克隆抗体)。旨在评估低剂量(即低于肿瘤学中使用的剂量)西曲利单抗在稳定核苷(酸)类似物(NA)病毒学抑制(VS)的 慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)参与者中的安全性、活性和药代动力学。皮下或静脉注射西曲利单抗的随机、双盲、安慰剂对照、多 队列研究、单剂量递增研究。由于战略决策,而非出于任何安全考虑,仅执行了一次 队列研究(45 毫克皮下注射)。参与者保持 NA 治疗 以避免 HBV 再激活。收集了 24 周内的血清西曲利单抗浓度、HBV 生物标志物、PD-1 受体占用率 (RO) 和安全性。11 名参与者被随机入组,其中 8 名参与者接受西曲利单抗,3 名参与者接受安慰剂,所有 11 名参与者均完成了研究。西曲利单抗的平均半衰期为 16.3 天,给药后达到最大西曲利单抗浓度的中位时间为 10.4 天。给药后 6 小时达到接近最大 RO,并随着时间的推移而减弱。乙型肝炎生物标志物或实验室值(包括肝功能测试和促甲状腺激素)没有临床相关的变化。没有报告临床相关的不良事件。患有 CHB 的 VS 参与者皮下注射单次 45 mg 剂量的西曲利单抗是安全且耐受性良好的。HBV 生物标志物没有变化,西曲利单抗治疗 HBV 的进一步开发被放弃。临床试验注册:ClinicalTrials.gov:NCT05242445;EudraCT 编号:2021-004857-21。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估低剂量西曲利单抗在稳定核苷(酸)类似物病毒学抑制的 慢性乙型肝炎 受试者中的安全性、活性和药代动力学。
设计与对象
该研究是一项皮下或静脉注射西曲利单抗的随机、双盲、安慰剂对照、多 队列研究、单剂量递增试验。由于战略决策,仅执行了一颗队列研究(45毫克皮下注射)。参与者维持其核苷(酸)类似物 治疗 以避免 HBV 再激活。
核心方法
该研究收集了 24 周内的血清西曲利单抗浓度、HBV 生物标志物、PD-1 受体占用率和安全性数据。通过稳定的核苷(酸)类似物对 慢性乙型肝炎 进行病毒学抑制的参与者皮下注射单次 45 mg 剂量的西曲利单抗。
主要结果
未观察到 HBV 生物标志物或实验室值(包括肝功能测试和促甲状腺激素)发生临床相关变化。没有报告临床相关的不良事件。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,在病毒学受到抑制的 慢性乙型肝炎 受试者中皮下注射单次 45 mg 西曲利单抗是安全且耐受性良好的。他们还指出,乙型肝炎生物标志物没有变化,导致放弃进一步开发乙型肝炎的西曲利单抗。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,西曲利单抗可能无法有效改变病毒学抑制的 慢性乙型肝炎 受试者的 HBV 生物标志物,这对开发 PD-1 抑制剂作为 HBV 治疗药物具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小,只有 11 名参与者,其中只有 8 名参与者接受了西曲利单抗治疗。由于缺乏比较组和研究持续时间短,证据受到限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

A Randomized, Double-Blind, Placebo-Controlled Study of the PD-1 Inhibitor Cetrelimab in Chronic Hepatitis B.

Authors: Tarik Asselah, Thomas N Kakuda, Sarah Kulke, Evelyn Walenta, Robin J Halter, Jay Ariyawansa, Simon Verheijden, Oliver Lenz, Zuleima Aguilar, Isabelle Lonjon-Domanec
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV)-induced T-cell exhaustion may be reversed using a programmed death receptor 1 (PD-1) inhibitor such as cetrelimab, an investigational fully human monoclonal antibody targeting PD-1. To evaluate the safety, activity and pharmacokinetics of low-dose (i.e., a dose lower than that used in oncology) cetrelimab in participants with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) virologically suppressed (VS) on a stable nucleos(t)ide analog (NA). Randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled, multi-cohort, single-dose escalation study of cetrelimab administered subcutaneously or intravenously. Due to a strategic decision, and not to any safety concern, only one cohort (45 mg subcutaneous) was executed. Participants maintained their NA treatment to avoid HBV reactivation. Serum cetrelimab concentrations, HBV biomarkers, PD-1 receptor occupancy (RO) and safety were collected over 24 weeks. Eleven participants were enrolled and randomized, eight to cetrelimab and three to placebo and all 11 completed the study. Mean cetrelimab half-life was 16.3 days with median time to maximum cetrelimab concentration 10.4 days post-dose. Near-maximal RO was achieved 6 h post-dose which waned over time. There were no clinically relevant changes in HBV biomarkers or laboratory values including liver function tests and thyroid-stimulating hormone. No clinically relevant adverse events were reported. A single 45 mg dose of cetrelimab administered subcutaneously in VS participants with CHB was safe and well tolerated. There was no change in HBV biomarkers and further development of cetrelimab for HBV was abandoned. Clinical Trial Registration: ClinicalTrials.gov: NCT05242445; EudraCT Number: 2021-004857-21.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the safety, activity and pharmacokinetics of low-dose cetrelimab in participants with chronic hepatitis B virologically suppressed on a stable nucleos(t)ide analog.
Design and population
The study was a randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled, multi-cohort, single-dose escalation trial of cetrelimab administered subcutaneously or intravenously. Due to a strategic decision, only one cohort (45 mg subcutaneous) was executed. Participants maintained their nucleos(t)ide analog treatment to avoid HBV reactivation.
Core methods
The study collected serum cetrelimab concentrations, HBV biomarkers, PD-1 receptor occupancy and safety data over 24 weeks. A single 45 mg dose of cetrelimab was administered subcutaneously to participants with chronic hepatitis B virologically suppressed on a stable nucleos(t)ide analog.
Main results
No clinically relevant changes in HBV biomarkers or laboratory values, including liver function tests and thyroid-stimulating hormone, were observed. No clinically relevant adverse events were reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that a single 45 mg dose of cetrelimab administered subcutaneously in virologically suppressed participants with chronic hepatitis B was safe and well tolerated. They also noted that there was no change in HBV biomarkers, leading to the abandonment of further development of cetrelimab for HBV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that cetrelimab may not be effective in altering HBV biomarkers in virologically suppressed participants with chronic hepatitis B, which has implications for the development of PD-1 inhibitors as therapeutic agents for HBV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had a small sample size of 11 participants, with only eight receiving cetrelimab. The evidence is limited by the lack of a comparator group and the short duration of the study, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence strength is moderate.

恩替卡韦与替诺福韦艾拉酚胺在慢性乙型肝炎患者中对肝细胞癌长期发病率的比较:REAL-B研究

Similar Long-Term Incidence of Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Chronic Hepatitis B Initiating Entecavir Versus Tenofovir Alafenamide: A REAL-B Study.
作者： Haruki Uojima, Rui Huang, Huy Trinh, Akito Nozaki, Philip Vutien, Hidenori Toyoda, Takashi Honda, Hiroshi Abe, Masanori Atsukawa, Tsunamasa Watanabe
摘要中文翻译关于恩替卡韦(ETV)和替诺福韦艾拉酚胺(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中对肝细胞癌(HCC)发病率潜在差异的数据有限。本回顾性多国研究纳入了2016年以来在4个国家的23个中心开始接受ETV或TAF治疗的、无HCC病史且未接受过抗病毒药物治疗的CHB成人患者。使用逆概率加权(IPTW)和倾向评分匹配(PSM)方法平衡基线特征。采用竞争风险分析方法分析HCC发病率及抗病毒治疗方案与HCC之间的关联。共纳入1437名患者(ETV组710名,TAF组727名),其平均随访时间相似(ETV组55.5 ± 29.8个月 vs. TAF组58.0 ± 25.5个月;p = 0.077)。接受ETV治疗的患者年龄更大,男性比例更高,且更可能患有肝硬化。在IPTW调整后,两组患者的特征趋于平衡(所有标准化差异均<0.1)。调整后随访期间,ETV组有23例患者和TAF组有20例患者发展为HCC,其HCC发病率分别为每100人年0.67和0.65(p = 0.853)。两组之间HCC风险无统计学显著差异(SHR:1.09;95% CI:0.44-2.65;p = 0.851),且在按年龄、性别、肝硬化、脂肪肝、HBeAg状态、基线肝酶水平、HBV DNA水平、糖尿病等亚组分析以及使用PSM进行敏感性分析中结果一致。在这一真实世界多国研究中,对于未接受过抗病毒治疗的CHB患者,ETV和TAF组的HCC发病率无显著差异,提示治疗选择应考虑成本和患者个体因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于恩替卡韦(ETV)和替诺福韦艾拉酚胺(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中对肝细胞癌(HCC)发病率潜在差异的数据有限。
设计与对象
本回顾性多国研究纳入了2016年以来在4个国家的23个中心开始接受ETV或TAF治疗的、无HCC病史且未接受过抗病毒药物治疗的CHB成人患者。
核心方法
使用逆概率加权(IPTW)和倾向评分匹配(PSM)方法平衡基线特征。
主要结果
共纳入1437名患者(ETV组710名,TAF组727名),其平均随访时间相似(ETV组55.5 ± 29.8个月 vs. TAF组58.0 ± 25.5个月;p = 0.077)。
解释与创新
在IPTW调整后随访期间,ETV组有23例患者和TAF组有20例患者发展为HCC,其HCC发病率分别为每100人年0.67和0.65(p = 0.853)。
科研与公卫意义
两组之间HCC风险无统计学显著差异(SHR:1.09;95% CI:0.44-2.65;p = 0.851),且在按年龄、性别、肝硬化、脂肪肝、HBeAg状态、基线肝酶水平、HBV DNA水平、糖尿病等亚组分析以及使用PSM进行敏感性分析中结果一致。
局限与证据强度
接受ETV治疗的患者年龄更大,男性比例更高,且更可能患有肝硬化。

Similar Long-Term Incidence of Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Chronic Hepatitis B Initiating Entecavir Versus Tenofovir Alafenamide: A REAL-B Study.

Authors: Haruki Uojima, Rui Huang, Huy Trinh, Akito Nozaki, Philip Vutien, Hidenori Toyoda, Takashi Honda, Hiroshi Abe, Masanori Atsukawa, Tsunamasa Watanabe
Original abstract (English)
Data on potential differences in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) incidence between entecavir (ETV) and tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients are limited. This retrospective multinational study included treatment-naïve adults with CHB without a history of HCC who initiated ETV or TAF therapy since 2016 at 23 centres in 4 countries. Baseline characteristics were balanced using inverse probability of treatment weighting (IPTW) and propensity score matching (PSM). HCC incidence and the association between antiviral regimen and HCC were analysed using competing-risk analysis. A total of 1437 patients (710 ETV, 727 TAF) were included, with similar mean follow-up durations (55.5 ± 29.8 vs. 58.0 ± 25.5 months; p = 0.077). ETV-treated patients were older, more frequently male, and more likely to have cirrhosis. After IPTW, both groups had balanced characteristics (all standardised differences < 0.1). After IPTW adjustment, during follow-up, 23 patients on ETV and 20 on TAF developed HCC, with incidence rates of 0.67 and 0.65 per 100 person-years, respectively (p = 0.853). There was no statistically significant difference in HCC risk between the groups (SHR: 1.09; 95% CI: 0.44-2.65; p = 0.851), with consistent findings across subgroups by age, sex, cirrhosis, hepatic steatosis, HBeAg, baseline liver enzymes, HBV DNA levels, diabetes mellitus and in sensitivity analysis using PSM. In this real-world multinational study of treatment-naïve CHB patients, HCC incidence did not significantly differ between ETV and TAF groups, suggesting therapeutic selection should consider cost and patient-specific factors.

Question and background
Data on potential differences in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) incidence between entecavir (ETV) and tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients are limited.
Design and population
This retrospective multinational study included treatment-naïve adults with CHB without a history of HCC who initiated ETV or TAF therapy since 2016 at 23 centres in 4 countries.
Core methods
Baseline characteristics were balanced using inverse probability of treatment weighting (IPTW) and propensity score matching (PSM).
Main results
A total of 1437 patients (710 ETV, 727 TAF) were included, with similar mean follow-up durations (55.5 ± 29.8 vs. 58.0 ± 25.5 months; p = 0.077).
Interpretation and novelty
After IPTW adjustment, during follow-up, 23 patients on ETV and 20 on TAF developed HCC, with incidence rates of 0.67 and 0.65 per 100 person-years, respectively (p = 0.853).
Scientific and public-health significance
There was no statistically significant difference in HCC risk between the groups (SHR: 1.09; 95% CI: 0.44-2.65; p = 0.851), with consistent findings across subgroups by age, sex, cirrhosis, hepatic steatosis, HBeAg, baseline liver enzymes, HBV DNA levels, diabetes mellitus and in sensitivity analysis using PSM.
Limitations and evidence strength
ETV-treated patients were older, more frequently male, and more likely to have cirrhosis.

对 Roche cobas 5800、Abbott Alinity m 和 Hologic Panther 的人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型肝炎病毒 和丙型肝炎病毒进行比较病毒载量评估

A comparative viral load evaluation for human immunodeficiency virus, hepatitis B virus and hepatitis C virus of the Roche cobas 5800, Abbott Alinity m and Hologic Panther
作者： Adriaan Meyer, Suné Saayman
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景和目的 人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的病毒载量 (VL) 定量对于临床监测、治疗 反应评估和捐赠后解释至关重要。随着自动化分子平台的引入,需要进行比较评估来评估定量 VL 范围内的平台间一致性、偏差和精度。材料和方法 使用来自献血者的 280 份血浆样本进行了比较研究。使用 Roche cobas 5800 系统、Abbott Alinity m 系统以及 Hologic Panther 系统上的相关 Aptima 定量分析进行 VL 测量。使用 Deming 回归、Passing-Bablok 回归、Lin 一致性相关系数和 Bland-Altman 分析来评估平台之间的一致性。使用变异系数 (CV) 进行重复性和再现性测试来评估精度。通过单向方差分析 (ANOVA) 评估平均 VL 测量值的差异。结果 该平台在所有目标上均显示出可比较的 log 10 VL 和可接受的精度。戴明回归确定了比较中的恒定偏差和一项 HCV 比较中的比例偏差。Passing-Bablok 回归和 Lin 的一致性相关系数进一步表明一致性不同。方差分析显示 HBV 和 HCV 没有显着差异,而 HIV 的 VL 值有显着差异。结论 该平台在定量 HIV、HBV 和 HCV VL 检测方面表现出可接受的精度。罗氏 cobas 5800 系统在多次重复性比较中显示出较低的 CV,而实验室内的重复性则显示出 HBV 和 HIV 的变异性。一致性分析确定了平台比较中的恒定偏差、比例偏差和变异性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的病毒载量 (VL) 定量对于临床监测、治疗 反应评估和捐赠后解释至关重要。该研究旨在评估用于 HBV、HIV 和 HCV VL 检测的自动化分子平台的平台间一致性、偏差和精度。
设计与对象
使用来自献血者的 280 个血浆样本进行了比较研究。该研究评估了 Roche cobas 5800 系统、Abbott Alinity m 系统以及 Hologic Panther 系统上相关 Aptima 定量分析在定量 VL 范围内的性能。
核心方法
使用 Deming 回归、Passing-Bablok 回归、Lin 一致性相关系数和 Bland-Altman 分析来评估平台之间的一致性。使用具有变异系数 (CV) 的重复性和再现性测试来评估精度。通过单向方差分析 (ANOVA) 评估平均 VL 测量值的差异。
主要结果
该平台在所有目标上均显示出可比的 log 10 VL 和可接受的精度。方差分析显示 HBV 和 HCV 没有显着差异,而 HIV 的 VL 值有显着差异。一致性分析确定了平台比较中的恒定偏差、比例偏差和变异性。
解释与创新
该平台在定量 HIV、HBV 和 HCV VL 检测方面表现出可接受的精度。该研究对 HBV、HIV 和 HCV 三个平台的 VL 测量值进行了直接比较,强调了在临床环境中评估平台间一致性的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为临床和公共卫生环境中选择合适的 HBV、HIV 和 HCV VL 检测平台提供参考。他们支持使用这些平台进行准确监控和 治疗 响应评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用了一组来自献血者的血浆样本,这可能会限制普遍性。证据是温和的,因为它基于一项具有可接受的精度和所有目标的一致性的比较研究。

A comparative viral load evaluation for human immunodeficiency virus, hepatitis B virus and hepatitis C virus of the Roche cobas 5800, Abbott Alinity m and Hologic Panther

Authors: Adriaan Meyer, Suné Saayman
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background and Objectives Viral load (VL) quantification for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) is essential for clinical monitoring, treatment response assessment and post‐donation interpretation. With the introduction of automated molecular platforms, comparative evaluations are required to assess inter‐platform agreement, bias and precision across the quantitative VL range. Materials and Methods A comparative study was conducted using 280 plasma samples from blood donors. VL measurements were performed using the Roche cobas 5800 system, Abbott Alinity m system and the relevant Aptima quantitative assays on the Hologic Panther system. Agreement between platforms was assessed using Deming regression, Passing–Bablok regression, Lin's concordance correlation coefficient and Bland–Altman analyses. Precision was evaluated using repeatability and reproducibility testing using coefficients of variation (CVs). Differences in mean VL measurements were assessed with one‐way analysis of variance (ANOVA). Results The platforms showed comparable log 10 VL and acceptable precision across all targets. Deming regression identified constant bias in comparisons and proportional bias in one HCV comparison. Passing–Bablok regression and Lin's concordance correlation coefficient further showed that agreement differed. ANOVA showed no significant differences for HBV and HCV, while HIV showed a significant difference in VL values. Conclusion The platforms demonstrated acceptable precision for quantitative HIV, HBV and HCV VL testing. Roche cobas 5800 system showed low CVs in several repeatability comparisons, while within‐laboratory reproducibility showed variability for HBV and HIV. Agreement analyses identified constant bias, proportional bias and variability in platform comparisons.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Viral load (VL) quantification for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) is essential for clinical monitoring, treatment response assessment and post‐donation interpretation. The study aimed to evaluate the inter-platform agreement, bias and precision of automated molecular platforms for HBV, HIV and HCV VL testing.
Design and population
A comparative study was conducted using 280 plasma samples from blood donors. The study assessed the performance of the Roche cobas 5800 system, Abbott Alinity m system and the relevant Aptima quantitative assays on the Hologic Panther system across the quantitative VL range.
Core methods
Agreement between platforms was assessed using Deming regression, Passing–Bablok regression, Lin's concordance correlation coefficient and Bland–Altman analyses. Precision was evaluated using repeatability and reproducibility testing with coefficients of variation (CVs). Differences in mean VL measurements were assessed with one‐way analysis of variance (ANOVA).
Main results
The platforms showed comparable log 10 VL and acceptable precision across all targets. ANOVA showed no significant differences for HBV and HCV, while HIV showed a significant difference in VL values. Agreement analyses identified constant bias, proportional bias and variability in platform comparisons.
Interpretation and novelty
The platforms demonstrated acceptable precision for quantitative HIV, HBV and HCV VL testing. The study provides a direct comparison of VL measurements across three platforms for HBV, HIV and HCV, highlighting the importance of evaluating inter-platform agreement in clinical settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the selection of appropriate platforms for HBV, HIV and HCV VL testing in clinical and public health settings. They support the use of these platforms for accurate monitoring and treatment response assessment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used a single set of plasma samples from blood donors, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single comparative study with acceptable precision and agreement across all targets.

澳大利亚 治疗 患者的抗病毒 治疗 资格和 治疗 的使用情况:REACH ‐B 研究

Anti‐Viral Treatment Eligibility and Treatment Uptake Among People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Australia: The REACH ‐B Study
作者： Behzad Hajarizadeh, Renjy Nelson, Jacob George, Josh Hanson, Jane Davies, Marianne Martinello, Tracey Brown, Ian Wong, Tasnim Hasan, Lester Mascarenhas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估澳大利亚 治疗 患者的 治疗 资格和接受情况,强调不同指南中 治疗 标准的差异。
设计与对象
REACH-B 研究招募了通过全国医疗保健服务网络管理的 慢性乙型肝炎 人员。参与者被分为三组:(1)接受抗病毒药物治疗;(2) 尽管符合资格但未接受治疗;(3)未经治疗且不符合资格的。在 3648 名参与者中(56% 为男性;中位年龄 48 岁;49% 出生于东北亚或东南亚;27% 为澳大利亚原住民),14% 的人 HBeAg 呈阳性,7% 的人患有肝硬化。
核心方法
治疗 资格是根据澳大利亚药品福利计划 (PBS) 标准和国际临床指南确定的。根据 治疗 状态和资格,参与者被分为三组。
主要结果
总体而言,47% (n = 1666) 接受 抗病毒药物 治疗,45% (n = 1573) 未接受治疗且不符合资格,7% (n = 251) 尽管根据澳大利亚 PBS 标准符合资格但未接受治疗。当应用 2024 年世界卫生组织指南时,尽管符合资格但未接受治疗的比例增加到 13%,根据其他指南,这一比例在 2% 到 44% 之间。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了不同指南中 治疗 资格标准的差异,以及这些差异对符合资格的个人采用 治疗 的影响。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入了解了符合条件的个人对 治疗 的吸收差异,这可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,以提高特定人口群体的 治疗 依从性和结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于大型 队列研究,但 治疗 资格的解释受到不同指南的影响,这可能会限制结果的普遍性。由于依赖观察数据以及指南变异可能影响结果,因此证据强度中等。

Anti‐Viral Treatment Eligibility and Treatment Uptake Among People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Australia: The REACH ‐B Study

Authors: Behzad Hajarizadeh, Renjy Nelson, Jacob George, Josh Hanson, Jane Davies, Marianne Martinello, Tracey Brown, Ian Wong, Tasnim Hasan, Lester Mascarenhas
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Treatment indication for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) varies across clinical guidelines. This study evaluated treatment eligibility and uptake in a large Australian cohort of people with CHB. The REACH‐B study recruited people with CHB managed through a national network of healthcare services. Participants were categorised into three groups: (1) receiving antiviral therapy; (2) untreated despite eligibility; (3) untreated and ineligible. Treatment eligibility was determined using Australian Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme (PBS) criteria, and by international clinical guidelines. Among 3648 participants (56% male; median age 48 years; 49% born in North‐East or South‐East Asia; 27% First Nations Australians), 14% were HBeAg positive and 7% had cirrhosis. Overall, 47% ( n = 1666) were on antiviral therapy, 45% ( n = 1573) were untreated and ineligible and 7% ( n = 251) were untreated despite eligibility based on Australian PBS criteria. In adjusted analysis, the likelihood of remaining untreated despite eligibility was higher among women (aOR 1.59, 95% CI 1.34, 1.88) and younger participants (age 41–60 vs. > 60 years: aOR 1.51, 95% CI 1.01–2.28; ≤ 40 vs. > 60 years: aOR 2.65, 95% CI 1.60, 4.40). The proportion untreated despite eligibility increased to 13% when applying 2024 World Health Organisation guidelines and ranged 2%–44% based on other guidelines, highlighting the variability across the guidelines. In conclusion, among REACH‐B participants, most eligible individuals receive treatment. Lower treatment uptake among women and younger people despite eligibility partly reflects lower threshold for elevated ALT in women, and clinician determination of non‐CHB related ALT elevation. As treatment guidelines evolve and eligibility criteria broaden, monitoring treatment patterns is important.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate treatment eligibility and uptake among people with chronic hepatitis B in Australia, highlighting the variability in treatment criteria across different guidelines.
Design and population
The REACH‐B study recruited people with chronic hepatitis B managed through a national network of healthcare services. Participants were categorised into three groups: (1) receiving antiviral therapy; (2) untreated despite eligibility; (3) untreated and ineligible. Among 3648 participants (56% male; median age 48 years; 49% born in North‐East or South‐East Asia; 27% First Nations Australians), 14% were HBeAg positive and 7% had cirrhosis.
Core methods
Treatment eligibility was determined using Australian Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme (PBS) criteria and international clinical guidelines. Participants were categorised into three groups based on their treatment status and eligibility.
Main results
Overall, 47% (n = 1666) were on antiviral therapy, 45% (n = 1573) were untreated and ineligible, and 7% (n = 251) were untreated despite eligibility based on Australian PBS criteria. The proportion untreated despite eligibility increased to 13% when applying 2024 World Health Organisation guidelines, ranging from 2%–44% based on other guidelines.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the variability in treatment eligibility criteria across different guidelines and the impact of these differences on treatment uptake among eligible individuals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the disparities in treatment uptake among eligible individuals, which can inform targeted interventions to improve treatment adherence and outcomes in specific demographic groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are based on a large cohort, but the interpretation of treatment eligibility is influenced by varying guidelines, which may limit the generalisability of the results. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on observational data and the potential for guideline variability to affect outcomes.

抗病毒药物 耐药 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的 HBsAg 持续下降和肝硬化显着逆转:病例报告 的 13 年随访

Sustained HBsAg Loss and Marked Reversal of Liver Cirrhosis in a Patient with Antiviral-Resistant Chronic Hepatitis B: A Case Report with 13-Year Follow-up
作者： wenxia Li, Li w
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 实现乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 持续消失并逆转已形成的肝硬化被认为​​极具挑战性,特别是对于既往具有 抗病毒药物 耐药性的患者。我们报告了一名 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的肝硬化显着逆转和持续 HBsAg 消失的病例,该患者之前标准 抗病毒药物 治疗失败。病例介绍 一名60岁男性患者于2006年被诊断患有CHB,在一家顶级医院接受标准抗病毒药物治疗6年后出现肝硬化。2012年2月,他的肝脏硬度(FibroScan)为19.9 kPa,HBV DNA为1.47×10^7 IU/ml。随后,他接受了中西医结合(“新中医”)治疗。经过7年零2个月的治疗治疗后,他的肝功能恢复正常,HBV DNA检测不到,FibroScan降至5.1 kPa。值得注意的是,患者的 HBsAg 消失且 Pre-S1 抗原呈阴性。所有药物均于 2019 年 4 月停止。到 2020 年 11 月,尽管患者因生活方式因素出现高脂血症和脂肪性肝炎,但影像学和弹性成像证实肝硬化明显逆转(FibroScan:6.2 kPa,正常门静脉直径为 12 mm),且没有疾病进展。截至 2025 年的长期随访一致显示 HBsAg 持续阴性(< 0.05 IU/ml)和无法检测到的高度敏感的 HBV DNA。结论 本病例表明,新中药对抗病毒药物耐药的慢性乙型肝炎患者能够显着逆转肝硬化并持续功能性治愈。治疗 停药后这些结果的持久性表明肝脏微环境的根本恢复和病毒 储存宿主 的消耗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
实现乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 持续消失并逆转已形成的肝硬化被认为​​极具挑战性,特别是对于既往具有 抗病毒药物 耐药性的患者。该研究旨在报告一名 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的肝硬化显着逆转和持续 HBsAg 消失的病例,该患者之前标准 抗病毒药物 治疗失败。
设计与对象
一名60岁男性患者于2006年被诊断出慢性乙型肝炎,在一家三甲医院接受标准抗病毒药物治疗6年后发展为肝硬化。该病例随访时间为2012年2月至2020年11月,治疗涉及中西医结合(“新中医”)。
核心方法
患者接受中西医结合(“新中医”)治疗治疗。在随访期间监测肝功能、HBV DNA 和肝硬度 (FibroScan)。
主要结果
经过7年零2个月的治疗治疗,患者的肝功能恢复正常,HBV DNA检测不到,FibroScan降至5.1 kPa。值得注意的是,患者的 HBsAg 消失且 Pre-S1 抗原呈阴性。截至 2025 年的长期随访一致显示 HBsAg 持续阴性(< 0.05 IU/ml)和无法检测到的高度敏感的 HBV DNA。
解释与创新
该病例表明,新中医药可以对抗病毒药物耐药的慢乙肝患者实现显着逆转肝硬化和持续功能性治愈治疗。治疗 停药后这些结果的持久性表明肝脏微环境的根本恢复和病毒 储存宿主 的消耗。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,新中医药可能为治疗 抗病毒药物 耐药的 CHB 提供一种有前途的方法,有可能导致持续的 功能性治愈 和逆转肝硬化。这可能对 慢性乙型肝炎 及相关并发症的治疗产生重大影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到 病例报告 设计的限制,无法推广到其他患者。由于缺乏对照组以及缺乏详细的 治疗 方案和结果,因此很难评估新中医疗法的具体贡献。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Sustained HBsAg Loss and Marked Reversal of Liver Cirrhosis in a Patient with Antiviral-Resistant Chronic Hepatitis B: A Case Report with 13-Year Follow-up

Authors: wenxia Li, Li w
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Achieving sustained hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss and reversing established cirrhosis is considered extremely challenging, especially in patients with prior antiviral resistance. We report a case of marked reversal of liver cirrhosis and sustained HBsAg loss in a patient with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) who previously failed standard antiviral therapy. Case Presentation A 60-year-old male patient was diagnosed with CHB in 2006 and developed cirrhosis after 6 years of standard antiviral therapy at a top-tier hospital. In February 2012, his liver stiffness (FibroScan) was 19.9 kPa, with HBV DNA at 1.47×10^7 IU/ml. He subsequently received combined Chinese-Western medicine ("Neo-Chinese Medicine") treatment. After 7 years and 2 months of treatment, his liver function normalized, HBV DNA became undetectable, and FibroScan decreased to 5.1 kPa. Notably, the patient achieved HBsAg loss and Pre-S1 antigen negativity. All medications were discontinued in April 2019. By November 2020, despite the patient developing hyperlipidemia and steatohepatitis due to lifestyle factors, imaging and elastography confirmed a marked reversal of cirrhosis (FibroScan: 6.2 kPa, normal portal vein diameter of 12 mm) without disease progression. Long-term follow-up up to 2025 consistently showed sustained HBsAg negativity (< 0.05 IU/ml) and undetectable highly sensitive HBV DNA. Conclusion This case demonstrates that Neo-Chinese Medicine can achieve marked reversal of cirrhosis and sustained functional cure in patients with antiviral-resistant CHB. The durability of these outcomes after treatment discontinuation suggests a fundamental restoration of the hepatic microenvironment and the depletion of the viral reservoir.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Achieving sustained hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss and reversing established cirrhosis is considered extremely challenging, especially in patients with prior antiviral resistance. The study aimed to report a case of marked reversal of liver cirrhosis and sustained HBsAg loss in a patient with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) who previously failed standard antiviral therapy.
Design and population
A 60-year-old male patient was diagnosed with CHB in 2006 and developed cirrhosis after 6 years of standard antiviral therapy at a top-tier hospital. The case was followed up from February 2012 to November 2020, with treatment involving combined Chinese-Western medicine ("Neo-Chinese Medicine").
Core methods
The patient received combined Chinese-Western medicine ("Neo-Chinese Medicine") treatment. Liver function, HBV DNA, and liver stiffness (FibroScan) were monitored over the follow-up period.
Main results
After 7 years and 2 months of treatment, the patient's liver function normalized, HBV DNA became undetectable, and FibroScan decreased to 5.1 kPa. Notably, the patient achieved HBsAg loss and Pre-S1 antigen negativity. Long-term follow-up up to 2025 consistently showed sustained HBsAg negativity (< 0.05 IU/ml) and undetectable highly sensitive HBV DNA.
Interpretation and novelty
The case demonstrates that Neo-Chinese Medicine can achieve marked reversal of cirrhosis and sustained functional cure in patients with antiviral-resistant CHB. The durability of these outcomes after treatment discontinuation suggests a fundamental restoration of the hepatic microenvironment and the depletion of the viral reservoir.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Neo-Chinese Medicine may offer a promising approach for treating antiviral-resistant CHB, potentially leading to sustained functional cures and reversal of cirrhosis. This could have significant implications for the management of chronic hepatitis B and related complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its case report design, which does not allow for generalization to other patients. The lack of a control group and the absence of detailed treatment protocols and outcomes make it difficult to assess the specific contributions of the Neo-Chinese Medicine regimen. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

接受酪氨酸激酶抑制剂的癌症患者中 乙型肝炎病毒 再激活的前瞻性评估

Prospective Evaluation of Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation in Cancer Patients Receiving Tyrosine Kinase Inhibitors
作者： Hung‐Chih Chiu, Chien‐Chung Lin, Chun‐Hui Lee, Yi‐Ting Yen, Yau‐Lin Tseng, Chin‐Wei Kuo, Chian‐Wei Chen, Jeng‐Shiuan Tsai, Ya‐Ting Hsu, Shih‐Chieh Chien
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于酪氨酸激酶抑制剂 (TKI) 治疗期间 乙型肝炎病毒 再激活 (HBVr) 的数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
我们前瞻性地招募了在台湾的一个中心(2023 年 1 月至 2025 年 3 月)接受 TKI 治疗肺癌或慢性粒细胞白血病的慢性或已治愈的 HBV 患者。
核心方法
每 12 周监测一次 HBV DNA 和肝酶,每 24 周监测一次 HBsAg。
主要结果
HBVr occurred in 7 (8.9%; 95% CI, 4.4%-17.2%): 5 HBsAg-positive (25.0%; 95% CI, 11.2%-46.9%), and 2 with resolved HBV (3.4%; 95% CI, 0.9%-11.5%).
解释与创新
HBVr 在 HBsAg 阳性患者中很常见,在接受 TKI 治疗 HBV 缓解的患者中较少见,但不可忽略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了对接受 TKI 的癌症患者,特别是慢性 HBV 感染患者进行 HBV 筛查和监测的必要性。
局限与证据强度
尽管不能排除评估之间短暂的发作,但没有观察到符合研究定义的 HBVr 相关肝炎发作。

Prospective Evaluation of Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation in Cancer Patients Receiving Tyrosine Kinase Inhibitors

Authors: Hung‐Chih Chiu, Chien‐Chung Lin, Chun‐Hui Lee, Yi‐Ting Yen, Yau‐Lin Tseng, Chin‐Wei Kuo, Chian‐Wei Chen, Jeng‐Shiuan Tsai, Ya‐Ting Hsu, Shih‐Chieh Chien
Original abstract (English)
Data on hepatitis B virus reactivation (HBVr) during tyrosine kinase inhibitor (TKI) therapy remain limited. We prospectively enrolled patients with chronic or resolved HBV receiving TKIs for lung cancer or chronic myeloid leukemia at one Taiwanese center (January 2023-March 2025). HBV DNA and liver enzymes were monitored 12-weekly and HBsAg 24-weekly. HBVr was defined by AASLD criteria, with APASL and EASL definitions applied secondarily. Among 79 patients (20 HBsAg-positive, 59 resolved HBV), none received antiviral prophylaxis. Median follow-up was 42 weeks (IQR, 19-65). HBVr occurred in 7 (8.9%; 95% CI, 4.4%-17.2%): 5 HBsAg-positive (25.0%; 95% CI, 11.2%-46.9%), and 2 with resolved HBV (3.4%; 95% CI, 0.9%-11.5%). APASL and EASL definitions yielded 30.0% and 35.0% in HBsAg-positive patients, with no additional events in resolved HBV. No HBVr-associated hepatitis flare meeting the study definition was observed, although transient flares between assessments could not be excluded and deterioration in one fatal case was attributed clinically to sepsis. Median HBVr detection time was 19 weeks (IQR, 14-30). Week-48 Kaplan-Meier HBVr incidence was 37.5% (95% CI, 17.0%-69.5%) in HBsAg-positive and 5.6% (95% CI, 1.4%-21.2%) in resolved HBV (log-rank p < 0.001); Aalen-Johansen estimates were 28.3% (95% CI, 9.5%-50.9%) and 4.3% (95% CI, 0.8%-13.2%), respectively. In exploratory Firth-corrected regression, HBsAg positivity was associated with HBVr (HR, 6.95; 95% CI, 1.60-40.19), while osimertinib exposure was not. HBVr was common among HBsAg-positive patients and less common but not negligible among those with resolved HBV receiving TKIs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Data on hepatitis B virus reactivation (HBVr) during tyrosine kinase inhibitor (TKI) therapy remain limited.
Design and population
We prospectively enrolled patients with chronic or resolved HBV receiving TKIs for lung cancer or chronic myeloid leukemia at one Taiwanese center (January 2023-March 2025).
Core methods
HBV DNA and liver enzymes were monitored 12-weekly and HBsAg 24-weekly.
Main results
HBVr occurred in 7 (8.9%; 95% CI, 4.4%-17.2%): 5 HBsAg-positive (25.0%; 95% CI, 11.2%-46.9%), and 2 with resolved HBV (3.4%; 95% CI, 0.9%-11.5%).
Interpretation and novelty
HBVr was common among HBsAg-positive patients and less common but not negligible among those with resolved HBV receiving TKIs.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for HBV screening and monitoring in cancer patients receiving TKIs, particularly those with chronic HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
No HBVr-associated hepatitis flare meeting the study definition was observed, although transient flares between assessments could not be excluded.

2023-2025 年中国江苏省 0.6-14 岁儿童乙型肝炎 血清流行率。

Hepatitis B seroprevalence among children aged 0.6-14 years in Jiangsu, China, in 2023-2025.
作者： Nacheng Lin, Chenyu Xu, Chang Liu, Jie Tang, Meng Gu, Guiping Zhou, Wei Zhou, Weifeng Shi, Haibo Li, Yue Jin
摘要中文翻译中国自 2002 年起在所有新生儿中实施全民乙型肝炎 传播,并自 2011 年起为 HBV 感染母亲的婴儿提供免费乙型肝炎免疫球蛋白,并自 2011 年起推荐对高病毒载量的 HBV 感染母亲进行常规 抗病毒药物 预防,从而加强对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 母婴 传播 的预防。2020年,我们对江苏省2010年至2024年出生的儿童进行了方便抽样,对就诊的儿童进行了乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)血清流行率的调查。2023年2月至2025年12月,我们对江苏省8个城市的20,317名年龄为7.9±4.1岁的儿童(其中男孩11477名,女孩8840名)进行了乙型肝炎标志物检测。其中14名(0.069%,95% CI:0.038-0.116)HBsAg阳性。0.5-<3、3-<6、6-<9、9-<12、12-<15岁组HBsAg阳性率分别为0%(0/2520)、0%(0/3858)、0.024%(1/4243)、0.136%(6/4401)、0.132% (7/5295),分别 (P = .01489)。HBsAg抗体总体阳性率为68.11%(12046/17686),随年龄增长呈下降趋势(从0.5~<3岁儿童的96.17%下降到12~<15岁儿童的48.41%)。HBsAg阴性儿童中,乙型肝炎核心抗原抗体阳性率为7.68%(1383/18007),从0.5-<3岁儿童的4.23%上升到12-<15岁儿童的7.84%(Z = 5.0398,P < .0001)。本研究表明,自从实施乙型肝炎围产期感染强化预防以来,江苏省儿童HBsAg 患病率已大幅下降。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过对就诊儿童进行便利抽样,对江苏省2010年至2024年出生的儿童乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)血清流行率进行调查。中国自 2002 年起在所有新生儿中实施全民乙型肝炎 传播,并自 2011 年起为 HBV 感染母亲的婴儿提供免费乙型肝炎免疫球蛋白,并自 2011 年起推荐对高病毒载量的 HBV 感染母亲进行常规 抗病毒药物 预防,从而加强对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 母婴 传播 的预防。2020.
设计与对象
该研究于2023年2月至2025年12月在江苏省8个城市进行,对20317名7.9±4.1岁儿童进行了乙型肝炎标志物检测,其中男孩11477名,女孩8840名。
核心方法
对研究期间到访医院的儿童进行了乙型肝炎标志物检测。该研究采用方便抽样,重点测量乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)以及针对HBsAg和乙型肝炎核心抗原的抗体的患病率。
主要结果
在接受测试的 20,317 名儿童中,有 14 名(0.069%,95% CI:0.038-0.116)呈 HBsAg 阳性。0.5-<3、3-<6、6-<9、9-<12、12-<15岁组HBsAg阳性率分别为0%(0/2520)、0%(0/3858)、0.024%(1/4243)、0.136%(6/4401)、0.132% (7/5295),分别 (P = .01489)。HBsAg阴性儿童中,乙型肝炎核心抗原抗体阳性率为7.68%(1383/18007),从0.5-<3岁儿童的4.23%上升到12-<15岁儿童的7.84%(Z = 5.0398,P < .0001)。
解释与创新
研究显示,江苏省儿童乙型肝炎病毒围产期感染强化预防以来,HBsAg 患病率 明显下降。这一下降凸显了预防策略在减少 HBV 传播 方面的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为江苏乙型肝炎预防策略的成功提供了证据,可为其他地区采取类似干预措施的公共卫生政策和 监测 乙型肝炎防治工作提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用了便利抽样,这可能会引入选择偏差。然而,考虑到 HBsAg 患病率 的大幅下降以及结果的统计显着性 (P < .0001),证据是强有力的。

Hepatitis B seroprevalence among children aged 0.6-14 years in Jiangsu, China, in 2023-2025.

Authors: Nacheng Lin, Chenyu Xu, Chang Liu, Jie Tang, Meng Gu, Guiping Zhou, Wei Zhou, Weifeng Shi, Haibo Li, Yue Jin
Original abstract (English)
China has implemented universal hepatitis B vaccination in all neonates since 2002 and has enhanced prophylaxis against mother-to-child transmission of hepatitis B virus (HBV) by providing charge-free hepatitis B immunoglobulin in infants of HBV-infected mothers since 2011 and by recommending routine antiviral prophylaxis in HBV-infected mothers with high viral loads since 2020. We aimed to survey the seroprevalence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) in children born from 2010 to 2024 in Jiangsu Province, based on convenience sampling in children who visited hospitals. We tested hepatitis B markers in 20,317 children aged 7.9 ± 4.1 years, with 11477 boys and 8840 girls, from 8 cities across Jiangsu during February 2023 to December 2025. Of them, 14 (0.069%, 95% CI: 0.038-0.116) were HBsAg positive. The positive rates of HBsAg in 0.5-<3, 3-<6, 6-<9, 9-<12, and 12-<15 years groups were 0% (0/2520), 0% (0/3858), 0.024% (1/4243), 0.136% (6/4401), and 0.132% (7/5295), respectively (P = .01489). The overall positive rate of antibody against HBsAg was 68.11% (12046/17686), with a decreasing trend over the ages (from 96.17% in 0.5-<3 year children to 48.41% in 12-<15 year children). In HBsAg-negative children, the positive rate of antibody against hepatitis B core antigen was 7.68% (1383/18007), increasing from 4.23% in 0.5-<3 year children to 7.84% in 12-<15 year children (Z = 5.0398, P < .0001). The present study showed that HBsAg prevalence among children in Jiangsu has substantially declined since the implementation of enhanced prevention of HBV perinatal infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to survey the seroprevalence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) in children born from 2010 to 2024 in Jiangsu Province, based on convenience sampling in children who visited hospitals. China has implemented universal hepatitis B vaccination in all neonates since 2002 and has enhanced prophylaxis against mother-to-child transmission of hepatitis B virus (HBV) by providing charge-free hepatitis B immunoglobulin in infants of HBV-infected mothers since 2011 and by recommending routine antiviral prophylaxis in HBV-infected mothers with high viral loads since 2020.
Design and population
The study was conducted in 8 cities across Jiangsu Province from February 2023 to December 2025. It tested hepatitis B markers in 20,317 children aged 7.9 ± 4.1 years, with 11477 boys and 8840 girls.
Core methods
Hepatitis B markers were tested in children who visited hospitals during the study period. The study used convenience sampling and focused on measuring the prevalence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and antibody against HBsAg and hepatitis B core antigen.
Main results
Of the 20,317 children tested, 14 (0.069%, 95% CI: 0.038-0.116) were HBsAg positive. The positive rates of HBsAg in the 0.5-<3, 3-<6, 6-<9, 9-<12, and 12-<15 years groups were 0% (0/2520), 0% (0/3858), 0.024% (1/4243), 0.136% (6/4401), and 0.132% (7/5295), respectively (P = .01489). In HBsAg-negative children, the positive rate of antibody against hepatitis B core antigen was 7.68% (1383/18007), increasing from 4.23% in 0.5-<3 year children to 7.84% in 12-<15 year children (Z = 5.0398, P < .0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The study showed that HBsAg prevalence among children in Jiangsu has substantially declined since the implementation of enhanced prevention of HBV perinatal infection. This decline highlights the effectiveness of the prevention strategies in reducing HBV transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide evidence of the success of hepatitis B prevention strategies in Jiangsu, which can inform public health policies and surveillance efforts for HBV in other regions with similar interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used convenience sampling, which may introduce selection bias. However, the evidence is strong given the substantial decline in HBsAg prevalence and the statistical significance of the results (P < .0001).

新生儿肌注和静脉注射联合注射乙型肝炎免疫球蛋白预防母婴 传播 的效果

Effectiveness of combined intramuscular and intravenous administration of hepatitis B immunoglobulin to newborns in preventing mother-to-child transmission of hepatitis B virus
作者： Koushi Yamaguchi, Keiko Tsukamoto, Reiko Ito, Michi Hisano:, Haruhiko Sago, Masachi Hanaoka
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的虽然乙肝病毒母婴预防成功率约为95%,但HBeAg阳性或高病毒载量母亲所生婴儿仍有10-20%受到感染。研究设计为加强出生时乙型肝炎母婴传播的预防,在产后立即肌肉注射乙型肝炎免疫球蛋白(HBIG)的常规方案中加入静脉注射,从而实现血液抗体水平更快的上升。一旦抗体滴度下降到一定水平,就启动疫苗接种。结果 该研究方法在产后约 4 个月内保持了比现有方法更高的水平。7例HBeAg阳性病例(包括2例宫内感染)和5例高病毒载量(≥5.3 log IU/mL)病例1岁时未检测到感染。结论 旨在通过在出生后立即增强对感染的防御来保护新生儿免受乙型肝炎病毒感染,这提供了一种新的选择。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估联合肌肉注射和静脉注射乙型肝炎免疫球蛋白(HBIG)在预防传播(HBV)母婴传播(MTCT)方面的有效性,因为目前的方法仍然导致HBeAg阳性或高病毒载量母亲所生的婴儿中有10-20%受到感染。
设计与对象
该研究旨在通过在分娩后立即肌肉注射HBIG的常规方案中增加静脉注射来加强出生时HBV母婴传播的预防。其中包括 HBeAg 阳性或高病毒载量母亲所生的婴儿。
核心方法
研究方法包括在分娩后立即对新生儿进行肌肉注射和静脉注射 HBIG,一旦抗体滴度下降到一定水平就开始 疫苗接种。
主要结果
该研究方法在分娩后约 4 个月内保持了比现有方法更高的水平。7 例 HBeAg 阳性病例(包括 2 例宫内感染)和 5 例高病毒载量病例(≥5.3 log IU/mL)在 1 岁时未检测到感染。
解释与创新
作者解释说,这些发现表明,肌肉注射和静脉注射 HBIG 联合给药,通过在出生后立即增强防御能力,为保护新生儿免受 HBV 感染提供了一种新选择。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这种组合方法可以改善乙型肝炎母婴传播的预防,特别是在高危病例中,并可能为未来更有效的产后干预策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,并且结果基于少量案例,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

Effectiveness of combined intramuscular and intravenous administration of hepatitis B immunoglobulin to newborns in preventing mother-to-child transmission of hepatitis B virus

Authors: Koushi Yamaguchi, Keiko Tsukamoto, Reiko Ito, Michi Hisano:, Haruhiko Sago, Masachi Hanaoka
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objective While achieving a good success rate of approximately 95% in preventing mother-to-child transmission of hepatitis B virus (HBV), 10-20% of infants born to HBeAg-positive or high-viral-load mothers still become infected. Study Design To strengthen the prevention of HBV MTCT at birth, intravenous injection was added to the conventional regimen of intramuscular hepatitis B immune globulin (HBIG) administration immediately after delivery, thereby achieving a more rapid rise in blood antibody levels. Vaccination was initiated once antibody titers had declined to a certain level. Results The study method maintained higher levels than existing methods for approximately 4 months post-delivery. No infection was detected at one year of age in the seven HBeAg-positive cases(including two involving in utero infection) and the five cases with high viral loads (≥5.3 log IU/mL). Conclusions Designed to protect newborns from HBV infection by enhancing their defenses against infection immediately after birth, this offers a new option.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of a combined intramuscular and intravenous administration of hepatitis B immunoglobulin (HBIG) in preventing mother-to-child transmission (MTCT) of hepatitis B virus (HBV), as current methods still result in 10-20% of infants born to HBeAg-positive or high-viral-load mothers becoming infected.
Design and population
The study was designed to strengthen the prevention of HBV MTCT at birth by adding intravenous injection to the conventional regimen of intramuscular HBIG administration immediately after delivery. It included infants born to HBeAg-positive or high-viral-load mothers.
Core methods
The study method involved administering both intramuscular and intravenous HBIG to newborns immediately after delivery, with vaccination initiated once antibody titers had declined to a certain level.
Main results
The study method maintained higher levels than existing methods for approximately 4 months post-delivery. No infection was detected at one year of age in the seven HBeAg-positive cases (including two involving in utero infection) and the five cases with high viral loads (≥5.3 log IU/mL).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that the combined administration of intramuscular and intravenous HBIG offers a new option for protecting newborns from HBV infection by enhancing their defenses immediately after birth.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this combined method could improve the prevention of HBV MTCT, particularly in high-risk cases, and may inform future strategies for more effective postnatal interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a small number of cases, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

为与男性和女性性工作者发生性关系的男性移民提供以人为本的 慢性乙型肝炎 护理

Enabling Person‐Centred Chronic Hepatitis B Care Among Migrant Men Who Have Sex With Men and Female Sex Workers
作者： Horas Tze Hoo Wong, Jack E. Wallace, Ian Anderson, Loren Brener, Jacqueline A. Richmond, Simin Yu, Anik Ray, Sarah Smith, Rick Varma
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 探索前往专业性健康诊所就诊的移民男男性接触者和女性性工作者的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 护理经验,并确定以人为本的护理的感知推动因素。设计定性访谈研究。方法 对 14 名在澳大利亚悉尼公立性健康诊所就诊的 14 名海外出生的男男性行为者(MSM,n = 7)以及顺性别和变性女性性工作者(n = 7)进行半结构化访谈。使用主题分析对数据进行分析,并根据以人为本的实践框架进行解释。结果 性健康服务被认为是安全的空间,在常规筛查过程中经常发现慢性乙型肝炎,使参与者能够开始或重新进行护理。护理参与度受到宏观背景因素的影响,包括签证状态、医疗保险准入、语言需求、对 CHB 的文化理解和转诊途径。与性健康从业者的信任关系支持了对性、移民和慢性乙型肝炎的讨论,尽管一些互动被认为是指导性的且过度关注风险,破坏了关系安全。参与者将慢性乙型肝炎描述为在日常生活中不显着,但在亲密时刻(例如披露、伴侣安抚和性工作决策)变得更加突出。对于一些参与者来说,PrEP 或 HIV 治疗提供了 HIV-CHB 双重益处,使 治疗 感觉更有价值且更容易接受。参与者通过同行网络、信息搜索和符合其需求的服务来克服系统障碍。结论 针对流动男男性接触者和女性性工作者以人为本的慢性乙型肝炎护理需要的不仅仅是临床监测。护理应应对移民、性行为、性工作、亲密关系和获得医疗服务的交叉影响,同时创造支持信任、披露和持续参与的文化和关系安全环境。意义 这项研究呼吁将慢性乙型肝炎护理纳入常规性健康和艾滋病毒护理中,加强语言支持,改善转诊协调,并支持临床医生使用反射性、非指导性沟通以避免说教或“仅风险”框架。患者或公众贡献 无。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨男男性行为者和女性性工作者的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 护理经验,并确定以人为本的护理的推动因素。
设计与对象
对 14 名在澳大利亚悉尼一家公共资助的性健康诊所就诊的海外出生的男男性行为者(MSM,n = 7)以及顺性别和跨性别女性性工作者(n = 7)进行了一项定性访谈研究。
核心方法
进行了半结构化访谈,并使用主题分析对数据进行了分析,并根据以人为本的实践框架进行了解释。
主要结果
性健康服务被认为是安全的空间,在常规筛查过程中经常发现慢性乙型肝炎,使参与者能够开始或重新参与护理。
解释与创新
研究结果强调,护理参与度受到宏观环境因素的影响,包括签证状态、医疗保险获取、语言需求、对 CHB 的文化理解和转诊途径。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明需要将慢性乙型肝炎护理纳入常规性健康和艾滋病毒护理中,加强语言支持,改善转诊协调,并支持临床医生使用反射性、非指导性沟通。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到定性设计和小样本量的限制,但由于它基于深入访谈和主题分析,因此证据有力。

Enabling Person‐Centred Chronic Hepatitis B Care Among Migrant Men Who Have Sex With Men and Female Sex Workers

Authors: Horas Tze Hoo Wong, Jack E. Wallace, Ian Anderson, Loren Brener, Jacqueline A. Richmond, Simin Yu, Anik Ray, Sarah Smith, Rick Varma
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Aim(s) To explore experiences of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) care among migrant MSM and female sex workers attending a specialist sexual health clinic and identify perceived enablers of person‐centred care. Design Qualitative interview study. Methods Semi‐structured interviews with 14 overseas‐born men who have sex with men (MSM, n = 7) and cisgender and transgender female sex workers ( n = 7) attending a publicly funded sexual health clinic in Sydney, Australia. Data were analysed using thematic analysis, with interpretation informed by the Person‐Centred Practice Framework. Results Sexual health services were experienced as safe spaces where CHB was often identified during routine screening, enabling participants to initiate or re‐engage with care. Care engagement was shaped by macro‐context factors including visa status, Medicare access, language needs, culturally embedded understandings of CHB and referral pathways. Trusting relationships with sexual health practitioners supported discussion of sex, migration and CHB, although some interactions were experienced as directive and overly risk‐focused, undermining relational safety. Participants described CHB as having low salience in everyday life but becoming more prominent during intimate moments such as disclosure, partner reassurance and sex work decisions. For some participants, PrEP or HIV therapy offered dual HIV–CHB benefits, making treatment feel more worthwhile and easier to take up. Participants navigated system barriers through peer networks, information‐seeking, and services aligned with their needs. Conclusion Person‐centred CHB care for migrant MSM and female sex workers requires more than clinical monitoring. Care should be responsive to the intersecting effects of migration, sexuality, sex work, intimacy and access to health services, while creating culturally and relationally safe environments that support trust, disclosure and sustained engagement. Implications This study calls for embedding CHB care within routine sexual health and HIV care, strengthening language support, improving referral coordination, and supporting clinicians to use reflexive, non‐directive communication to avoid moralising or ‘risk‐only’ framings. Patient or Public Contribution None.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore experiences of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) care among migrant men who have sex with men and female sex workers and identify perceived enablers of person-centred care.
Design and population
A qualitative interview study was conducted with 14 overseas-born men who have sex with men (MSM, n = 7) and cisgender and transgender female sex workers (n = 7) attending a publicly funded sexual health clinic in Sydney, Australia.
Core methods
Semi-structured interviews were performed, and data were analysed using thematic analysis, with interpretation informed by the Person-Centred Practice Framework.
Main results
Sexual health services were experienced as safe spaces where CHB was often identified during routine screening, enabling participants to initiate or re-engage with care.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that care engagement is shaped by macro-context factors including visa status, Medicare access, language needs, culturally embedded understandings of CHB and referral pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study informs the need for embedding CHB care within routine sexual health and HIV care, strengthening language support, improving referral coordination, and supporting clinicians to use reflexive, non-directive communication.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its qualitative design and small sample size, but the evidence is strong as it is based on in-depth interviews and thematic analysis.

慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的 JNJ-73763989 siRNA 触发剂肝脏浓度:INSIGHT 研究小组 3 结果

Liver concentrations of JNJ-73763989 siRNA triggers in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB): The INSIGHT study panel 3 results
作者： Tarik Asselah, Valérie Vilgrain, Thomas N. Kakuda, Emmanuel Njumbe Ediage, Jan Snoeys, Sabrina Menasria Benazzouz, John Jezorwski, Lomi Kim, Oliver Lenz, Carine Guinard-Azadian
摘要中文翻译背景 JNJ-73763989 是一种靶向 乙型肝炎病毒 基因组 的 S(daplusiran)和 X(tomligisiran)区域的 2:1 组合的 N-乙酰半乳糖胺(GalNAc)偶联小干扰 RNA(siRNA)。在 II 期 2b 研究中,基于 JNJ-73763989 的组合 治疗 持续 48 周可导致病毒标志物(包括乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原)剂量依赖性下降。INSIGHT 研究第三部分的目标是量化在 治疗 治疗期间和治疗后,人类肝脏中 siRNA 的肝内浓度。方法 在一项开放标签、单臂、单中心研究中,患有 慢性乙型肝炎 的受试者接受了 48 周的 治疗 治疗,每 4 周给予 JNJ-73763989 200 mg,并联合核苷(酸)类似物治疗,可选择在 治疗 第 40 周后每周添加聚乙二醇化干扰素 α2a 180 μg。核心肝活检分别在第 12 周(TW12)、第 40 周(TW40)和随访第 24 周(FUW24)进行。结果 所有 4 名受试者在 TW12 均提供了肝活检样本,3 名受试者在 TW40 和 FUW24 提供了样本。在 TW12 和 TW40 之间,daplusiran 和 tomligisiran 的肝内浓度增加了 1.5 至 2.6 倍,与肝内蓄积一致。在最后一次 JNJ-73763989 注射后 28 周,仍有 2 名受试者检测到可测量的触发物,其浓度较峰值水平降低了 4.5 至 15 倍,估计肝内半衰期范围为约 2.9 至 16.1 周。结论 这是首个在人类中通过连续活检评估 GalNAc-siRNA 肝内浓度的研究。JNJ-73763989 在人类肝脏中的长期存在可能部分解释了 II 期研究中观察到的病毒参数持续抑制效应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在量化 慢性乙型肝炎 患者使用 JNJ-73763989 期间和之后的 siRNA 肝内浓度,JNJ-73763989 是 GalNAc 缀合 siRNA 的 2:1 组合,靶向 乙型肝炎病毒 基因组 的 S 和 X 区域。
设计与对象
这项开放标签、单臂、单中心研究涉及 慢性乙型肝炎 受试者,他们在 治疗 第 40 周后接受了为期 48 周的 治疗 治疗,每 4 周服用 JNJ-73763989 200 mg,联合核苷(酸)类似物,并可选择每周服用聚乙二醇化干扰素 α2a 180 μg。
核心方法
在 治疗 期间和之后的特定时间点收集肝脏活检,以测量 siRNA 触发物的浓度,并在 治疗 第 12、40 周和后续第 24 周获得核心活检。
主要结果
治疗 第 12 周的所有 4 名参与者以及 治疗 第 40 周和随访第 24 周的 3 名参与者均获得了肝活检。在 治疗 第 12 周和第 40 周之间,daplusiran 和 tomligisiran 的肝脏浓度增加了 1.5 至 2.6 倍,与肝内蓄积一致。最后一次注射后 28 周,3 名参与者中有 2 名仍可测量到触发因素,其浓度较峰值水平降低了 4.5 至 15 倍。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,2 期研究中观察到的对病毒参数的长期抑制作用可能部分归因于 JNJ-73763989 siRNA 触发器在人类肝脏中的长期持续存在。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 GalNAc-siRNA 疗法在肝脏中的药代动力学的见解,这可以为 慢性乙型肝炎 开发更有效的 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小以及并非所有参与者在所有时间点都可获得肝活检的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

Liver concentrations of JNJ-73763989 siRNA triggers in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB): The INSIGHT study panel 3 results

Authors: Tarik Asselah, Valérie Vilgrain, Thomas N. Kakuda, Emmanuel Njumbe Ediage, Jan Snoeys, Sabrina Menasria Benazzouz, John Jezorwski, Lomi Kim, Oliver Lenz, Carine Guinard-Azadian
Original abstract (English)
Background JNJ-73763989 is a 2:1 combination of 2 N-acetylgalactosamine (GalNAc)–conjugated short interfering RNAs (siRNA) targeting the S (daplusiran) and X (tomligisiran) regions of the hepatitis B virus genome. In phase 2b studies, 48 weeks of JNJ-73763989-based combination treatments led to dose-dependent reductions in viral markers, including hepatitis B surface antigen. The objective of the INSIGHT study Panel 3 was to quantify intrahepatic concentrations of the siRNAs in human livers during and after treatment. Methods In the open-label, single-arm, single-center study, participants with chronic hepatitis B received 48 weeks of treatment with JNJ-73763989 200 mg every 4 weeks and nucleos(t)ide analog, with optional pegylated interferon α2a 180 μg weekly added after Treatment Week (TW) 40. Core liver biopsies were collected at TW12, TW40, and at Follow-up Week (FUW) 24. Results Liver biopsies were available from all 4 participants at TW12 and from 3 at TW40 and FUW24. Between TW12 and TW40, liver concentrations of daplusiran and tomligisiran increased 1.5- to 2.6-fold, consistent with intrahepatic accumulation. Twenty-eight weeks after the last injection of JNJ-73763989, the triggers were still measurable in 2 of 3 participants with 4.5- to 15-fold reduced concentrations from peak levels; the estimated liver half-life ranged from approximately 2.9 to 16.1 weeks. Conclusions In this first study to assess liver concentrations of GalNAc-siRNA in humans with sequential biopsies, long persistence of JNJ-73763989 triggers in human livers may partially explain the prolonged suppressive effect on viral parameters observed in phase 2 studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to quantify intrahepatic concentrations of siRNAs in patients with chronic hepatitis B during and after treatment with JNJ-73763989, a 2:1 combination of GalNAc-conjugated siRNAs targeting the S and X regions of the hepatitis B virus genome.
Design and population
This open-label, single-arm, single-center study involved participants with chronic hepatitis B who received 48 weeks of treatment with JNJ-73763989 200 mg every 4 weeks combined with nucleos(t)ide analog, and optional pegylated interferon α2a 180 μg weekly after Treatment Week 40.
Core methods
Liver biopsies were collected at specific time points during and after treatment to measure the concentrations of the siRNA triggers, with core biopsies obtained at Treatment Week 12, 40, and Follow-up Week 24.
Main results
Liver biopsies were available from all 4 participants at Treatment Week 12 and from 3 at Treatment Week 40 and Follow-up Week 24. Between Treatment Week 12 and 40, liver concentrations of daplusiran and tomligisiran increased 1.5- to 2.6-fold, consistent with intrahepatic accumulation. Twenty-eight weeks after the last injection, the triggers were still measurable in 2 of 3 participants with 4.5- to 15-fold reduced concentrations from peak levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the prolonged suppressive effect on viral parameters observed in phase 2 studies may be partially explained by the long persistence of JNJ-73763989 siRNA triggers in human livers.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the pharmacokinetics of GalNAc-siRNA therapies in the liver, which could inform the development of more effective treatment strategies for chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by the small sample size and the fact that liver biopsies were not available for all participants at all time points, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

乙型肝炎病毒 感染母亲母乳喂养意愿的影响因素:ABC-X 横断面分析

Factors influencing breastfeeding intention among hepatitis B virus-infected mothers: An ABC-X cross-sectional analysis
作者： YaNan Jiang, Zhijie Geng, Wei Yang, Li Tian
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管国际指南建议支持乙型肝炎病毒感染母亲母乳喂养,但母乳喂养率仍然不理想。
设计与对象
2025 年 1 月至 12 月,对 119 名 HBV 感染孕妇进行了一项以医院为基础的横断面研究。
核心方法
结构化问卷评估了人口特征、疾病相关因素、社会支持、自我效能、耻辱感、母乳喂养态度和母乳喂养意愿。进行了描述性统计、t 检验、卡方检验和多元逻辑回归。
主要结果
在参与者中,45.3% (n = 54) 打算母乳喂养。单变量分析显示,有和没有母乳喂养意愿的母亲之间的自我效能(p = 0.003)和母乳喂养态度评分(p = 0.046)存在显着差异。
解释与创新
After adjustment for potential confounders, self-efficacy was the only independent predictor of breastfeeding intention (OR = 3.71, 95% CI: 1.35–10.19, p = 0.011).
科研与公卫意义
提高自我效能并明确传达母乳喂养安全与免疫预防的干预措施可能会改善该人群的母乳喂养支持。
局限与证据强度
预测模型显示出良好的区分度,ROC 曲线下面积为 0.782。

Factors influencing breastfeeding intention among hepatitis B virus-infected mothers: An ABC-X cross-sectional analysis

Authors: YaNan Jiang, Zhijie Geng, Wei Yang, Li Tian
Original abstract (English)
Despite international guideline recommendations supporting breastfeeding among HBV-infected mothers, breastfeeding rates remain suboptimal. This study investigated factors influencing breastfeeding intention among HBV-infected mothers using the ABC-X theoretical framework. A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted from January to December 2025 among 119 HBV-infected pregnant women. Structured questionnaires assessed demographic characteristics, disease-related factors, social support, self-efficacy, stigma, breastfeeding attitudes, and breastfeeding intention. Descriptive statistics, t-tests, chi-square tests, and multivariate logistic regression were performed. Among participants, 45.3% (n = 54) intended to breastfeed. Univariate analysis showed significant differences in self-efficacy (p = 0.003) and breastfeeding attitude scores (p = 0.046) between mothers with and without breastfeeding intention. After adjustment for potential confounders, self-efficacy was the only independent predictor of breastfeeding intention (OR = 3.71, 95% CI: 1.35–10.19, p = 0.011). The prediction model showed good discrimination, with an area under the ROC curve of 0.782. Maternal self-efficacy was the strongest predictor of breastfeeding intention among HBV-infected mothers. Interventions enhancing self-efficacy and clearly communicating breastfeeding safety with immunoprophylaxis may improve breastfeeding support in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite international guideline recommendations supporting breastfeeding among HBV-infected mothers, breastfeeding rates remain suboptimal.
Design and population
A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted from January to December 2025 among 119 HBV-infected pregnant women.
Core methods
Structured questionnaires assessed demographic characteristics, disease-related factors, social support, self-efficacy, stigma, breastfeeding attitudes, and breastfeeding intention. Descriptive statistics, t-tests, chi-square tests, and multivariate logistic regression were performed.
Main results
Among participants, 45.3% (n = 54) intended to breastfeed. Univariate analysis showed significant differences in self-efficacy (p = 0.003) and breastfeeding attitude scores (p = 0.046) between mothers with and without breastfeeding intention.
Interpretation and novelty
After adjustment for potential confounders, self-efficacy was the only independent predictor of breastfeeding intention (OR = 3.71, 95% CI: 1.35–10.19, p = 0.011).
Scientific and public-health significance
Interventions enhancing self-efficacy and clearly communicating breastfeeding safety with immunoprophylaxis may improve breastfeeding support in this population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The prediction model showed good discrimination, with an area under the ROC curve of 0.782.

马来西亚 HBsAg 阴性维持性血液透析患者中​​的隐匿性 乙型肝炎病毒 感染:与抗 HBs 血清状态相关

Occult Hepatitis B Virus Infection Among HBsAg-Negative Maintenance Haemodialysis Patients in Malaysia: Association with Anti-HBs Serostatus
作者： Xin Yi Jolynne Tan, Jun Min Em, Man Le Ng, Soo Kun Lim, Azwani Abdullah, Lim Soo Kun
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
隐匿性 传播 感染 (OBI)——定义为 HBsAg 阴性个体中存在有复制能力的 HBV DNA——在血液透析单位造成严重的 传播 和再激活风险。本研究旨在探讨 HBsAg 阴性维持性血液透析患者,特别是抗 HBc 阳性患者的 患病率 和可检测 HBV DNA 的临床特征。
设计与对象
一项回顾性观察研究涉及 2,146 名于 2019 年 1 月 1 日至 2024 年 6 月 30 日期间在马来亚大学医学中心(UMMC)透析室就诊的患者。包括接受维持性血液透析的患者,而 HBsAg 阳性或数据缺失的患者被排除在外。
核心方法
从电子病历 (EMR) 中提取患者人口统计数据、临床变量和生化信息。使用REDCap版本14.3.13进行数据收集,并使用SPSS版本29.0和Python版本3.10.16进行统计分析。利用 OBI 状态作为因变量构建了 Firth 惩罚多变量二元逻辑回归模型,以识别独立的临床预测因子,解决标准逻辑回归中固有的潜在小样本偏差。
主要结果
在 2,146 名筛查患者中,纳入了 1,635 名患者。23.5% (N = 384) 的 队列研究 检测出抗 HBc IgG 阳性。根据研究期间流行的检测实践,未对 146 名患者进行 HBV DNA 检测。在测试的核心阳性亚组 (N = 238) 中,总体 OBI 患病率 为 9.2% (N = 22)。
解释与创新
The multivariable Firth penalised logistic regression model revealed that while an Anti-HBs titre ≥ 10 IU/L was a significant negative predictor of OBI (OR 0.18, 95% CI 0.07–0.50, p = 0.001), it failed to exclude active viraemia entirely.
科研与公卫意义
我们的研究发现,经过测试的马来西亚 ESKD 队列研究 的总体 OBI 患病率 为 9.2%。在免疫功能低下的人群中,仅依靠抗 HBs IgG 来推断病毒清除是不够的,特别是考虑到超过一半 (54.6%) 的 OBI 患者保持抗 HBs IgG 阳性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性或证据的强度。

Occult Hepatitis B Virus Infection Among HBsAg-Negative Maintenance Haemodialysis Patients in Malaysia: Association with Anti-HBs Serostatus

Authors: Xin Yi Jolynne Tan, Jun Min Em, Man Le Ng, Soo Kun Lim, Azwani Abdullah, Lim Soo Kun
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Occult Hepatitis B infection (OBI)—defined as the presence of replication-competent HBV DNA in HBsAg-negative individuals—poses a severe transmission and reactivation risk in haemodialysis units. This study aims to investigate the prevalence and clinical characteristics of detectable HBV DNA among HBsAg-negative maintenance haemodialysis patients, particularly those with anti-HBc positivity. Methods A retrospective observational study was conducted involving 2,146 patients who attended the dialysis unit at Universiti Malaya Medical Centre (UMMC) from 1 January 2019 to 30 June 2024. Patients established on maintenance haemodialysis were included, while those with HBsAg positivity or missing data were excluded. Patient demographics, clinical variables, and biochemical information were extracted from the Electronic Medical Record (EMR). Data collection was conducted using REDCap version 14.3.13, and statistical analysis was performed using SPSS version 29.0 and Python version 3.10.16. A Firth penalised multivariable binary logistic regression model was constructed utilising OBI status as the dependent variable to identify independent clinical predictors, addressing potential small-sample bias inherent in standard logistic regression. Results Among 2,146 screened patients, 1,635 patients were included. Anti-HBc IgG positivity was identified in 23.5% (N = 384) of the cohort. HBV DNA testing was not performed in 146 patients under the testing practices prevailing during the study period. Within the tested core-positive subgroup (N = 238), overall OBI prevalence was 9.2% (N = 22). In patients with positive HBV DNA, 54.6% were concurrently Anti-HBs IgG positive (≥ 10 IU/L), and 18.2% possessed an Anti-HBs IgG level ≥ 100 IU/L. The multivariable Firth penalised logistic regression model revealed that while an Anti-HBs titre ≥ 10 IU/L was a significant negative predictor of OBI (OR 0.18, 95% CI 0.07–0.50, p = 0.001), it failed to exclude active viraemia entirely. Conclusion Our study identified an overall OBI prevalence of 9.2% in a tested Malaysian ESKD cohort. Firth penalised logistic regression model utilising OBI status as the dependent variable confirmed that relying solely on Anti-HBs IgG to infer viral clearance is inadequate in this immunocompromised population, especially given that over half (54.6%) of OBI patients maintained Anti-HBs IgG positivity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Occult Hepatitis B Virus infection (OBI)—defined as the presence of replication-competent HBV DNA in HBsAg-negative individuals—poses a severe transmission and reactivation risk in haemodialysis units. This study aims to investigate the prevalence and clinical characteristics of detectable HBV DNA among HBsAg-negative maintenance haemodialysis patients, particularly those with anti-HBc positivity.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted involving 2,146 patients who attended the dialysis unit at Universiti Malaya Medical Centre (UMMC) from 1 January 2019 to 30 June 2024. Patients established on maintenance haemodialysis were included, while those with HBsAg positivity or missing data were excluded.
Core methods
Patient demographics, clinical variables, and biochemical information were extracted from the Electronic Medical Record (EMR). Data collection was conducted using REDCap version 14.3.13, and statistical analysis was performed using SPSS version 29.0 and Python version 3.10.16. A Firth penalised multivariable binary logistic regression model was constructed utilising OBI status as the dependent variable to identify independent clinical predictors, addressing potential small-sample bias inherent in standard logistic regression.
Main results
Among 2,146 screened patients, 1,635 patients were included. Anti-HBc IgG positivity was identified in 23.5% (N = 384) of the cohort. HBV DNA testing was not performed in 146 patients under the testing practices prevailing during the study period. Within the tested core-positive subgroup (N = 238), overall OBI prevalence was 9.2% (N = 22).
Interpretation and novelty
The multivariable Firth penalised logistic regression model revealed that while an Anti-HBs titre ≥ 10 IU/L was a significant negative predictor of OBI (OR 0.18, 95% CI 0.07–0.50, p = 0.001), it failed to exclude active viraemia entirely.
Scientific and public-health significance
Our study identified an overall OBI prevalence of 9.2% in a tested Malaysian ESKD cohort. Relying solely on Anti-HBs IgG to infer viral clearance is inadequate in this immunocompromised population, especially given that over half (54.6%) of OBI patients maintained Anti-HBs IgG positivity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the specific limitations of the study design or the strength of the evidence.

非洲 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的肝脂肪变性:患病率、相关性和临床意义。

Hepatic Steatosis in People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Africa: Prevalence, Correlates, and Clinical Significance.
作者： Freeman W Chabala, Edford Sinkala, Alana T Brennan, Amy Miskimon Goss, Lamin Bojang, Yusupha Samateh, Alexander J Stockdale, Roger Sombie, Arouna Sessouma, Alice Nanelin Guingane
摘要中文翻译为了解决非洲地区代谢性肝病的数据缺口,我们在非洲乙型肝炎合作网络(HEPSANET)中评估了慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者是否患有脂肪性肝病(SLD),使用了瞬时弹性成像结合控制衰减参数(CAP)。通过多变量逻辑回归分析,我们确定了与SLD(CAP ≥ 248 dB/m)相关的社会人口学、人体测量学和临床特征,并评估了SLD严重程度是否独立与纤维化相关。我们还使用Cox比例风险模型评估了SLD对死亡率的短期影响。此外,我们评估了B型超声波在SLD诊断中的表现,以CAP为参考标准。在6个国家的1601名参与者(年龄范围12-80岁;39.5%女性;4%患有糖尿病;11%有不健康饮酒行为;中位数体重指数24.4)中,421名(26.3%)患有SLD;219名(13.7%)患有严重脂肪变(CAP ≥ 280 dB/m)。年龄较大、男性性别、超重和肥胖以及HIV阴性状态与SLD相关。在调整年龄、性别、HIV状态、HBV 治疗和国家后,严重脂肪变(而非轻度和中度形式,即248-279 dB/m)与纤维化概率增加8.4%(95%置信区间1.9%-14.9%)相关。在有限的随访时间(中位数2.69年)和较少的死亡病例下,SLD与全因或肝相关死亡率无显著关联。B型超声波对SLD的敏感性较低,无论是整体还是在严重情况下,但特异性为95%。总之,SLD在非洲CHB患者中较为常见,其严重形式与肝纤维化相关。这些数据支持在非洲HBV管理中纳入代谢性共病的筛查措施以及治疗的监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为了解决非洲地区代谢性肝病的数据缺口,我们在非洲乙型肝炎合作网络(HEPSANET)中评估了慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者是否患有脂肪性肝病(SLD),使用了瞬时弹性成像结合控制衰减参数(CAP).
设计与对象
通过多变量逻辑回归分析,我们确定了与SLD(CAP ≥ 248 dB/m)相关的社会人口学、人体测量学和临床特征,并评估了SLD严重程度是否独立与纤维化相关.
核心方法
在6个国家的1601名参与者(年龄范围12-80岁;39.5%女性;4%患有糖尿病;11%有不健康饮酒行为;中位数体重指数24.4)中,421名(26.3%)患有SLD;219名(13.7%)患有严重脂肪变(CAP ≥ 280 dB/m).
主要结果
年龄较大、男性性别、超重和肥胖以及HIV阴性状态与SLD相关。在调整年龄、性别、HIV状态、HBV 治疗和国家后,严重脂肪变(而非轻度和中度形式,即248-279 dB/m)与纤维化概率增加8.4%(95%置信区间1.9%-14.9%)相关.
解释与创新
总之,SLD在非洲CHB患者中较为常见,其严重形式与肝纤维化相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些数据支持将代谢合并症筛查作为非洲 HBV 护理和 治疗 的一部分。
局限与证据强度
在有限的随访时间(中位数2.69年)和较少的死亡病例下,SLD与全因或肝相关死亡率无显著关联。

Hepatic Steatosis in People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Africa: Prevalence, Correlates, and Clinical Significance.

Authors: Freeman W Chabala, Edford Sinkala, Alana T Brennan, Amy Miskimon Goss, Lamin Bojang, Yusupha Samateh, Alexander J Stockdale, Roger Sombie, Arouna Sessouma, Alice Nanelin Guingane
Original abstract (English)
To address data gaps around metabolic liver disease in Africa, within the Hepatitis B in Africa Collaborative Network (HEPSANET), we evaluated participants with chronic hepatitis B virus infection (CHB) for steatotic liver disease (SLD) using transient elastography with controlled attenuation parameter (CAP). Using multivariable logistic regression, we identified sociodemographic, anthropometric, and clinical associations with SLD (CAP ≥ 248 dB/m), and evaluated whether SLD severity was independently associated with fibrosis. Short-term impacts of SLD on mortality were also assessed with Cox proportional hazards analysis. We also evaluated the diagnostic performance of B-mode ultrasound for SLD, with CAP as reference. Across 6 countries and 1601 participants (age range, 12-80 years; 39.5% female; 4% with diabetes; 11% unhealthy alcohol use; median body mass index, 24.4), 421 (26.3%) had SLD; 219 (13.7%) had severe steatosis (CAP ≥ 280 dB/m). Older age, male sex, overweight and obesity, and HIV-negative status were associated with SLD. After adjusting for age, sex, HIV status, HBV treatment, and country, severe steatosis, but not mild and moderate forms (i.e., 248-279 dB/m), was associated with a 8.4% (95%, 1.9%-14.9%) increased probability of fibrosis. Over limited follow-up (median 2.69 years) and small number of deaths, SLD was not associated with all-cause or liver-related mortality. B-mode ultrasound had low sensitivity for SLD, overall or when severe, but specificity was 95%. In conclusion, SLD was common in people with CHB in Africa and its severe form was associated with liver fibrosis. These data support screening for metabolic comorbidities as part of HBV care and treatment in Africa.
