慢性乙型肝炎 成人的体力活动和 死亡率 风险以及肝脏相关事件:一项全国范围的 队列研究 研究。

Becoming Physically Active and Risks of Mortality and Liver-Related Events in Adults With Chronic Hepatitis B: A Nationwide Cohort Study.
作者： Byeong Geun Song, Myeongcheol Lee, Juhee Cho, Geum-Youn Gwak, Danbee Kang, Dong Hyun Sinn
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
体力活动 (PA) 具有多种健康益处;然而,在缺乏身体活动的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 成人中增加 PA 是否与较低的不良临床结果风险相关尚不清楚。
设计与对象
我们纳入了 318,131 名慢性乙型肝炎成人患者,他们在初次健康筛查(检查 1)时身体缺乏活动,并在 4 年内完成了后续筛查(检查 2)。
核心方法
我们使用了全国性的韩国国民健康保险数据库。
主要结果
在中位数7.9年的随访期间(四分位数范围,5.2-9.5),发生了7609例死亡。
解释与创新
MVPA initiation was associated with lower all-cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84-0.94), a lower risk of liver-related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92-0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91-0.99) compared with those remaining inactive.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了大规模的流行病学证据,支持在慢性乙型肝炎患者中进一步评估体力活动推广的必要性。
局限与证据强度
这种关联在老年人、女性、从未吸烟者、非饮酒者和未接受治疗的参与者中更为显著(所有交互作用的p值≤0.03)。

Becoming Physically Active and Risks of Mortality and Liver-Related Events in Adults With Chronic Hepatitis B: A Nationwide Cohort Study.

Authors: Byeong Geun Song, Myeongcheol Lee, Juhee Cho, Geum-Youn Gwak, Danbee Kang, Dong Hyun Sinn
Original abstract (English)
Physical activity (PA) confers several health benefits; however, whether increasing PA among physically inactive adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) is associated with lower risks of adverse clinical outcomes remains unclear. We used the nationwide Korean National Health Insurance database. We included 318,131 adults with CHB who were physically inactive at the initial health screening (Exam 1) and completed a subsequent screening within 4 years (Exam 2). Among them, 58,703 initiated moderate-to-vigorous PA (MVPA), defined as vigorous activity ≥ 20 min/day on ≥ 3 days/week or moderate activity ≥ 30 min/day on ≥ 5 days/week. The primary outcome was all-cause mortality, and the secondary outcomes were liver-related events (hepatic decompensation, liver transplantation, hepatocellular carcinoma [HCC]) and HCC. Multivariable Cox and Fine-Grey competing-risk models were adjusted for demographic, clinical, and behavioural factors. During a median 7.9-year follow-up (IQR, 5.2-9.5), 7609 deaths occurred. MVPA initiation was associated with lower all-cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84-0.94), a lower risk of liver-related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92-0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91-0.99) compared with those remaining inactive. The association was more pronounced in older adults, women, never smokers, non-drinkers, and untreated participants (all p for interaction ≤ 0.03). Initiating MVPA among physically inactive adults with CHB was associated with lower risks of mortality and liver-related outcomes. These findings provide large-scale epidemiological evidence supporting further evaluation of PA promotion in physically inactive patients with CHB.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Physical activity (PA) confers several health benefits; however, whether increasing PA among physically inactive adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) is associated with lower risks of adverse clinical outcomes remains unclear.
Design and population

Core methods

Main results
During a median 7.9-year follow-up (IQR, 5.2-9.5), 7609 deaths occurred.
Interpretation and novelty
MVPA initiation was associated with lower all-cause mortality (hazard ratio [HR], 0.89; 95% CI, 0.84-0.94), a lower risk of liver-related events (subdistribution HR [subHR], 0.94; 95% CI, 0.92-0.97), and, more modestly, of HCC (subHR, 0.95; 95% CI, 0.91-0.99) compared with those remaining inactive.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The association was more pronounced in older adults, women, never smokers, non-drinkers, and untreated participants (all p for interaction ≤ 0.03).

tenofovir alafenamide与entecavir在慢性乙型肝炎和慢性肾脏病患者中的肾脏安全性及抗病毒药物有效性:一项真实世界队列研究研究。

Renal Safety and Antiviral Effectiveness of Tenofovir Alafenamide Versus Entecavir in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis B and Chronic Kidney Disease: A Real-World Cohort Study.
作者： Danfeng Ren, Xiangyun Dang, Shan Fu, Ze Zhang, Tianzhi Ni, Wentao Zhang, Yingren Zhao, Jinfeng Liu
摘要中文翻译关于替诺福韦艾拉酚胺(TAF)的现实世界数据,尤其是在肾功能严重受损的患者中,仍然非常有限。我们评估了TAF与恩替卡韦(ETV)在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)合并已有慢性肾脏病(CKD)患者中的肾脏安全性及有效性。本研究为一项回顾性单中心研究,纳入了接受TAF或ETV治疗的CHB合并已有CKD的患者。主要结局是48周内估算肾小球滤过率(eGFR)的变化。次要结局包括病毒学、生化和代谢反应。通过CKD阶段和透析状态进行了探索性亚组分析。在54名患者(35名接受TAF治疗,19名接受ETV治疗)中,两个组的肾功能均保持稳定。从基线到第48周,TAF组与ETV组的eGFR变化无显著差异(1.56 ± 8.14 vs. -0.53 ± 9.78 mL/min/1.73 m2;变化差值,2.09;95%置信区间,-0.35至7.68;p = 0.46)。两个组均实现了持续病毒抑制,第48周时检测不到HBV DNA无显著差异。TAF组的HBsAg下降幅度大于ETV组(-0.36 ± 0.42 vs. 0.02 ± 0.34 log10 IU/mL;p = 0.01)。在eGFR < 30 mL/min/1.73 m2的患者中,无论是否接受透析,TAF均表现出有效的病毒抑制和肾脏安全性。代谢参数保持稳定,无需复杂的剂量调整。TAF在CKD合并CHB患者中是安全且有效的,包括那些肾功能严重受损的患者。其肾脏安全性和有效性与ETV相比无显著差异,且具有固定剂量无需复杂调整的实用性优势。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于tenofovir alafenamide(TAF)的真实世界数据,尤其是在肾功能严重受损患者中,仍较为有限。
设计与对象
我们评估了TAF与entecavir(ETV)在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)合并已有慢性肾脏病(CKD)患者中的肾脏安全性和抗病毒药物有效性。
核心方法
在本项回顾性单中心研究中,纳入了接受TAF或ETV治疗的CHB合并已有CKD患者。次要结局包括病毒学、生化和代谢反应。
主要结果
Among 54 patients (35 TAF, 19 ETV), renal function remained stable in both groups. eGFR change from baseline to week 48 was not significantly different between the TAF and ETV groups (1.56 ± 8.14 vs. -0.53 ± 9.78 mL/min/1.73 m2; Δchange, 2.09; 95% CI, -0.35 to 7.68; p = 0.46).
解释与创新
两组均实现了持续病毒抑制,第48周时检测不到HBV DNA无显著差异。与ETV相比,TAF在HBsAg减少方面更显著(-0.36 ± 0.42 vs. 0.02 ± 0.34 log10 IU/mL;p = 0.01)。
科研与公卫意义
TAF在慢性乙型肝炎合并慢性肾脏病的患者中是安全且有效的,包括那些肾功能严重受损的患者。其肾脏安全性和抗病毒药物有效性与ETV无显著差异,且具有固定剂量无需复杂调整的实用优势。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告研究结果的可推广性或证据强度。

Renal Safety and Antiviral Effectiveness of Tenofovir Alafenamide Versus Entecavir in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis B and Chronic Kidney Disease: A Real-World Cohort Study.

Authors: Danfeng Ren, Xiangyun Dang, Shan Fu, Ze Zhang, Tianzhi Ni, Wentao Zhang, Yingren Zhao, Jinfeng Liu
Original abstract (English)
Real-world data on tenofovir alafenamide (TAF), especially in patients with advanced renal impairment, are scarce. We evaluated its renal safety and antiviral effectiveness versus entecavir (ETV) in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and pre-existing chronic kidney disease (CKD). In this retrospective single-centre study, CHB patients with pre-existing CKD treated with TAF or ETV were included. The primary outcome was change in estimated glomerular filtration rate (eGFR) over 48 weeks. Secondary outcomes included virological, biochemical and metabolic responses. Exploratory subgroup analyses were performed by CKD stage and dialysis status. Among 54 patients (35 TAF, 19 ETV), renal function remained stable in both groups. eGFR change from baseline to week 48 was not significantly different between the TAF and ETV groups (1.56 ± 8.14 vs. -0.53 ± 9.78 mL/min/1.73 m2; Δchange, 2.09; 95% CI, -0.35 to 7.68; p = 0.46). Both groups achieved sustained viral suppression, with no significant difference in undetectable HBV DNA in week-48. HBsAg reduction was greater with TAF than with ETV (-0.36 ± 0.42 vs. 0.02 ± 0.34 log10 IU/mL; p = 0.01). Exploratory analyses in patients with eGFR < 30 mL/min/1.73 m2, TAF showed effective viral suppression and renal safety regardless of dialysis status. Metabolic parameters remained stable, without complex dose adjustments. TAF was safe and effective in CHB patients with CKD, including those with advanced renal impairment. Its renal safety and antiviral effectiveness did not differ significantly from those of ETV, with the practical advantage of fixed dosing without complex adjustment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Real-world data on tenofovir alafenamide (TAF), especially in patients with advanced renal impairment, are scarce.
Design and population
We evaluated its renal safety and antiviral effectiveness versus entecavir (ETV) in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and pre-existing chronic kidney disease (CKD).
Core methods
In this retrospective single-centre study, CHB patients with pre-existing CKD treated with TAF or ETV were included. Secondary outcomes included virological, biochemical and metabolic responses.
Main results
Among 54 patients (35 TAF, 19 ETV), renal function remained stable in both groups. eGFR change from baseline to week 48 was not significantly different between the TAF and ETV groups (1.56 ± 8.14 vs. -0.53 ± 9.78 mL/min/1.73 m2; Δchange, 2.09; 95% CI, -0.35 to 7.68; p = 0.46).
Interpretation and novelty
Both groups achieved sustained viral suppression, with no significant difference in undetectable HBV DNA in week-48. HBsAg reduction was greater with TAF than with ETV (-0.36 ± 0.42 vs. 0.02 ± 0.34 log10 IU/mL; p = 0.01).
Scientific and public-health significance
TAF was safe and effective in CHB patients with CKD, including those with advanced renal impairment. Its renal safety and antiviral effectiveness did not differ significantly from those of ETV, with the practical advantage of fixed dosing without complex adjustment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the generalizability of the findings or the strength of the evidence.

Inarigivir 通过抑制病毒聚合酶活性,在体外表现出不依赖于 RIG-I 的 抗病毒药物 抗 HBV DNA 复制的活性

Inarigivir exhibits RIG-I-independent antiviral activity against HBV DNA replication in vitro by inhibiting viral polymerase activity
作者： Jun-Jie Zhang, Danni Colledge, Andrea Jurado, Ning Sun, Hu Zhang, Kathy Jackson, Vitina Sozzi, Xin Li, Nicholas S. Eyre, Michael R. Beard
摘要中文翻译Inarigivir (SB 9200) 是一种线性二核苷酸 免疫应答 药物,设计用作 RIG-I 激动剂,激活细胞固有的 免疫应答。丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 患者的临床试验已证实 Inarigivir 具有先天免疫激活 抗病毒药物 活性。在 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 患者中,使用 Inarigivir 的临床试验已证明 HBV DNA 和 RNA 水平显着降低,尽管在较高剂量下观察到异质性药物引起的肝损伤。为了进一步探讨 Inarigivir 抗 HBV 的作用机制,我们表征了其 抗病毒药物 在各种肝细胞来源的细胞系中的作用,包括瞬时转染的 HepG2 和 Huh7 细胞、诱导型 HBV 稳定细胞系 HepAD38 和 HepDES19 以及 HBV 感染的人肝细胞。我们的结果表明,Inarigivir 以剂量依赖性方式抑制 HBV DNA 复制,而不影响 HBV RNA 水平或 pgRNA 衣壳化。HBV RNA 免疫沉淀试验显示 Inarigivir 不影响病毒聚合酶与 pgRNA 的结合,而体外内源聚合酶反应 (EPR) 试验表明 Inarigivir 抑制衣壳内聚合酶催化的 HBV DNA 合成。此外,我们提供了几条证据,表明 Inarigivir 的 抗病毒药物 作用独立于肝细胞衍生细胞中的 RIG-I 信号通路。综上所述,我们的研究证明了 Inarigivir 作为双核苷非链终止逆转录酶抑制剂抑制 HBV DNA 复制,具有独立于 RIG-I 的 抗病毒药物 机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在以 Inarigivir 抗丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的 乙型肝炎病毒 活性为基础,研究 Inarigivir 抗 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA 复制的作用机制。
设计与对象
该研究表征了 Inarigivir 抗病毒药物 在肝细胞衍生细胞系中的作用,包括瞬时转染的 HepG2 和 Huh7 细胞、诱导型 HBV 稳定细胞系 HepAD38 和 HepDES19 以及 HBV 感染的人肝细胞。
核心方法
使用 HBV RNA 免疫沉淀测定和体外内源聚合酶反应 (EPR) 测定来评估 Inarigivir 对病毒聚合酶与 pgRNA 结合以及衣壳内聚合酶催化的 DNA 合成的影响。
主要结果
Inarigivir 以剂量依赖性方式抑制 HBV DNA 复制,而不影响 HBV RNA 水平或 pgRNA 衣壳化。HBV RNA 免疫沉淀测定显示对病毒聚合酶与 pgRNA 的结合没有影响,而 EPR 测定则显示衣壳内聚合酶催化的 HBV DNA 合成受​​到抑制。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,Inarigivir 对 HBV 的 抗病毒药物 作用独立于 RIG-I 信号通路,凸显了作为双核苷非链终止逆转录酶抑制剂的新作用机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为开发针对 HBV 感染的新治疗策略提供了见解,特别是在不依赖先天免疫途径的情况下靶向 DNA 复制。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外分析,证据基于细胞系模型,可能无法完全代表人类肝脏生理学。由于缺乏作用机制的临床试验数据,证据强度中等。

Inarigivir exhibits RIG-I-independent antiviral activity against HBV DNA replication in vitro by inhibiting viral polymerase activity

Authors: Jun-Jie Zhang, Danni Colledge, Andrea Jurado, Ning Sun, Hu Zhang, Kathy Jackson, Vitina Sozzi, Xin Li, Nicholas S. Eyre, Michael R. Beard
Original abstract (English)
Inarigivir (SB 9200) is a linear dinucleotide antiviral drug designed to act as a RIG-I agonist, activating cellular innate immune responses. Clinical trials in hepatitis C virus (HCV) patients have confirmed the innate immune-activating antiviral activity of Inarigivir. In hepatitis B virus (HBV) patients, clinical trials using Inarigivir have demonstrated significant reductions in HBV DNA and RNA levels, although heterogeneous drug-induced liver injury has been observed at higher doses. To further explore Inarigivir's mechanism of action against HBV, we characterized its antiviral effect in various hepatocyte-derived cell lines, including transiently transfected HepG2 and Huh7 cells, inducible HBV stable cell lines HepAD38 and HepDES19, and HBV-infected human hepatocytes. Our results show that Inarigivir inhibits HBV DNA replication in a dose-dependent manner without affecting HBV RNA levels or pgRNA encapsidation. HBV RNA immunoprecipitation assays revealed that Inarigivir does not affect viral polymerase binding to pgRNA, while the in vitro endogenous polymerase reaction (EPR) assays demonstrated that Inarigivir inhibits HBV DNA synthesis catalyzed by polymerase within the capsid. Additionally, we provided several lines of evidence indicating that Inarigivir's antiviral effect is independent of the RIG-I signalling pathway in hepatocyte-derived cells. Taken together, our study demonstrates a RIG-I-independent antiviral mechanism of Inarigivir by inhibiting HBV DNA replication as a dinucleosidic non-chain-terminating reverse transcriptase inhibitor.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the mechanism of action of Inarigivir against hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA replication, building on its established antiviral activity against hepatitis C virus (HCV).
Design and population
The study characterized Inarigivir's antiviral effect in hepatocyte-derived cell lines, including transiently transfected HepG2 and Huh7 cells, inducible HBV stable cell lines HepAD38 and HepDES19, and HBV-infected human hepatocytes.
Core methods
HBV RNA immunoprecipitation assays and in vitro endogenous polymerase reaction (EPR) assays were used to assess the effect of Inarigivir on viral polymerase binding to pgRNA and DNA synthesis catalyzed by polymerase within the capsid.
Main results
Inarigivir inhibited HBV DNA replication in a dose-dependent manner without affecting HBV RNA levels or pgRNA encapsidation. HBV RNA immunoprecipitation assays revealed no effect on viral polymerase binding to pgRNA, while EPR assays demonstrated inhibition of HBV DNA synthesis catalyzed by polymerase within the capsid.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that Inarigivir's antiviral effect against HBV is independent of the RIG-I signalling pathway, highlighting a novel mechanism of action as a dinucleosidic non-chain-terminating reverse transcriptase inhibitor.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the development of new therapeutic strategies for HBV infection, particularly for targeting DNA replication without relying on innate immune pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro analyses, and the evidence is based on cell line models, which may not fully represent human liver physiology. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of clinical trial data on the mechanism of action.

日本下关牙科保健工作者乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率和与 疫苗 吸收相关的因素

Hepatitis B Vaccination Coverage and Factors Associated with Vaccine Uptake Among Dental Healthcare Workers in Shimonoseki, Japan
作者： Yumiko Nagao, Kiyohide Tomooka
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:牙科保健工作者 (DHW) 由于职业暴露而面临 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的高风险。尽管乙型肝炎 (HB) 疫苗接种 在日本得到推荐,但 疫苗接种 的覆盖范围和与 疫苗 摄取相关的因素仍不清楚。本研究旨在澄清 HB 疫苗接种 覆盖率,确定与 疫苗 吸收相关的因素,并探讨日本下关地区 DHW 中非 疫苗接种 的原因。方法:对下关牙科协会下属的 470 名 DHW 进行了一项横断面网络调查。收集了有关人口统计、HBV/丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 检测、针刺伤、疫苗接种 状态以及非 疫苗接种 原因的数据。使用卡方或费舍尔精确检验分析探索性未调整的组间差异。结果:在受邀的 470 名生活卫生工作者中,有 66 名参与(回应率为 14.0%)。受访者中,50.0%有HB 疫苗接种病史;然而,只有 22.7% 完成了三剂系列。牙科工作人员的完成率显着低于牙医(6.3% vs. 疫苗接种)与 HBV 相关教育 (p < 0.001)、HBV/HCV 检测史 (HBV p < 0.001;HCV p < 0.001) 和机构政策 (p = 0.0363) 相关,但与针刺伤无关。在 27 名未接种疫苗的受访者中,最常报告的原因是结论:在受访者中,自我报告的三剂 HB 疫苗接种 完成率较低,特别是在牙科工作人员中,疫苗接种 状态与教育程度、既往 HBV/HCV 检测和报告的工作场所 疫苗接种 政策相关,但未观察到与自我报告的针刺伤史相关。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,以提高牙科保健工作者中的 HBV 疫苗接种 率,特别是通过解决教育差距和机构政策。

Hepatitis B Vaccination Coverage and Factors Associated with Vaccine Uptake Among Dental Healthcare Workers in Shimonoseki, Japan

Authors: Yumiko Nagao, Kiyohide Tomooka
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Dental healthcare workers (DHWs) are at high risk of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection due to occupational exposure. Although hepatitis B (HB) vaccination is recommended in Japan, vaccination coverage and factors associated with vaccine uptake remain unclear. This study aimed to clarify HB vaccination coverage, identify factors associated with vaccine uptake, and explore reasons for non-vaccination among DHWs in Shimonoseki, Japan. Methods: A cross-sectional web-based survey was conducted among 470 DHWs affiliated with the Shimonoseki Dental Association. Data on demographics, HBV/hepatitis C virus (HCV) testing, needlestick injuries, vaccination status, and reasons for non-vaccination were collected. Exploratory unadjusted group differences were analyzed using Chi-square or Fisher’s exact tests. Results: Of the 470 DHWs invited, 66 participated (response rate, 14.0%). Among respondents, 50.0% had a history of HB vaccination; however, only 22.7% had completed the three-dose series. Completion was significantly lower among dental staff than dentists (6.3% vs. Vaccination was associated with HBV-related education (p < 0.001), HBV/HCV testing history (HBV p < 0.001; HCV p < 0.001), and institutional policies (p = 0.0363), but not with needlestick injuries. Among the 27 unvaccinated respondents, the most frequently reported reason for non-vaccination was lack of personal recommendation. Conclusions: Among respondents, self-reported three-dose HB vaccination completion was low, particularly among dental staff. Vaccination status was associated with educational exposure, previous HBV/HCV testing, and reported workplace vaccination policies, whereas no association with self-reported needlestick injury history was observed.

Question and background
Dental healthcare workers (DHWs) are at high risk of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection due to occupational exposure. The study aimed to address the lack of clarity regarding HBV vaccination coverage and factors influencing vaccine uptake among DHWs in Shimonoseki, Japan.
Design and population
A cross-sectional web-based survey was conducted among 470 DHWs affiliated with the Shimonoseki Dental Association. Of these, 66 participated, resulting in a response rate of 14.0%. The study focused on DHWs in Shimonoseki, Japan.
Core methods
The study collected data on demographics, HBV/hepatitis C virus (HCV) testing, needlestick injuries, vaccination status, and reasons for non-vaccination. Exploratory unadjusted group differences were analyzed using Chi-square or Fisher’s exact tests.
Main results
Of the 470 DHWs invited, 66 participated (response rate, 14.0%). Among respondents, 50.0% had a history of HB vaccination; however, only 22.7% had completed the three-dose series. Completion was significantly lower among dental staff than dentists (6.3% vs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that self-reported three-dose HB vaccination completion was low, particularly among dental staff. The study highlights the association between vaccination status and educational exposure, previous HBV/HCV testing, and reported workplace vaccination policies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform targeted interventions to improve HBV vaccination rates among dental healthcare workers, particularly by addressing educational gaps and institutional policies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study has a low response rate, which may limit the generalizability of findings. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on self-reported data and the lack of detailed follow-up.

印度牙科学生的乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 覆盖率 – 系统回顾和荟萃分析

Hepatitis B vaccination coverage among Indian dental students – Systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Deepika Chenna, Abhinav N Tadikonda, Mithun BH Pai, Maanissa Phukan, Danica Alicia Menezes, Kalyana Chakravarthy Pentapati
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目的 估算印度牙科学生乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 (HBV) 覆盖率的汇总估计值。
设计与对象
本次系统评价是根据 PRISMA 指南进行的。总共纳入了 24 项报告 HBV 的研究。(样本总数:8740(年龄范围:18-27 岁)。
核心方法
通过 OpenMeta [Analyst](采用限制最大似然法的随机效应模型)进行荟萃分析。截至 2025 年 7 月 1 日,在 PubMed(1946 年起)、CINAHL(1937 年起)、Dentistry and Oral Sciences Source(期刊特定覆盖)、Embase(1947 年起)、Web of Science(1900 年起)和 Scopus(1970 年起)中进行了系统检索。
主要结果
The pooled estimate of complete HBV coverage was 53.9% (95%CI: 41.84, 65.97), with high heterogeneity (I 2 = 99.7). Monitoring of the antibody titer was reported in only 5 studies ( n = 504). However, studies on clinical dental students reported higher estimates than studies that combined preclinical and clinical students (coefficient: 0.37; P < 0.01).
解释与创新
作者将这一发现解释为表明大约一半的牙科学生尚未完成三剂乙肝疫苗的注射。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为旨在提高印度牙科学生乙肝病毒覆盖率的有针对性的 疫苗接种 计划和公共卫生举措提供信息。
局限与证据强度
荟萃回归显示年龄或性别分布对 HBV 覆盖率的估计没有显着影响。Egger 回归显示没有发表偏倚(Z = 1.73;P = 0.08)。由于异质性高且抗体滴度监测有限,证据强度中等。

Hepatitis B vaccination coverage among Indian dental students – Systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Deepika Chenna, Abhinav N Tadikonda, Mithun BH Pai, Maanissa Phukan, Danica Alicia Menezes, Kalyana Chakravarthy Pentapati
Original abstract (English)
Objective To estimate the pooled estimates of hepatitis B vaccination (HBV) coverage among Indian dental students. Methods This systematic review was conducted in accordance with the PRISMA guidelines. The protocol was registered in PROSPERO. A systematic search was conducted in PubMed (1946 onward), CINAHL (1937 onward), Dentistry and Oral Sciences Source (journal-specific coverage), Embase (1947 onward), Web of Science (1900 onward), and Scopus (1970 onward) till July 1st, 2025. Cross-sectional studies conducted among Indian dental students that reported HBV coverage and studies reported in English were included. The meta-analysis was performed via OpenMeta [Analyst] (random effects model with the restricted maximum likelihood method). Results A total of 24 studies that reported the HBV were included. (total sample: 8740 (age range: 18–27 years). The pooled estimate of complete HBV coverage was 53.9% (95%CI: 41.84, 65.97), with high heterogeneity (I 2 = 99.7). Monitoring of the antibody titer was reported in only 5 studies ( n = 504). Egger's regression revealed no publication bias (Z = 1.73; P = 0.08). Meta-regression revealed no significant effect of age or sex distribution on the estimates of HBV coverage. However, studies on clinical dental students reported higher estimates than studies that combined preclinical and clinical students (coefficient: 0.37; P < 0.01). Conclusion Approximately half of the dental students had not completed the three-dose HBV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Objective To estimate the pooled estimates of hepatitis B vaccination (HBV) coverage among Indian dental students.
Design and population
This systematic review was conducted in accordance with the PRISMA guidelines. A total of 24 studies that reported the HBV were included. (total sample: 8740 (age range: 18–27 years).
Core methods
The meta-analysis was performed via OpenMeta [Analyst] (random effects model with the restricted maximum likelihood method). A systematic search was conducted in PubMed (1946 onward), CINAHL (1937 onward), Dentistry and Oral Sciences Source (journal-specific coverage), Embase (1947 onward), Web of Science (1900 onward), and Scopus (1970 onward) till July 1st, 2025.
Main results
The pooled estimate of complete HBV coverage was 53.9% (95%CI: 41.84, 65.97), with high heterogeneity (I 2 = 99.7). Monitoring of the antibody titer was reported in only 5 studies ( n = 504). However, studies on clinical dental students reported higher estimates than studies that combined preclinical and clinical students (coefficient: 0.37; P < 0.01).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that approximately half of the dental students had not completed the three-dose HBV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted vaccination programs and public health initiatives aimed at improving HBV coverage among dental students in India.
Limitations and evidence strength
Meta-regression revealed no significant effect of age or sex distribution on the estimates of HBV coverage. Egger's regression revealed no publication bias (Z = 1.73; P = 0.08). The evidence strength is moderate due to high heterogeneity and limited monitoring of antibody titer.

血清学-自我报告 疫苗接种 的锚定比较:报告的乙型肝炎系列完成率超过 疫苗-同一参与者的一致免疫力

Serology-Anchored Comparison of Self-Reported Vaccination: Reported Hepatitis B Series Completion Exceeds Vaccine-Consistent Immunity in the Same Participants
作者： Irina Naskinova, Mikhail Kolev, Hristo Kalinov, Meglena D. Lazarova, Velin Klisurov
摘要中文翻译大量二手文献从国家健康和营养检查调查 (NHANES) 中提取了成人 疫苗接种 状态,但其准确性很少与之一起量化。本文的目的是量化同一参与者的准确性,因为其大小和方向决定这些项目是否可以支持承保索赔。我们使用的方法汇总了 10 个 NHANES 周期(1999-2018 年,成人 ≥ 20 岁;疫苗 分析 n 13,992-48,959),并将自我报告的完整系列吸收与人体内生物学参考进行比较。该参考仅适用于 2015-2016 年的乙型肝炎,并不能验证甲型肝炎或人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的估计值。我们的结果是,在采用这两种措施的 4473 名受访者中,自我报告的 疫苗接种 比率(33.5%,95% CI:30.5–36.5%)比 疫苗 一致的 血清学(22.6%,19.9–25.2%)高出 10.9 个百分点(7.5–14.3),比率为1.48(1.30–1.67)。他们在两个方向上都存在分歧,845 人报告了该系列但没有血清学特征,294 人携带了特征但没有报告,因此差异并不是过度报告的衡量标准。经过直接测试,该倍数在性别、年龄和种族中几乎恒定。已发布的国家健康访谈调查覆盖范围与剂量定义、人口和年份相匹配,给出的比率高达 3.00。四位调整后的估计者将常规护理来源对乙型肝炎的影响定为 5.7-6.5 个百分点,对 HPV 的影响为 2.5-3.2 个百分点,其中三位排除了零和因果森林覆盖它的更广泛区间。得出的结论是,这两种衡量标准不可互换,并且 NHANES 数据应解读为报告的采用率,而不是覆盖率 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在量化国家健康和营养检查调查 (NHANES) 中自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态与 乙型肝炎病毒 的准确性,因为该数据经常使用但很少经过验证。
设计与对象
该研究汇集了十个 NHANES 周期(1999-2018),重点关注 20 岁以上的成年人,疫苗 的分析样本量为 13,992-48,959,并将自我报告的 疫苗接种 与生物 血清学 数据进行比较。
核心方法
这些方法涉及将自我报告的 疫苗接种 率与乙型肝炎的生物 血清学 数据进行比较,使用内部参考来评估自我报告数据的准确性。
主要结果
在采用这两种措施的 4473 名受访者中,自我报告的 疫苗接种 率(33.5%,95% CI:30.5–36.5%)比 疫苗 一致的 血清学(22.6%,19.9–25.2%)高 10.9 个百分点(7.5–14.3),比例为1.48(1.30–1.67)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,这两种衡量标准不可互换,并且 NHANES 数据应解释为报告的采用率,而不是覆盖率 监测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告不要使用自我报告的 疫苗接种 数据作为实际免疫力的代理,这对公共卫生 监测 和有关乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 计划的政策决策具有影响。
局限与证据强度
研究发现,性别、年龄和种族之间的差异是一致的,表明倍数接近恒定,但参考数据仅限于 2015-2016 年,这可能会影响普遍性。

Serology-Anchored Comparison of Self-Reported Vaccination: Reported Hepatitis B Series Completion Exceeds Vaccine-Consistent Immunity in the Same Participants

Authors: Irina Naskinova, Mikhail Kolev, Hristo Kalinov, Meglena D. Lazarova, Velin Klisurov
Original abstract (English)
A large secondary literature draws adult vaccination status from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES), whose accuracy is rarely quantified alongside it. The objective of this article is to quantify that accuracy in the same participants, because its size and direction decide whether these items can support a coverage claim. The methods we used pooled ten NHANES cycles (1999–2018, adults ≥ 20 years; analytic n 13,992–48,959 by vaccine) and compared self-reported, completed-series uptake with a within-person biological reference. That reference exists for hepatitis B in 2015–2016 only and does not validate the hepatitis A or human papillomavirus (HPV) estimates. Our results are that among the 4473 respondents with both measures, self-reported vaccination rates (33.5%, 95% CI: 30.5–36.5%) stood 10.9 percentage points (7.5–14.3) above vaccine-consistent serology (22.6%, 19.9–25.2%), a ratio of 1.48 (1.30–1.67). They disagreed in both directions, 845 reporting the series without the serologic profile and 294 carrying the profile without reporting it, so the difference is not a measure of over-reporting. Tested directly, it was a near-constant multiple across sex, age, and race. Published National Health Interview Survey coverage, matched on dose definition, population, and year, gave ratios up to 3.00. Four adjusted estimators placed the effect of a usual source of care at 5.7–6.5 percentage points for hepatitis B and 2.5–3.2 for HPV, three excluding zero and the causal forest’s wider interval covering it. The conclusions drawn are that the two measures are not interchangeable and that NHANES figures should be read as reported uptake, not coverage surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to quantify the accuracy of self-reported vaccination status against hepatitis B virus in the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES), as this data is frequently used but rarely validated.
Design and population
The study pooled ten NHANES cycles (1999–2018) focusing on adults aged ≥ 20 years, with an analytic sample size of 13,992–48,959 by vaccine, and compared self-reported vaccination with biological serology data.
Core methods
The methods involved comparing self-reported vaccination rates with biological serology data for hepatitis B, using a within-person reference to assess the accuracy of self-reported data.
Main results
Among 4473 respondents with both measures, self-reported vaccination rates (33.5%, 95% CI: 30.5–36.5%) were 10.9 percentage points (7.5–14.3) higher than vaccine-consistent serology (22.6%, 19.9–25.2%), with a ratio of 1.48 (1.30–1.67).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the two measures are not interchangeable, and that NHANES figures should be interpreted as reported uptake rather than as coverage surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution against using self-reported vaccination data as a proxy for actual immunity, which has implications for public health surveillance and policy decisions regarding hepatitis B vaccination programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study found that the discrepancy was consistent across sex, age, and race, suggesting a near-constant multiple, but the reference data was limited to 2015–2016, which may affect generalisability.

沙特阿拉伯高危人群乙型和丙型肝炎筛查覆盖率:监测 数据的全国分析以及与世界卫生组织病毒性肝炎消除目标(2016-2023 年)的一致性

Hepatitis B and C Screening Coverage among High-risk Populations in Saudi Arabia: A National Analysis of Surveillance Data and Alignment with the World Health Organization Viral Hepatitis Elimination Targets (2016–2023)
作者： Ahmed Abdulaziz Almohammadi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Viral hepatitis B and C represent major global public health challenges, collectively responsible for approximately 1.1 million deaths annually. The study aimed to address the lack of comprehensive analyses on screening coverage among high-risk populations in Saudi Arabia.
设计与对象
这项横断面描述性研究分析了来自世界卫生组织全球卫生观察站估计、沙特卫生部 监测 报告以及已发布的流行病学调查(2016-2023 年)的二手数据。该研究重点关注医护人员、献血者、囚犯、血液透析患者和孕妇等高危人群。
核心方法
描述性统计数据,包括 95% 置信区间 (CI),按人口组、地理区域和时间趋势描述了覆盖模式。Comparative analysis assessed alignment with the WHO GHSS targets.
主要结果
2023年,高危人群乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)检测总体覆盖率达到74.3%(95% CI:74.2-74.4),丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)检测覆盖率达到68.9%(95% CI:68.8-69.0)。献血者筛查几乎实现了全民覆盖(HBsAg 和抗 HCV 均为 99.8%,95% CI:99.79–99.81)。医护人员筛查显示 HBsAg 覆盖率为 81.4%(95% CI:81.3–81.5),抗 HCV 覆盖率为 76.2%(95% CI:76.0–76.4)。血液透析患者的 HBsAg 筛查达到 94.7%(95% CI:94.4–95.0)。全国 HBsAg 产前筛查率达到 89.3%(95% CI:89.2–89.4)。然而,囚犯的 HBsAg 筛查率仍然最低,为 41.2%(95% CI:40.8–41.6),抗 HCV 筛查率为 38.7%(95% CI:38.4–39.0)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,沙特阿拉伯在临床高危人群中实现了强大的筛查覆盖率,但在被监禁人群和边境地区仍然存在严重差距。这项研究对符合世卫组织消除目标的筛查覆盖率进行了全国分析,强调了有针对性的干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要有针对性地扩大筛查计划、与初级卫生保健相结合,并加强与护理的联系,以实现世卫组织 2030 年消除病毒性肝炎的目标。他们还强调了解决地区差异和改善弱势群体筛查的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于二手数据,不包括直接测量或干预措施。证据强度中等,因为结果来自描述性统计和现有报告的比较分析。

Hepatitis B and C Screening Coverage among High-risk Populations in Saudi Arabia: A National Analysis of Surveillance Data and Alignment with the World Health Organization Viral Hepatitis Elimination Targets (2016–2023)

Authors: Ahmed Abdulaziz Almohammadi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Viral hepatitis B and C represent major global public health challenges, collectively responsible for approximately 1.1 million deaths annually. Saudi Arabia has historically demonstrated endemic hepatitis B transmission and carries a significant hepatitis C burden, yet comprehensive analyses of screening coverage among high-risk populations remain limited. Objectives: The objectives of the study were to characterize hepatitis B and C screening coverage patterns among high-risk populations in Saudi Arabia and evaluate alignment with the World Health Organization (WHO) Global Health Sector Strategy (GHSS) targets for viral hepatitis elimination by 2030. Materials and Methods: This cross-sectional descriptive study analyzed secondary data from the WHO Global Health Observatory estimates, Saudi Ministry of Health surveillance reports, and published epidemiological surveys (2016–2023). Screening coverage was assessed among defined high-risk groups including healthcare workers, blood donors, prisoners, patients on hemodialysis, and pregnant women. Descriptive statistics, including 95% confidence intervals (CIs), characterized coverage patterns by population group, geographic region, and temporal trends. Comparative analysis assessed alignment with the WHO GHSS targets. Results: Overall hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) testing coverage among high-risk populations reached 74.3% (95% CI: 74.2–74.4) in 2023, while anti-hepatitis C virus (HCV) testing coverage reached 68.9% (95% CI: 68.8–69.0). Blood donor screening achieved near-universal coverage (99.8%, 95% CI: 99.79–99.81, for both HBsAg and anti-HCV). Healthcare worker screening demonstrated 81.4% (95% CI: 81.3–81.5) HBsAg and 76.2% (95% CI: 76.0–76.4) anti-HCV coverage. Hemodialysis patient screening reached 94.7% (95% CI: 94.4–95.0) for HBsAg. Antenatal screening for HBsAg achieved 89.3% (95% CI: 89.2–89.4) nationally. However, prisoner screening remained lowest at 41.2% (95% CI: 40.8–41.6) for HBsAg and 38.7% (95% CI: 38.4–39.0) for anti-HCV. Regional disparities persisted, with border regions demonstrating 15–22 percentage point gaps below national averages. Temporal analysis revealed a modest improvement from 61.4% (95% CI: 61.3–61.5) in 2016 to 74.3% (95% CI: 74.2–74.4) in 2023 for HBsAg screening overall. Conclusions: Saudi Arabia has achieved strong screening coverage in clinical high-risk groups, but critical gaps persist among incarcerated populations and in border regions. Targeted expansion of screening programs, integration with primary health care, and strengthened linkage to care are essential to achieve the WHO 2030 viral hepatitis elimination targets.

Question and background
Viral hepatitis B and C represent major global public health challenges, collectively responsible for approximately 1.1 million deaths annually. The study aimed to address the lack of comprehensive analyses on screening coverage among high-risk populations in Saudi Arabia.
Design and population
This cross-sectional descriptive study analyzed secondary data from the WHO Global Health Observatory estimates, Saudi Ministry of Health surveillance reports, and published epidemiological surveys (2016–2023). The study focused on high-risk populations such as healthcare workers, blood donors, prisoners, hemodialysis patients, and pregnant women.
Core methods
Descriptive statistics, including 95% confidence intervals (CIs), characterized coverage patterns by population group, geographic region, and temporal trends. Comparative analysis assessed alignment with the WHO GHSS targets.
Main results
Overall hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) testing coverage among high-risk populations reached 74.3% (95% CI: 74.2–74.4) in 2023, while anti-hepatitis C virus (HCV) testing coverage reached 68.9% (95% CI: 68.8–69.0). Blood donor screening achieved near-universal coverage (99.8%, 95% CI: 99.79–99.81, for both HBsAg and anti-HCV). Healthcare worker screening demonstrated 81.4% (95% CI: 81.3–81.5) HBsAg and 76.2% (95% CI: 76.0–76.4) anti-HCV coverage. Hemodialysis patient screening reached 94.7% (95% CI: 94.4–95.0) for HBsAg. Antenatal screening for HBsAg achieved 89.3% (95% CI: 89.2–89.4) nationally. However, prisoner screening remained lowest at 41.2% (95% CI: 40.8–41.6) for HBsAg and 38.7% (95% CI: 38.4–39.0) for anti-HCV.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that Saudi Arabia has achieved strong screening coverage in clinical high-risk groups, but critical gaps persist among incarcerated populations and in border regions. This study provides a national analysis of screening coverage aligned with WHO elimination targets, highlighting the need for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted expansion of screening programs, integration with primary health care, and strengthened linkage to care to achieve the WHO 2030 viral hepatitis elimination targets. They also highlight the importance of addressing regional disparities and improving screening among vulnerable populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on secondary data and does not include direct measurements or interventions. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are derived from descriptive statistics and comparative analysis of existing reports.

尼日利亚高原州乔斯惩教中心囚犯的乙型肝炎表面抗原和丁型肝炎抗体阳性

Hepatitis B Surface Antigen and Hepatitis D Antibody Positivity among Inmates of Jos Correctional Centre, Plateau State, Nigeria
作者： Ajala Ese Kate, Cosmas Nanma Tongnan, Chindah Evelyn Chingarum, Abdulrazaq Madinah, Ezekiel Tamar Amos, Jila Queen Torkwase, Abel, Callum Ndubuisi, Chingarum Evelyn Chindah, Callum N. Abel
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 共享 传播 途径,并且持续的 HDV 复制需要 HBV 表面抗原。来自尼日利亚惩教设施的证据仍然有限。这项研究评估了乔斯惩教中心囚犯的乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和抗 HDV 抗体阳性情况。方法:在一项横断面血清学调查中,使用酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 对 235 名被监禁参与者的血浆样本进行了 HBsAg 检测。随后通过 ELISA 检测 HBsAg 反应性样本的抗 HDV 抗体。计算描述频率和 Wilson 95% 置信区间 (CI);Fisher 精确检验用于选定的性别和暴露分层 HBsAg 比较。结果:235 名参与者中有 43 名检测到 HBsAg(18.3%;95% CI:13.9–23.7%)。43 名 HBsAg 阳性参与者中有 5 名抗 HDV 阳性(11.6%;95% CI:5.1-24.5%)。男性 HBsAg 阳性率为 42/227 (18.5%),女性为 1/8 (12.5%)(Fisher 精确 p = 1.00)。5 名男性出现抗丁型肝炎病毒阳性,无女性;只有一名 HBsAg 阳性女性接受了抗 HDV 筛查。较小的亚组计数排除了可靠的风险因素推断。结论:在该矫正人群中观察到 HBsAg 和抗 HDV 抗体阳性。研究结果支持进行 HBV 筛查、易感个体的 疫苗接种、HBsAg 检测后的 HDV 检测以及与临床评估的联系。仅抗 HDV 阳性并不能确定活动性 HDV 感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 共享 传播 途径,并且持续的 HDV 复制需要 HBV 表面抗原。来自尼日利亚惩教设施的证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
这项研究评估了乔斯惩教中心囚犯的乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和抗 HDV 抗体阳性情况。235 名参与者中有 43 名检测到 HBsAg(18.3%;95% CI:13.9–23.7%)。

主要结果
43 名 HBsAg 阳性参与者中有 5 名抗 HDV 阳性(11.6%;95% CI:5.1-24.5%)。男性 HBsAg 阳性率为 42/227 (18.5%),女性为 1/8 (12.5%)(Fisher 精确 p = 1.00)。5 名男性出现抗丁型肝炎病毒阳性,无女性;只有一名 HBsAg 阳性女性接受了抗 HDV 筛查。
解释与创新
在该矫正人群中观察到 HBsAg 和抗 HDV 抗体阳性。仅抗 HDV 阳性并不能确定活动性 HDV 感染。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持进行 HBV 筛查、易感个体的 疫苗接种、HBsAg 检测后的 HDV 检测以及与临床评估的联系。
局限与证据强度
较小的亚组计数排除了可靠的风险因素推断。由于该研究受到横断面设计和小样本量的限制,因此证据是中等的。

Hepatitis B Surface Antigen and Hepatitis D Antibody Positivity among Inmates of Jos Correctional Centre, Plateau State, Nigeria

Authors: Ajala Ese Kate, Cosmas Nanma Tongnan, Chindah Evelyn Chingarum, Abdulrazaq Madinah, Ezekiel Tamar Amos, Jila Queen Torkwase, Abel, Callum Ndubuisi, Chingarum Evelyn Chindah, Callum N. Abel
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) share transmission pathways, and ongoing HDV replication requires HBV surface antigen. Evidence from correctional facilities in Nigeria remains limited. This study assessed hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and anti-HDV antibody positivity among inmates of Jos Correctional Centre. Methods: In a cross-sectional serological survey, plasma samples from 235 incarcerated participants was tested for HBsAg using an enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA). HBsAg-reactive samples were subsequently tested for anti-HDV antibodies by ELISA. Descriptive frequencies and Wilson 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were calculated; Fisher’s exact test was used for selected sex- and exposure-stratified HBsAg comparisons. Results: HBsAg was detected in 43 of 235 participants (18.3%; 95% CI: 13.9–23.7%). Five of the 43 HBsAg-positive participants were anti-HDV positive (11.6%; 95% CI: 5.1–24.5%). HBsAg positivity was 42/227 (18.5%) among men and 1/8 (12.5%) among women (Fisher’s exact p = 1.00). Anti-HDV positivity occurred in five men and no women; only one HBsAg-positive woman was screened for anti-HDV. Small subgroup counts precluded reliable risk-factor inference. Conclusion: HBsAg and anti-HDV antibody positivity were observed in this correctional population. The findings support access to HBV screening, vaccination of susceptible individuals, HDV testing after HBsAg detection, and linkage to clinical assessment. Anti-HDV positivity alone does not establish active HDV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) share transmission pathways, and ongoing HDV replication requires HBV surface antigen. Evidence from correctional facilities in Nigeria remains limited.

XT1061(一种 HBV 衣壳组装调节剂)的发现,作为 HBV 感染的临床 治疗 有希望的治疗候选药物

Discovery of XT1061, a HBV Capsid Assembly Modulator, as a Promising Therapeutic Candidate for Clinical Treatment of HBV Infection
作者： Weibo Guo, Chenghao Wang, Chi Zhang, Kangjia Sheng, Dengke Zhang, Xiuhong Wen, Yuan Wang, Weidong Yue, Yungen Xu
摘要中文翻译摘要 乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)仍然是一种尚未解决的主要传染病。患者通常需要终生服用核苷(酸)类似物(NA)或干扰素,但很少达到功能性治愈。最近的研究表明,HBV核心组装调节剂(CAM)与NAs和干扰素联合使用时,可以更有效地降低血浆中的HBsAg,从而促进功能性治愈。在这项研究中,我们通过之前报道的GLP-26的结构优化发现了化合物39(命名为XT1061),其在体外表现出有效的抗HBV活性(EC50 = 1.0 ± 0.1 nM)。在小鼠 AAV/HBV 模型中,XT1061 诱导 HBV DNA 快速减少,并在整个 治疗 期间持续清除病毒 DNA。值得注意的是,XT1061 与甲磺酸普拉德福韦 (PDV) 的联合治疗与单一疗法相比显示出显着增强的疗效。进一步的临床前评估和I期临床试验(NCT06280534)证实XT1061具有良好的药物样特性和较低的安全风险。总的来说,XT1061代表了一种有前途的抗乙型肝炎治疗候选药物,为慢性乙型肝炎感染的临床管理提供了可行的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于当前 治疗 无法实现 功能性治愈 的局限性,该研究旨在解决慢性 HBV 感染有效候选治疗药物的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及化合物 XT1061 的体外和小鼠 AAV/HBV 模型的发现和评估。
核心方法
XT1061通过GLP-26的结构优化得到鉴定,并在体外和体内测试了抗HBV活性。
主要结果
XT1061 在体外表现出有效的抗 HBV 活性(EC50 = 1.0 ± 0.1 nM),并在小鼠 AAV/HBV 模型中诱导 HBV DNA 快速减少,并在整个 治疗 期间持续清除病毒 DNA。XT1061和甲磺酸普拉德福韦的联合治疗显示出显着增强的疗效。
解释与创新
作者解释说,XT1061 代表了一种有前途的慢性 HBV 感染治疗候选药物,与其他 治疗 联合使用时,有可能促进 功能性治愈 的发展。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,XT1061 可以为慢性 HBV 感染的临床管理提供可行的策略,有可能导致 功能性治愈。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其临床前性质以及需要在更大、更多样化的人群中进行进一步评估而受到限制。由于缺乏人体临床试验数据,证据强度中等。

Discovery of XT1061, a HBV Capsid Assembly Modulator, as a Promising Therapeutic Candidate for Clinical Treatment of HBV Infection

Authors: Weibo Guo, Chenghao Wang, Chi Zhang, Kangjia Sheng, Dengke Zhang, Xiuhong Wen, Yuan Wang, Weidong Yue, Yungen Xu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Hepatitis B virus (HBV) persists as a major unresolved infectious disease. Patients typically require lifelong administration of nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) or interferons but seldom achieve functional cure. Recent studies indicate that HBV core assembly modulators (CAMs), when combined with NAs and interferons, can more effectively reduce HBsAg in plasma, thereby facilitating functional cure. In this research, we discovered compound 39 (designated XT1061) through structural optimization of previously reported GLP-26, which exhibited potent anti-HBV activity in vitro (EC50 = 1.0 ± 0.1 nM). In the murine AAV/HBV model, XT1061 induced rapid reduction of HBV DNA and sustained viral DNA clearance throughout treatment. Notably, Combination therapy of XT1061 with pradefovir mesylate (PDV) exhibited significantly enhanced efficacy compared with monotherapy. Further preclinical evaluation and Phase I clinical trials (NCT06280534) confirmed that XT1061 possesses favorable drug-like properties and low safety risks. Collectively, XT1061 represents a promising anti-HBV therapeutic candidate, providing a viable strategy for the clinical management of chronic HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for effective therapeutic candidates for chronic HBV infection, given the limitations of current treatments which do not achieve functional cure.
Design and population
The research involved the discovery and evaluation of compound XT1061 in vitro and in a murine AAV/HBV model.
Core methods
XT1061 was identified through structural optimization of GLP-26 and tested for anti-HBV activity in vitro and in vivo.
Main results
XT1061 exhibited potent anti-HBV activity in vitro (EC50 = 1.0 ± 0.1 nM) and induced rapid reduction of HBV DNA in the murine AAV/HBV model, with sustained viral DNA clearance throughout treatment. Combination therapy with XT1061 and pradefovir mesylate showed significantly enhanced efficacy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that XT1061 represents a promising therapeutic candidate for chronic HBV infection, with potential to facilitate functional cure when combined with other treatments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that XT1061 could provide a viable strategy for the clinical management of chronic HBV infection, potentially leading to functional cure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its preclinical nature and the need for further evaluation in larger, more diverse populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of human clinical trial data.

韩国全国范围内的乙型和丙型肝炎感染与年龄相关性黄斑变性的风险 队列研究

Hepatitis B and C infections and risk of age related macular degeneration in a nationwide Korean cohort
作者： Kyung Jun Choi, Hyeon Cheol Roh, Woong-Sub Park, Sang-Wook Yi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查韩国 队列研究 人群中 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 感染是否与随后发生的年龄相关性黄斑变性 (AMD) 相关。摘要 关于病毒性肝炎和 AMD 的证据仍然有限,特别是来自长期前瞻性研究的证据。
设计与对象
一项全国性的 队列研究 研究利用国民健康保险服务数据进行,包括 12,386,563 名 40 岁以上成年人,他们在 2009 年至 2010 年期间接受了健康检查,并随访至 2023 年。在随访期间,388,652 名参与者出现了 AMD 事件。
核心方法
使用诊断代码来识别乙型肝炎病毒和丙型肝炎病毒感染。使用罕见难治性疾病注册代码将事件 AMD 分类为渗出性,使用韩国疾病分类代码将事件 AMD 分类为非渗出性。使用多变量 Cox 比例风险模型来评估相关性。
主要结果
In multivariable Cox proportional hazards models, HBV infection was associated with increased risks of exudative AMD (hazard ratio [HR] 1.18, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.05–1.32) and nonexudative AMD (HR 1.68, 95% CI 1.60–1.76). HCV infection was also associated with increased risks of exudative AMD (HR 1.53, 95% CI 1.27–1.86) and nonexudative AMD (HR 1.41, 95% CI 1.27–1.57).
解释与创新
作者将这一发现解释为表明 HBV 和 HCV 感染与渗出性和非渗出性 AMD 风险增加相关。这是一项将病毒性肝炎与 AMD 联系起来的新观察。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,病毒性肝炎感染可能会增加AMD的风险,这可以为未来的监测和肝炎高发人群的AMD预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖诊断代码来识别感染和 AMD,这可能会引入错误分类偏差。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

Hepatitis B and C infections and risk of age related macular degeneration in a nationwide Korean cohort

Authors: Kyung Jun Choi, Hyeon Cheol Roh, Woong-Sub Park, Sang-Wook Yi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Evidence on viral hepatitis and age-related macular degeneration (AMD) remains limited, particularly from long-term prospective studies. We investigated whether hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) infections are associated with the subsequent development of AMD in a nationwide Korean cohort. Using National Health Insurance Service data, we included 12,386,563 adults aged ≥ 40 years who underwent health examinations in 2009–2010 and were followed through 2023. HBV and HCV infections were identified using diagnostic codes. Incident AMD was classified as exudative using Rare Intractable Disease registration codes and as nonexudative using Korean Classification of Diseases codes. During follow-up, 388,652 participants developed incident AMD. In multivariable Cox proportional hazards models, HBV infection was associated with increased risks of exudative AMD (hazard ratio [HR] 1.18, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.05–1.32) and nonexudative AMD (HR 1.68, 95% CI 1.60–1.76). HCV infection was also associated with increased risks of exudative AMD (HR 1.53, 95% CI 1.27–1.86) and nonexudative AMD (HR 1.41, 95% CI 1.27–1.57). Associations appeared stronger in women in sex-stratified analyses. HBV and HCV infections were associated with increased risks of both exudative and nonexudative AMD.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) infections are associated with the subsequent development of age-related macular degeneration (AMD) in a nationwide Korean cohort. Abstract Evidence on viral hepatitis and AMD remains limited, particularly from long-term prospective studies.
Design and population
A nationwide cohort study was conducted using National Health Insurance Service data, including 12,386,563 adults aged ≥ 40 years who underwent health examinations in 2009–2010 and were followed through 2023. During follow-up, 388,652 participants developed incident AMD.
Core methods
HBV and HCV infections were identified using diagnostic codes. Incident AMD was classified as exudative using Rare Intractable Disease registration codes and as nonexudative using Korean Classification of Diseases codes. Multivariable Cox proportional hazards models were used to assess the associations.
Main results
In multivariable Cox proportional hazards models, HBV infection was associated with increased risks of exudative AMD (hazard ratio [HR] 1.18, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.05–1.32) and nonexudative AMD (HR 1.68, 95% CI 1.60–1.76). HCV infection was also associated with increased risks of exudative AMD (HR 1.53, 95% CI 1.27–1.86) and nonexudative AMD (HR 1.41, 95% CI 1.27–1.57).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that HBV and HCV infections are associated with increased risks of both exudative and nonexudative AMD. This is a novel observation linking viral hepatitis to AMD.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that viral hepatitis infections may contribute to the risk of AMD, which could inform future surveillance and prevention strategies for AMD in populations with high hepatitis prevalence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on diagnostic codes for identifying infections and AMD, which may introduce misclassification bias. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

慢性乙型肝炎 和脂肪肝病:从双向相互作用到肝细胞癌风险

Chronic Hepatitis B and Steatotic Liver Disease: From Bidirectional Interaction to Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk
作者： Georgiana-Elena Sarbu, Otilia Nedelciuc, Andreea O. Lungu, Alina-Ecaterina Jucan, Carmen Atodiresei, Vasile-Claudiu Mihai, Ionut Iulian Lungu, Ioana Ruxandra Mihai, Mihaela Dranga, Irina Ciortescu
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 和脂肪肝病 (SLD) 是全球最常见的慢性肝病之一,并且越来越多地共存,特别是在 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然流行的地区。报道的慢性乙型肝炎肝脂肪变性的 患病率 差异很大,反映了诊断方式和研究的人群,而不是单一的基础数据。在使用肝活检、超声或受控衰减参数 (CAP) 的系列研究中,CHB 中的肝脂肪变性与 宿主 代谢和遗传因素的相关性比与病毒复制标志物的相关性更一致。肝脂肪变性反复与较低的血清 HBV DNA、较低的乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 定量、较低的乙型肝炎包膜抗原 (HBeAg) 阳性率以及较高的 HBsAg 血清清除概率相关。这些病毒学关联并不能一致地预测长期肝脏结果,并且 HBsAg 血清清除后肝细胞癌 (HCC) 风险仍然存在。使用肝脂肪变性作为暴露的研究报告了 HCC 的不一致结果,调整后的估计范围从反向到强正。代谢功能障碍与 HCC 存在更一致的关联,特别是糖尿病、肥胖和多种并发心脏代谢危险因素,并且这种关联在病毒学抑制的患者和 HBsAg 血清清除后持续存在。因此,肝脂肪变性、代谢功能障碍、脂肪性肝炎和纤维化应作为不同的预后变量而不是作为单一实体进行评估,这对 HCC 风险分层和 CHB 中的 监测 具有影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 和脂肪肝病 (SLD) 是全球最常见的慢性肝病之一,并且越来越多地共存,特别是在 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然流行的地区。
设计与对象
该研究利用肝活检、超声和受控衰减参数 (CAP) 等多种诊断方法的数据,探讨了 慢性乙型肝炎 患者肝脂肪变性与病毒学标志物之间的关联。
核心方法
该研究使用肝活检、超声和受控衰减参数 (CAP) 分析多个来源的数据来评估肝脂肪变性,并将这些结果与血清 HBV DNA、定量乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和乙型肝炎包膜抗原 (HBeAg) 等病毒学标志物相关联。
主要结果
报道的慢性乙型肝炎肝脂肪变性的 患病率 差异很大,反映了诊断方式和研究的人群,而不是单一的基础数据。
解释与创新
这些病毒学关联并不能一致地预测长期肝脏结果,并且 HBsAg 血清清除后肝细胞癌 (HCC) 风险仍然存在。
科研与公卫意义
因此,肝脂肪变性、代谢功能障碍、脂肪性肝炎和纤维化应作为不同的预后变量而不是作为单一实体进行评估,这对 HCC 风险分层和 CHB 中的 监测 具有影响。
局限与证据强度
使用肝脂肪变性作为暴露的研究报告了 HCC 的不一致结果,调整后的估计范围从反向到强正。

Chronic Hepatitis B and Steatotic Liver Disease: From Bidirectional Interaction to Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk

Authors: Georgiana-Elena Sarbu, Otilia Nedelciuc, Andreea O. Lungu, Alina-Ecaterina Jucan, Carmen Atodiresei, Vasile-Claudiu Mihai, Ionut Iulian Lungu, Ioana Ruxandra Mihai, Mihaela Dranga, Irina Ciortescu
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and steatotic liver disease (SLD) are among the most prevalent chronic liver diseases worldwide and increasingly coexist, particularly in regions where hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains endemic. Reported prevalence of hepatic steatosis in CHB varies widely, reflecting the diagnostic modality and population studied rather than a single underlying figure. Across series using liver biopsy, ultrasound or controlled attenuation parameter (CAP), hepatic steatosis in CHB is associated more consistently with host metabolic and genetic factors than with markers of viral replication. Hepatic steatosis is repeatedly associated with lower serum HBV DNA, lower quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), lower hepatitis B envelope antigen (HBeAg) positivity and a higher probability of HBsAg seroclearance. These virological associations do not consistently predict long-term liver outcomes, and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists after HBsAg seroclearance. Studies using hepatic steatosis as the exposure report inconsistent findings for HCC, with adjusted estimates ranging from inverse to strongly positive. Metabolic dysfunction shows a more consistent association with HCC, particularly diabetes, obesity and multiple concurrent cardiometabolic risk factors, and this association persists in virologically suppressed patients and after HBsAg seroclearance. Hepatic steatosis, metabolic dysfunction, steatohepatitis and fibrosis should therefore be assessed as distinct prognostic variables rather than as a single entity, with implications for HCC risk stratification and surveillance in CHB.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and steatotic liver disease (SLD) are among the most prevalent chronic liver diseases worldwide and increasingly coexist, particularly in regions where hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains endemic.
Design and population
The study examined the association between hepatic steatosis and virological markers in patients with chronic hepatitis B, using data from various diagnostic methods including liver biopsy, ultrasound, and controlled attenuation parameter (CAP).
Core methods
The study analyzed data from multiple sources using liver biopsy, ultrasound, and controlled attenuation parameter (CAP) to assess hepatic steatosis, and correlated these findings with virological markers such as serum HBV DNA, quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), and hepatitis B envelope antigen (HBeAg).
Main results
Reported prevalence of hepatic steatosis in CHB varies widely, reflecting the diagnostic modality and population studied rather than a single underlying figure.
Interpretation and novelty
These virological associations do not consistently predict long-term liver outcomes, and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists after HBsAg seroclearance.
Scientific and public-health significance
Hepatic steatosis, metabolic dysfunction, steatohepatitis and fibrosis should therefore be assessed as distinct prognostic variables rather than as a single entity, with implications for HCC risk stratification and surveillance in CHB.
Limitations and evidence strength
Studies using hepatic steatosis as the exposure report inconsistent findings for HCC, with adjusted estimates ranging from inverse to strongly positive.

乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌中 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴超出程序性细胞死亡蛋白 1

CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis beyond programmed cell death protein 1 in hepatitis B virus-associated hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Hai Nguyen Ngoc Dang, Hau Phuc Tran, Hung Minh Vu, Trung Tran Nguyen, Bich Ngoc Hoang Thi, Thanh Van Ho, Adegbesan Abiodun Christopher, Hung Tran Xuan, Thang Viet Luong
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝细胞癌在慢性炎症、耐受性肝脏微环境中发生。在这种情况下,T 细胞功能障碍限制了程序性死亡 1 (PD-1) 阻断的功效。Liu 等人发表在《世界胃肠肿瘤学杂志》上的新证据表明,CD161 可能代表耗尽的 CD8+ T 细胞内的另一个抑制轴,该轴未被 PD-1/程序性死亡配体-1 通路完全捕获。在他们的研究中,CD161+CD8+T细胞在肿瘤组织中富集,即使PD-1+区室在PD-1抑制后收缩,CD161表达仍然持续,这表明CD161介导的抑制可能在治疗压力下保持活跃。从机制上讲,CD161-凝集素样转录物 1 相互作用的持续参与可能会损害 T 细胞效应器功能,并有助于 HBV 感染肝脏中的适应性免疫抵抗。这些发现强调了 CD161 蛋白质水平评估和 PD-1 平行评估对于准确表征免疫抑制的重要性。关键知识差距包括肿瘤微环境中凝集素样转录物 1 的细胞来源、持续的 HBV 病毒学活性对 CD161+ T 细胞亚群的影响,以及临床有意义的进展终点的识别。在这篇社论中,我们将这些发现置于机制和转化背景中,强调了 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴作为 PD-1 阻断之外的免疫抵抗的补充途径的潜在作用。总的来说,当前的证据支持谨慎的、假设驱动的转化策略,以评估精心挑选的 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者中 CD161 和 PD-1 轴的联合调节。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 CD161 在 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 T 细胞功能障碍中的作用,解决当前免疫疗法(例如 PD-1 阻断)的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究旨在调查 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者肿瘤组织中的 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴。
核心方法
该研究涉及分析 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者的肿瘤组织样本,重点关注 CD161 表达及其与 PD-1 的相互作用。
主要结果
CD161+CD8+T细胞在肿瘤组织中富集,即使在PD-1抑制后CD161表达仍然持续,表明持续的免疫抵抗。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CD161 介导的抑制可能在治疗压力下保持活跃,凸显了在 HBV 相关肝细胞癌中评估 CD161 与 PD-1 的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者的 CD161 和 PD-1 轴的联合调节策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
主要局限性包括需要进一步研究凝集素样转录物 1 的细胞来源以及持续的 HBV 病毒学活性对 CD161+ T 细胞亚群的影响。

CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis beyond programmed cell death protein 1 in hepatitis B virus-associated hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Hai Nguyen Ngoc Dang, Hau Phuc Tran, Hung Minh Vu, Trung Tran Nguyen, Bich Ngoc Hoang Thi, Thanh Van Ho, Adegbesan Abiodun Christopher, Hung Tran Xuan, Thang Viet Luong
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV)-associated hepatocellular carcinoma develops in a chronically inflamed, tolerogenic liver microenvironment. In this setting, T-cell dysfunction limits the efficacy of programmed death-1 (PD-1) blockade. Emerging evidence from Liu et al published in the World Journal of Gastrointestinal Oncology suggests that CD161 may represent an additional inhibitory axis within exhausted CD8+ T cells that is not fully captured by the PD-1/ programmed death ligand-1 pathway. In their study, CD161+CD8+ T cells were enriched in tumor tissue, and CD161 expression persisted even as PD-1+ compartments contracted after PD-1 inhibition, suggesting that CD161-mediated restraint may remain active under therapeutic pressure. Mechanistically, sustained engagement of the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 interaction may impair T-cell effector function and contribute to adaptive immune resistance in the HBV-infected liver. These findings highlight the importance of protein-level assessment of CD161 and parallel evaluation with PD-1 to accurately characterize immune inhibition. Key knowledge gaps include the cellular source of lectin-like transcript 1 within the tumor microenvironment, the influence of ongoing HBV virological activity on CD161+ T-cell subsets, and the identification of clinically meaningful progression endpoints. In this editorial, we place these findings in mechanistic and translational context, highlighting the potential role of the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis as a complementary pathway of immune resistance beyond PD-1 blockade. Collectively, the current evidence supports a cautious, hypothesis-driven translational strategy to evaluate combined modulation of the CD161 and PD-1 axes in carefully selected patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the role of CD161 in T-cell dysfunction in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma, addressing the limitations of current immunotherapies such as PD-1 blockade.
Design and population
The study was conducted to investigate the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis in tumor tissue from patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Core methods
The study involved analyzing tumor tissue samples from patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma, focusing on CD161 expression and its interaction with PD-1.
Main results
CD161+CD8+ T cells were enriched in tumor tissue, and CD161 expression persisted even after PD-1 inhibition, suggesting ongoing immune resistance.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CD161-mediated restraint may remain active under therapeutic pressure, highlighting the importance of evaluating CD161 alongside PD-1 in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform the development of combined modulation strategies targeting both CD161 and PD-1 axes in patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
Key limitations include the need for further investigation into the cellular source of lectin-like transcript 1 and the influence of ongoing HBV virological activity on CD161+ T-cell subsets.

通过机器学习实现针对慢性乙型肝炎相关肝纤维化的个性化预测

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning
作者： Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
摘要中文翻译尽管长期抗病毒药物治疗可以逆转慢性乙型肝炎患者的肝纤维化,但个体对治疗的反应差异显著,目前仍缺乏可靠的预测方法。传统的临床工具如肝硬度测量和天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶与血小板比值指数最初设计用于横断面分期,而非纵向预测,其捕捉纤维化逆转动态过程的能力有限。在本观点综述中,我们讨论了机器学习(ML)如何帮助解决这一未满足的需求。我们重点关注了机器学习在三个方面的早期但有希望的结果:利用机器学习进行图像的定量分析、通过多模态模型整合病理和临床数据,以及基于抗病毒药物治疗期间的连续测量动态预测治疗反应。我们认为,机器学习的最大价值并不在于取代现有评估工具,而在于补充它们,特别是在早期识别那些仅通过抗病毒药物治疗难以实现纤维化逆转的患者方面。然而,我们也提醒,目前大多数模型都是基于小规模、单中心的回顾性队列研究开发的,其临床应用需要前瞻性多中心验证、提高模型的可解释性以及遵循标准化的报告框架。

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning

Authors: Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Although long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in a considerable proportion of patients with chronic hepatitis B, treatment response differs markedly among individuals, and reliable methods for predicting who will benefit remain lacking. Conventional clinical tools such as liver stiffness measurement and the aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index were originally dewsigned for cross-sectional staging rather than longitudinal prediction, and their ability to capture the dynamic process of fibrosis reversal is limited. In this opinion review, we discuss how machine learning (ML) may help address this unmet need. We focus on three areas where ML has shown early but encouraging results: Quantitative analysis of images using ML, integration of pathological and clinical data through multimodal models, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements during antiviral therapy. In our view, the greatest value of ML lies not in replacing existing assessment tools but in complementing them, particularly through the early identification of patients who are unlikely to achieve fibrosis reversal with antiviral therapy alone. However, we also caution that most current models have been developed from small, single-center retrospective cohorts, and their clinical adoption will require prospective multi-center validation, improved model interpretability, and adherence to standardized reporting frameworks.

科特迪瓦献血者医疗随访中心 (CMSDS) 对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性献血者中的 δ 型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 进行 患病率 跟踪

Prevalence of hepatitis delta virus (HDV) among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) in Cte dIvoire
作者： Renaud Dechi Jacques, Jeanne Armande Ake Aya, Kabore Saydou, Philippe N'Din Jean-Louis, Moussa Salimata, Oura Doris, Dembele Bamory, Siransy Kouabla Liliane, Mamadou Sekongo Yassongui, Saydou Kaboré
摘要中文翻译慢性丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染会加剧 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝病。在科特迪瓦,未对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性献血者进行系统性筛查,大多数区域估计依赖于抗 HDV 血清学,这表明暴露但不活跃复制。本研究旨在确定 HBsAg 阳性献血者中 HDV RNA 复制阳性情况。科特迪瓦国家输血中心 (CNTSCI) 的献血者医疗随访中心 (CMSDS) 使用巢式逆转录聚合酶链式反应 (RT-PCR)。2021 年 3 月进行了一项基于非概率便利样本的横断面研究。通过针对两个基因组区域(核苷酸 888-1122 和 313-485)的巢式 RT-PCR 检测了 HDV RNA。51 名捐献者中其中,75% 为男性 (n = 38),平均年龄为 37 ± 9 岁。43 名捐献者 (84%) 可获得 HBV DNA 结果。其中,可检测到 HBV 病毒血症的 HDV RNA 阳性率为 88.9% (32/36),病毒载量无法检测到的患者为 100% (7/7),总体阳性率为 90.7% (39/43)。该数字仅适用于具有可用病毒载量数据的捐献者,而不是 51 名捐献者的全部 队列研究。在接受富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 且 HBV 病毒血症受到抑制的捐献者中也检测到 HDV RNA。考虑到已经处于后续护理中的捐献者的方便样本,研究结果是假设生成而不是人群水平估计。这些结果支持更大规模的代表性研究和个体随访期间的系统性 HDV 筛查关键词:乙型肝炎病毒/丁型肝炎病毒(HBV/HDV)合并感染,巢式逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR),分子流行病学,方便样本,输血医学,西非。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染会加剧 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关的肝病。在科特迪瓦,丁型肝炎病毒并未在乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性的献血者中进行系统筛查,大多数地区的估计依赖于抗丁型肝炎病毒 血清学,这表明存在暴露,但不代表活跃复制。
设计与对象
2021 年 3 月,科特迪瓦国家输血中心 (CNTSCI) 的献血者医疗随访中心 (CMSDS) 进行了一项基于非概率便利样本的横断面研究。在 51 名捐赠者中,75% 是男性 (n = 38),平均年龄为 37 ± 9 岁。
核心方法
HDV RNA 通过针对两个基因组区域(核苷酸 888-1122 和 313-485)的巢式逆转录聚合酶链式反应 (RT-PCR) 进行检测。43 名捐献者 (84%) 可获得 HBV DNA 结果。
主要结果
在 43 名可获得 HBV DNA 结果的捐献者中,可检测到 HBV 病毒血症的 HDV RNA 阳性率为 88.9% (32/36),而病毒载量无法检测到的捐献者则为 100% (7/7),总体阳性率为 90.7% (39/43)。该数字仅适用于具有可用病毒载量数据的捐赠者,而不是 51 名捐赠者的整个 队列研究。在接受富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 且 HBV 病毒血症受到抑制的捐赠者中也检测到了 HDV RNA。
解释与创新
这些结果支持在慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者的随访过程中进行更大规模的代表性研究和系统性丁型肝炎病毒筛查。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了丁型肝炎病毒筛查对慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者的重要性,特别是在可获得病毒载量数据的情况下,并表明需要进一步研究以更好地了解丁型肝炎病毒 患病率 及其对公共卫生的影响。
局限与证据强度
鉴于已经接受后续护理的捐赠者的方便样本,研究结果是假设生成而不是人口水平的估计。该证据是中等的,因为它基于一项具有有限普遍性的研究。

Prevalence of hepatitis delta virus (HDV) among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) in Cte dIvoire

Authors: Renaud Dechi Jacques, Jeanne Armande Ake Aya, Kabore Saydou, Philippe N'Din Jean-Louis, Moussa Salimata, Oura Doris, Dembele Bamory, Siransy Kouabla Liliane, Mamadou Sekongo Yassongui, Saydou Kaboré
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis delta virus (HDV) infection exacerbates hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related liver disease.In Côte d'Ivoire, HDV is systematic not systematically screened among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)positive blood donors, and most regional estimates rely on anti-HDV serology, which indicates exposure but not active replication.This study aimed to determine HDV RNA replication positivity among HBsAgpositive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) of the National Blood Transfusion Centre of Côte d'Ivoire (CNTSCI), using nested reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR).A cross-sectional study based on a non-probability convenience sample was conducted in March 2021.HDV RNA was detected by nested RT-PCR targeting two genomic regions (nucleotides 888-1122 and 313-485).Of 51 donors included, 75% were male (n = 38) and and mean age was 37 ± 9 years.HBV DNA results were available for 43 donors (84%).Among them, HDV RNA positivity was 88.9% (32/36) in those with detectable HBV viraemia and 100% (7/7) in those with undetectable viral load, giving an overall positivity 90.7% (39/43).This figure applies only to donors with available viral load data not the entire cohort of 51 donors.HDV RNA was also detected in donors receiving tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) with suppressed HBV viraemia.Given the convenience sample of donors already in follow-up care, findings are hypothesis-generating rather than population-level estimates.These results support larger representative studies and systematic HDV screening during follow-up of individuals with chronic HBV infection.Key words: Hepatitis B virus/hepatitis delta virus (HBV/HDV) co-infection, nested reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR), molecular epidemiology, convenience sample, transfusion medicine, West Africa.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis delta virus (HDV) infection exacerbates hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related liver disease. In Côte d'Ivoire, HDV is not systematically screened among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive blood donors, and most regional estimates rely on anti-HDV serology, which indicates exposure but not active replication.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study based on a non-probability convenience sample was conducted in March 2021 at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) of the National Blood Transfusion Centre of Côte d'Ivoire (CNTSCI). Of 51 donors included, 75% were male (n = 38) and the mean age was 37 ± 9 years.
Core methods
HDV RNA was detected by nested reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) targeting two genomic regions (nucleotides 888-1122 and 313-485). HBV DNA results were available for 43 donors (84%).
Main results
Among the 43 donors with available HBV DNA results, HDV RNA positivity was 88.9% (32/36) in those with detectable HBV viraemia and 100% (7/7) in those with undetectable viral load, giving an overall positivity of 90.7% (39/43). This figure applies only to donors with available viral load data, not the entire cohort of 51 donors. HDV RNA was also detected in donors receiving tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) with suppressed HBV viraemia.
Interpretation and novelty
These results support larger representative studies and systematic HDV screening during follow-up of individuals with chronic HBV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of HDV screening in individuals with chronic HBV infection, particularly in settings where viral load data is available, and suggest the need for further research to better understand HDV prevalence and its implications for public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
Given the convenience sample of donors already in follow-up care, findings are hypothesis-generating rather than population-level estimates. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single study with limited generalizability.

聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 添加到核苷(酸)类似物可减少肝细胞癌和晚期纤维化的失代偿 慢性乙型肝炎

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B
作者： Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
摘要中文翻译背景目前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。然而,其在改善 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者预后方面的价值以及该联合策略的合适候选者仍有待进一步探索。目的 评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。方法 这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了诊断为 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化的患者。参与者被分为两组:(1)接受联合治疗(Peg-IFNα-2b 附加到正在进行的 NA 治疗);(2) 接受 NA 单一疗法的患者。采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。结果 该分析包括 302 对配对,中位随访时间为 220 周。与 NA 单一疗法相比,联合治疗与发生 HCC(风险比 = 0.22;95% CI:0.11-0.48)和失代偿性肝硬化(风险比 = 0.39;95% CI:0.20-0.77)的风险显着降低相关。这些益处在经验证的 HCC 预测模型分类为中高风险的患者以及持续生化异常持续 > 12 个月的患者中尤其明显。结论 Peg-IFNα-2b 与 NA 的附加治疗与患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者的 HCC 减少和失代偿显着相关,支持其在该人群中的使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。当前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。

采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Peg-IFNα-2b 联合治疗可能为减少高危 HBV 患者的 HCC 和失代偿带来重要益处。这可以为未来 治疗 治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 晚期纤维化和肝硬化的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性队列研究研究,可能会引入偏倚和混杂因素。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于倾向评分匹配和分层生存分析。

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure. However, its value in improving prognosis and the suitable candidates for this combination strategy in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis remain to be further explored. AIM To evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. METHODS This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy. A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores. RESULTS The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy. These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. CONCLUSION Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy.
Core methods
A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores.
Main results
The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy.
Interpretation and novelty
These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that combination therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b may offer important benefits for reducing HCC and decompensation in high-risk HBV patients. This could inform future treatment strategies for managing advanced fibrosis and cirrhosis in chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective cohort study, which may introduce bias and confounding factors. The evidence is moderate as it relies on propensity score matching and stratified survival analysis.

IL-10 (rs1800896) 基因多态性和血清 IL-10 水平与 慢性乙型肝炎 感染的关联

Association of IL-10 (rs1800896) Gene Polymorphism and Serum IL-10 Levels with Chronic Hepatitis B Infection
作者： Hussain Naeim Mohammed, Alaa Ali Matrood Matrood
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染是主要的普遍有效性威胁。该病毒在多种因素的影响下进化,包括免疫遗传因素。本研究评估了白细胞介素10基因(IL10)多态性与这些感染发生之间的相关性。本研究是一项回顾性病例对照研究,纳入了64例慢性HBV感染病例、39例HBV清除组和31例对照组。"四引物扩增阻断PCR"(Tetra-primer ARMS-PCR)是一种用于研究IL10启动子区单核苷酸多态性(SNPs)(-1082A>G;rs1800896)的技术。通过酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)检测了慢性HBV感染患者、HBV清除组和对照组的IL-10水平。基于等位基因多态性分析,确定了三种基因型:AA(46.8 %)、AG(43.7 %)和GG(9.3 %)。HBV患者的IL10水平显著高于其他组。此外,这些发现表明,多态性可能不影响初始感染,而更可能影响疾病进展或疾病免疫应答。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估IL-10基因多态性与慢性乙型肝炎感染发展之间的相关性,解决了解影响HBV感染进展的免疫遗传因素的科学问题。
设计与对象
进行了一项回顾性病例对照研究,涉及 64 名慢性 HBV 感染者、39 名 HBV 清除组和 31 名对照。
核心方法
该研究采用“Tetra-primer ARMS-PCR”分析IL10启动子(-1082A>G;rs1800896)处的单核苷酸多态性(SNP)和酶联免疫吸附试验(ELISA)来测定慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染患者、乙型肝炎清除率和对照组的IL-10水平。
主要结果
Based on allele polymorphism analysis, three genotypes were identified: AA (46.8 %), AG (43.7 %) and GG (9.3 %). The level of IL10 in HBV patients was significantly greater than that in other sets.
解释与创新
这些发现表明,多态性可能不会影响初始感染,而是影响疾病进展或疾病 免疫应答。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来关于 IL-10 多态性在疾病进展和 免疫应答 中的作用的研究提供信息,可能有助于改进 免疫应答 和靶向治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制,但证据得到了对特定群体中 IL-10 多态性和血清水平的直接分析的支持,提供了中等程度的置信度。

Association of IL-10 (rs1800896) Gene Polymorphism and Serum IL-10 Levels with Chronic Hepatitis B Infection

Authors: Hussain Naeim Mohammed, Alaa Ali Matrood Matrood
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a main general validity threat. It evolves under the influence of various factors, including immunogenetic ones. This study assessed the correlation between the occurrence of interleukin 10 gene (IL10) polymorphisms and the development of these infections. This retrospective case control study 64 chronic HBV infections, 39 HBV clearance groups and 31 controls. "Tetra-primer ARMS-PCR" is a technique that used to study the single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) at IL10 promoter (-1082A>G; rs1800896). The IL-10 levels were determined in patients with chronic HBV infections, HBV clearance and control group by using an Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA). Based on allele polymorphism analysis, three genotypes were identified: AA (46.8 %), AG (43.7 %) and GG (9.3 %). The level of IL10 in HBV patients was significantly greater than that in other sets. In addition, these findings indicate that polymorphism may not affect initial infection but rather disease progression or disease immune response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the correlation between IL-10 gene polymorphisms and the development of chronic hepatitis B infection, addressing the scientific problem of understanding immunogenetic factors influencing HBV infection progression.
Design and population
A retrospective case control study was conducted involving 64 chronic HBV infections, 39 HBV clearance groups and 31 controls.
Core methods
The study used "Tetra-primer ARMS-PCR" to analyze single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) at IL10 promoter (-1082A>G; rs1800896) and Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA) to determine IL-10 levels in patients with chronic HBV infections, HBV clearance and control group.
Main results
Based on allele polymorphism analysis, three genotypes were identified: AA (46.8 %), AG (43.7 %) and GG (9.3 %). The level of IL10 in HBV patients was significantly greater than that in other sets.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings indicate that polymorphism may not affect initial infection but rather disease progression or disease immune response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future studies on the role of IL-10 polymorphisms in disease progression and immune response, potentially contributing to improved surveillance and targeted therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is supported by direct analysis of IL-10 polymorphisms and serum levels in defined groups, providing a moderate level of confidence.
