转化多重拉曼病理成像对 HBV 相关肝细胞癌的精确 诊断

Translational Multiplexed Raman Pathological Imaging for Precise Diagnosis of HBV-Associated Hepatocellular Carcinoma
作者： Wei Zhu, Wen-Jing Xu, Xin-Ping Zhan, Jia-Cheng Chen, Yao Wang, Kai Dai, Ji-Ming Hu, Ai-Guo Shen, Jürgen Popp
摘要中文翻译摘要:多重振动成像在组织分子分析方面具有巨大潜力,但适合常规临床组织病理学的完全标准化工作流程仍然很少。在此,我们报告了一种使用单分散三键拉曼聚合物纳米颗粒(∼50 nm)的临床适用方法。该方法集成了一步标记和单次扫描采集,可实现肝细胞癌 (HCC) 组织中 5 种蛋白质标记物的高性能多重拉曼成像。针对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关 HCC,我们的生物标志物组合将病毒蛋白 HBx 与 4 个临床使用的指标相结合:AFP、GS、GPC3 和 CD34。我们进一步揭示了临床上关键的分离:几个高级别 HBV-HCC 标本表现出显着升高的致癌标志物,而 HBx 表达相对较低,这与 抗病毒药物 疗法抑制病毒蛋白产生而不逆转肿瘤侵袭性一致。利用生物沉默区域(1800−2800 cm−1)内三键标签产生的超窄、高分辨率信号,我们实现了高保真空间映射和定量分析。与依赖形态学观察、临床表现或单标志物检测的传统病理方法不同,该技术同时评估病毒病原学、肿瘤进展和转移潜力,有效降低病毒和致癌谱不一致患者的错误分类风险,并在约24小时内完成整个检测。这项研究建立了一个快速、可靠的组织病理学平台,整合了病原体识别和全面的癌症表征,展示了对癌症研究和临床诊断的巨大价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过开发一种临床适用的方法来精确检测 HBV 相关肝细胞癌的 诊断,从而解决常规临床组织病理学缺乏完全标准化工作流程的问题。
设计与对象
该研究报告了一种临床适用的方法,使用单分散三键拉曼聚合物纳米颗粒(∼50 nm)实现肝细胞癌(HCC)组织中 5 种蛋白质标记物的高性能多重拉曼成像。
核心方法
该技术集成了一步标记和单次扫描采集,可同时评估病毒病因、肿瘤进展和转移潜力,有效降低病毒和致癌谱不一致患者的错误分类风险,并在约 24 小时内完成整个检测。
主要结果
该方法针对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关 HCC,将病毒蛋白 HBx 与 4 个临床使用的指标相结合:AFP、GS、GPC3 和 CD34。它进一步揭示了临床上关键的分离:几个高级别 HBV-HCC 标本表现出显着升高的致癌标志物,而 HBx 表达相对较低。
解释与创新
作者解释说,这种方法通过利用生物沉默区域(1800−2800 cm−1)内三键标签产生的超窄、高分辨率信号来实现高保真空间绘图和定量分析,这是病原体识别和全面癌症表征的新颖整合。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现证明了对癌症研究和临床诊断的巨大价值,特别是在提高 诊断 对 HBV 相关肝细胞癌的准确性方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,并且该方法在常规临床环境中的有效性尚未得到充分验证,因此可信度评估为中等。

Translational Multiplexed Raman Pathological Imaging for Precise Diagnosis of HBV-Associated Hepatocellular Carcinoma

Authors: Wei Zhu, Wen-Jing Xu, Xin-Ping Zhan, Jia-Cheng Chen, Yao Wang, Kai Dai, Ji-Ming Hu, Ai-Guo Shen, Jürgen Popp
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Multiplexed vibrational imaging holds great potential for tissue molecular analysis, yet fully standardized workflows suitable for routine clinical histopathology remain scarce. Herein, we report a clinically applicable methodology using monodisperse triple-bond Raman polymer nanoparticles (∼50 nm). This approach integrates one-step labeling and single-scan acquisition to achieve high-performance multiplexed Raman imaging of 5 protein markers in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) tissues. Targeting hepatitis B virus (HBV)-associated HCC, our biomarker panel combines the viral protein HBx with 4 clinically used indicators: AFP, GS, GPC3, and CD34. We further reveal a clinically critical dissociation: several high-grade HBV-HCC specimens exhibit markedly elevated oncogenic markers with comparatively low HBx expression, consistent with antiviral therapy suppressing viral protein production without reversing tumor aggressiveness. Taking advantage of the ultranarrow, well-resolved signals generated by triple-bond tags within the biological silent region (1800−2800 cm−1), we achieve high-fidelity spatial mapping and quantitative analysis. Unlike conventional pathological methods relying on morphological observation, clinical manifestations, or single-marker detection, this technique simultaneously evaluates viral etiology, tumor progression, and metastatic potential, effectively reducing misclassification risk in patients with discordant viral and oncogenic profiles, and completes the entire assay within approximately 24 h. This study establishes a rapid and reliable histopathological platform that integrates pathogen identification and comprehensive cancer characterization, demonstrating substantial value for both cancer research and clinical diagnostics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the lack of fully standardized workflows for routine clinical histopathology by developing a clinically applicable methodology for precise diagnosis of HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Design and population
The study reported a clinically applicable methodology using monodisperse triple-bond Raman polymer nanoparticles (∼50 nm) to achieve high-performance multiplexed Raman imaging of 5 protein markers in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) tissues.
Core methods
The technique integrates one-step labeling and single-scan acquisition to simultaneously evaluate viral etiology, tumor progression, and metastatic potential, effectively reducing misclassification risk in patients with discordant viral and oncogenic profiles, and completes the entire assay within approximately 24 h.
Main results
The method targets hepatitis B virus (HBV)-associated HCC, combining the viral protein HBx with 4 clinically used indicators: AFP, GS, GPC3, and CD34. It further reveals a clinically critical dissociation: several high-grade HBV-HCC specimens exhibit markedly elevated oncogenic markers with comparatively low HBx expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that this approach enables high-fidelity spatial mapping and quantitative analysis by leveraging ultranarrow, well-resolved signals generated by triple-bond tags within the biological silent region (1800−2800 cm−1), which is a novel integration of pathogen identification and comprehensive cancer characterization.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings demonstrate substantial value for both cancer research and clinical diagnostics, particularly in improving the accuracy of diagnosis for HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the methodology's effectiveness in routine clinical settings has not been fully validated, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

代谢组学揭示不同药物干预下与慢性乙型肝炎感染临床治愈相关的代谢特征

Metabolomics reveals metabolite profiles associated with clinical cure of chronic HBV infection under different drug interventions
作者： Peng Peng, Huiping Xu, Xianghong Kong, Junling Wang, Wei Zhang, 孔祥红
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定接受不同恩替卡韦和聚乙二醇干扰素-α 治疗方案治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者与完全治愈相关的差异代谢物。
设计与对象
中国山东省菏泽市牡丹市人民医院开展了一项回顾性病例对照研究,纳入了2021年6月2日至2023年12月30日期间入组的乙型肝炎病毒患者样本。根据治疗方案和完全治愈的证据对患者进行分组。
核心方法
使用超高效液相色谱-质谱和代谢组学数据库来识别表明临床治愈的差异代谢物和网络。使用R语言Ropls包分析数据。
主要结果
Of the 50 patients, 13(26%) each were in Group A and Group B, while 12(24%) each were in Group E and Group W. A total of 52 differential metabolites showing altered abundance in Group A compared with the other groups were identified. The fold-change values were 1.28 for 9,10-DHOME, 1.12 for 12,13-DHOME and 1.33 for kaempferide, and guanidoacetic acid ranked first among the differential metabolites in Group A versus Group B (fold-change: 1.27, p=2.56×10-8).
解释与创新
山奈、9,10-二羟基-12Z-十八碳烯酸和12,13-二羟基-9Z-十八碳烯酸是常见的差异代谢物,这表明对慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的临床治愈具有潜在意义。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为监测 治疗 反应和预测 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的临床治愈的生物标志物的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性病例对照研究,样本量相对较小,这可能会限制结果的普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它是基于代谢组学分析和统计比较。

Metabolomics reveals metabolite profiles associated with clinical cure of chronic HBV infection under different drug interventions

Authors: Peng Peng, Huiping Xu, Xianghong Kong, Junling Wang, Wei Zhang, 孔祥红
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: To identify differential metabolites associated with complete cure in chronic hepatitis B patients treated with various regimens of entecavir and pegylated interferon-alpha. Methods: The retrospective, case-control study was conducted at the Mudan People's Hospital of Heze, Shandong, China, and comprised samples from the institutional laboratory department of hepatitis B virus patients enrolled between June 2, 2021, and December 30, 2023. The patients were grouped based on the treatment protocol and the evidence of complete cure. Groups A and B comprised patients who were and were not clinically cured after receiving 48 weeks of sequential entecavir and pegylated interferon-alpha. Group E patients received entecavir monotherapy for over 2 years without a clinical cure, and group W included untreated patients. Ultra-performance liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry and metabolomics databases were utilised to identify differential metabolites and networks indicative of clinical cure. Data was analysed using the R language Ropls package. Results: Of the 50 patients, 13(26%) each were in Group A and Group B, while 12(24%) each were in Group E and Group W. The median age was similar across groups (Group A: 42 [40-47] years; Group B: 43 [40-49] years; Group E: 43.5 [39-48] years; Group W: 43 [40-48] years), and male patients predominated in each group (Group A: 10(77%); Group B: 10(77%); Group E: 9(75%); Group W: 9(75%)). A total of 52 differential metabolites showing altered abundance in Group A compared with the other groups were identified. Notably, 9,10-dihydroxy-12Z-octadecenoic acid, 12,13-dihydroxy-9Z-octadecenoic acid and kaempferide were common differential metabolites across all intergroup comparisons. Beyond these common metabolites, the top-ranked differential metabolites unique to each comparison included guanidoacetic acid, caffeine and uracil 5-carboxylate in Group A versus Group B; 3-ketosphingosine in Group A versus Group E; and L-2-hydroxyglutaric acid and 3,4-dihydroxybenzeneacetic acid in Group A versus Group W. The fold-change values were 1.28 for 9,10-DHOME, 1.12 for 12,13-DHOME and 1.33 for kaempferide, and guanidoacetic acid ranked first among the differential metabolites in Group A versus Group B (fold-change: 1.27, p=2.56×10-8). These metabolites were enriched in 42 metabolic pathways, including linoleic acid, caffeine and amino acid metabolism. Conclusions: Kaempferide, 9,10-dihydroxy-12Z-octadecenoic acid and 12,13-dihydroxy-9Z-octadecenoic acid were common differential metabolites, indicating potential significance for achieving clinical cure in chronic hepatitis B virus infection. Guanidoacetic acid, caffeine, uracil 5-carboxylate, 3-ketosphingosine, L-2-hydroxyglutaric acid, and 3,4-dihydroxybenzeneacetic acid were the top three differential metabolites in each group beyond the common ones.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify differential metabolites associated with complete cure in chronic hepatitis B patients treated with various regimens of entecavir and pegylated interferon-alpha.
Design and population
A retrospective, case-control study was conducted at the Mudan People's Hospital of Heze, Shandong, China, involving samples from hepatitis B virus patients enrolled between June 2, 2021, and December 30, 2023. Patients were grouped based on the treatment protocol and the evidence of complete cure.
Core methods
Ultra-performance liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry and metabolomics databases were used to identify differential metabolites and networks indicative of clinical cure. Data was analysed using the R language Ropls package.
Main results
Of the 50 patients, 13(26%) each were in Group A and Group B, while 12(24%) each were in Group E and Group W. A total of 52 differential metabolites showing altered abundance in Group A compared with the other groups were identified. The fold-change values were 1.28 for 9,10-DHOME, 1.12 for 12,13-DHOME and 1.33 for kaempferide, and guanidoacetic acid ranked first among the differential metabolites in Group A versus Group B (fold-change: 1.27, p=2.56×10-8).
Interpretation and novelty
Kaempferide, 9,10-dihydroxy-12Z-octadecenoic acid and 12,13-dihydroxy-9Z-octadecenoic acid were common differential metabolites, indicating potential significance for achieving clinical cure in chronic hepatitis B virus infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of biomarkers for monitoring treatment response and predicting clinical cure in chronic hepatitis B virus infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective case-control study with a relatively small sample size, which may limit the generalisability of the results. The evidence is moderate as it is based on metabolomics analysis and statistical comparisons.

针对失代偿性乙型肝炎肝硬化新诊断院内细菌和真菌感染的试点模型的开发和内部验证:单中心回顾性 队列研究 研究

Development and Internal Validation of a Pilot Model for Newly Diagnosed in-Hospital Bacterial and Fungal Infections in Decompensated Hepatitis B Cirrhosis: A Single-Center Retrospective Cohort Study
作者： Yangyang Jin, Ying Huang, Runqiu Jiang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管常规测量可能反映已经发生的感染,但认识到住院期间的感染风险对于失代偿性肝硬化很重要。
设计与对象
这项单中心回顾性 队列研究 纳入了 2023 年至 2025 年入院的 150 名患者。完整病例分析包括 148 名患者和 20 起事件。
核心方法
入院中性粒细胞与淋巴细胞比率 (NLR) 和终末期肝病模型评分 (MELD) 被固定为主要 Firth/Jeffreys 惩罚逻辑模型的预测因子。我们在 1000 个 bootstrap 样本中重新拟合了固定模型,评估了校准和歧视,并检查了早期事件排除和仅限准入的岭模型。医院记录的手术变量被排除在预测之外。
主要结果
Among 148 modeled patients, the NLR and MELD odds ratios were 1.073 (95% confidence interval [CI], 1.025–1.124) and 1.062 (95% CI, 1.002–1.125). Apparent area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) was 0.716 versus 0.633 for MELD alone. AUCs after separate exclusions of infections recorded by hospital day 2 or day 3 were 0.750 and 0.731, respectively. Fine–Gray subdistribution hazard ratios were 1.069 (95% CI 1.042–1.098) for NLR and 1.064 (1.021–1.109) for MELD.
解释与创新
这种录取模型显示出适度的歧视和不精确的校准。
科研与公卫意义
在模型指导 监测 或 治疗 之前,需要在定义的预测时间和范围内进行前瞻性外部验证。
局限与证据强度
回顾性确定和有限验证排除了临床使用。

Development and Internal Validation of a Pilot Model for Newly Diagnosed in-Hospital Bacterial and Fungal Infections in Decompensated Hepatitis B Cirrhosis: A Single-Center Retrospective Cohort Study

Authors: Yangyang Jin, Ying Huang, Runqiu Jiang
Original abstract (English)
Background and Aims: Recognizing infection risk during hospitalization is important in decompensated cirrhosis, although routine measurements may reflect infection already developing. We developed and internally validated an admission-time pilot model for newly diagnosed in-hospital bacterial or fungal infection in decompensated hepatitis B virus-related cirrhosis. Methods: This single-center retrospective cohort included 150 patients admitted in 2023– 2025. Admission neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR) and Model for End-Stage Liver Disease score (MELD) were fixed as predictors for the primary Firth/Jeffreys penalized logistic model. Complete-case analysis included 148 patients and 20 events. We refitted the fixed model in 1000 bootstrap samples, assessed calibration and discrimination, and examined early-event exclusions and an admission-only ridge model. The hospital-recorded procedure variable was excluded from prediction. Secondary analyses used Aalen–Johansen cumulative incidence, Fine–Gray regression and cause-specific Cox, with infection-free discharge and death as competing events. Results: Among 148 modeled patients, the NLR and MELD odds ratios were 1.073 (95% confidence interval [CI], 1.025– 1.124) and 1.062 (95% CI, 1.002– 1.125). Apparent area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) was 0.716 versus 0.633 for MELD alone. Optimism-corrected AUC was 0.700 (approximate 95% CI 0.566– 0.846), corrected Brier score was 0.103, and corrected calibration slope was 0.981 (approximate bootstrap interval 0.506– 1.646). AUCs after separate exclusions of infections recorded by hospital day 2 or day 3 were 0.750 and 0.731, respectively. Fine–Gray subdistribution hazard ratios were 1.069 (95% CI 1.042– 1.098) for NLR and 1.064 (1.021– 1.109) for MELD. Conclusion: This admission model showed modest discrimination with imprecise calibration. Retrospective ascertainment and limited validation preclude clinical use. Prospective external validation at a defined prediction time and horizon is needed before the model guides surveillance or treatment. Keywords: hepatitis B, decompensated cirrhosis, infection, prediction model, internal validation, nomogram
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Recognizing infection risk during hospitalization is important in decompensated cirrhosis, although routine measurements may reflect infection already developing.
Design and population
This single-center retrospective cohort included 150 patients admitted in 2023–2025. Complete-case analysis included 148 patients and 20 events.
Core methods
Admission neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR) and Model for End-Stage Liver Disease score (MELD) were fixed as predictors for the primary Firth/Jeffreys penalized logistic model. We refitted the fixed model in 1000 bootstrap samples, assessed calibration and discrimination, and examined early-event exclusions and an admission-only ridge model. The hospital-recorded procedure variable was excluded from prediction.
Main results
Among 148 modeled patients, the NLR and MELD odds ratios were 1.073 (95% confidence interval [CI], 1.025–1.124) and 1.062 (95% CI, 1.002–1.125). Apparent area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) was 0.716 versus 0.633 for MELD alone. AUCs after separate exclusions of infections recorded by hospital day 2 or day 3 were 0.750 and 0.731, respectively. Fine–Gray subdistribution hazard ratios were 1.069 (95% CI 1.042–1.098) for NLR and 1.064 (1.021–1.109) for MELD.
Interpretation and novelty
This admission model showed modest discrimination with imprecise calibration.
Scientific and public-health significance
Prospective external validation at a defined prediction time and horizon is needed before the model guides surveillance or treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
Retrospective ascertainment and limited validation preclude clinical use.

尼日利亚埃博尼州结核病患者乙型和丙型肝炎病毒感染的 流行病学

Epidemiology of Hepatitis B and C Viral Infections Among Tuberculosis Patients in Ebonyi State, Nigeria
作者： Ogudu Emmanuel Ogbonnia, Ikpeamarom Anurika Peace, Okoye Ebele Linda, Ikpeamarom Nnamdi Alex, UCHENNA EBENEZER, Ibemere Chidinma, James-Onyekwere Joan Miracle Ifeoma
摘要中文翻译合并感染 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 或丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的结核病患者在 治疗 期间可能特别容易受到肝损伤。关于埃博尼州这些感染的负担和临床影响的证据仍然有限。该研究的目的是评估尼日利亚埃博尼州结核病患者中乙型和丙型肝炎病毒感染的 流行病学。具体目标是 (i) 确定埃博尼州结核病患者中 HBV、HCV 和 HBV/HCV 合并感染的 患病率,(ii) 确定埃博尼州乙型和丙型病毒性肝炎合并感染结核病患者的常见危险因素。(iii) 确定结核病患者对 HBV 和 HCV 感染的知识、态度和做法。本研究评估了 HBV/HCV 患病率、危险因素以及患者的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决尼日利亚埃博尼州结核病患者乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎感染的负担和临床影响的有限证据。由于同时感染 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 或丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的结核病患者在 治疗 期间可能容易遭受肝损伤,因此需要进行这项研究。
设计与对象
该研究在尼日利亚埃博尼州的结核病患者中进行,旨在评估乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎病毒感染的 流行病学。它包括确定 HBV、HCV 和合并感染的 患病率 的具体目标,以及确定危险因素并评估结核病患者对这些感染的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
核心方法
该研究评估了 HBV/HCV 患病率、危险因素以及患者的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。它重点关注尼日利亚埃博尼州的结核病患者,并包括评估这些感染的 流行病学 的一般方法。
主要结果
它直接观察了尼日利亚埃博尼州结核病患者乙型和丙型肝炎感染情况的患病率。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了对结核病和病毒性肝炎感染进行综合管理的必要性。该研究的新颖性在于其重点关注埃博尼州结核病患者中 HBV 和 HCV 的 流行病学。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为尼日利亚埃博尼州结核病和病毒性肝炎感染综合管理的公共卫生策略提供参考。他们强调了在 治疗 期间解决合并感染以预防肝损伤的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,可能无法捕捉详细的方法或结果。由于缺乏全面的数据且侧重于一般观察,证据强度中等。

Epidemiology of Hepatitis B and C Viral Infections Among Tuberculosis Patients in Ebonyi State, Nigeria

Authors: Ogudu Emmanuel Ogbonnia, Ikpeamarom Anurika Peace, Okoye Ebele Linda, Ikpeamarom Nnamdi Alex, UCHENNA EBENEZER, Ibemere Chidinma, James-Onyekwere Joan Miracle Ifeoma
Original abstract (English)
Tuberculosis patients coinfected with hepatitis B virus (HBV) or hepatitis C virus (HCV) may be particularly vulnerable to liver injury during treatment. Evidence on the burden and clinical implications of these infections in Ebonyi State remains limited. The aim of the study is to assess the epidemiology of Hepatitis B and C Viruses Infection among tuberculosis patients in Ebonyi State, Nigeria. The specific objectives were to (i) determine the prevalence of HBV, HCV, and HBV/HCV co-infection among tuberculosis patients in Ebonyi State, (ii) to identify common risk factors among co-infected TB-patients with viral hepatitis B and C in Ebonyi State. (iii) to determine the knowledge, attitude and practice of TB patients towards HBV and HCV infections. This study assessed HBV/HCV prevalence, risk factors, and patients’ knowledge, attitudes and practices (KAP).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limited evidence on the burden and clinical implications of hepatitis B and C infections among tuberculosis patients in Ebonyi State, Nigeria. The research was needed due to the potential vulnerability of tuberculosis patients coinfected with hepatitis B virus (HBV) or hepatitis C virus (HCV) to liver injury during treatment.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Ebonyi State, Nigeria, among tuberculosis patients to assess the epidemiology of hepatitis B and C viral infections. It included specific objectives to determine the prevalence of HBV, HCV, and co-infection, as well as to identify risk factors and evaluate knowledge, attitude, and practices (KAP) of TB patients towards these infections.
Core methods
The study assessed HBV/HCV prevalence, risk factors, and patients’ knowledge, attitudes and practices (KAP). It focused on tuberculosis patients in Ebonyi State, Nigeria, and included general methods to evaluate the epidemiology of these infections.
Main results
It directly observed the prevalence of hepatitis B and C infections among tuberculosis patients in Ebonyi State, Nigeria.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the need for integrated management of tuberculosis and viral hepatitis infections. The study's novelty lies in its focus on the epidemiology of HBV and HCV among TB patients in Ebonyi State.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for integrated management of tuberculosis and viral hepatitis infections in Ebonyi State, Nigeria. They emphasize the importance of addressing co-infections to prevent liver injury during treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its abstract-only evidence, which may not capture detailed methodologies or outcomes. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of comprehensive data and the focus on general observations.

根据国家 2 岁以下儿童 EPI 时间表,免疫应答 为乙型肝炎 疫苗接种

Immune Response to Hepatitis B Vaccination According to National EPI Schedule among Children Below 2 Years
作者： Kamrun Nahar, Aparup Kanti Das, Shanjana Islam, Asma Ferdousi, Mohammed Jabed Bin Amin Chowdhury, Syeda Humaida Hasan
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒感染是全球公共卫生负担。我们在EPI计划中纳入了Hep-B 疫苗。目前尚无关于我国HBV 疫苗在一年内免疫应答的全面数据。本研究旨在评估该年龄组HBV疫苗接种儿童的免疫应答情况。材料和方法:本横断面研究于2022年1月至12月在锡尔赫特医学系医院EPI户外进行。从375名接种疫苗的儿童中采集血液样本,采用ELISA技术检测其Anti-HBs抗体滴度,检测时间为9个月至15个月之间。统计分析使用Statistical Package for Social Sciences 23.0版本。结果:本研究显示,年龄较大的儿童和男性在疫苗接种后具有更好的免疫应答,而营养不良是导致抗体滴度低的显著风险因素。结果显示,79.2%的儿童Anti-HBs抗体滴度达到满意或高水平(≥10 mIU/mL)。然而,20.8%的儿童抗体滴度较低(<10 mIU/mL),表明其免疫保护不足。结论:目前EPI计划下大多数接种HBV疫苗的儿童能够建立对乙型肝炎的保护性免疫;一小部分由于免疫应答不足而仍处于易感状态。Chatt Maa Shi Hosp Med Coll J;Vol.25 (2);July 2026;Page 10-15
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 感染是全球公共卫生的负担。我国尚无乙型肝炎病毒疫苗一年内免疫应答的全面数据。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究于 2022 年 1 月至 12 月在吉大港医学院医院室外 EPI 进行。在 9 个月至 15 个月内,通过 ELISA 技术对 375 名接种疫苗的儿童的血液样本进行了抗-HBs 滴度检测。
核心方法
使用社会科学统计包 23.0 版进行统计分析。
主要结果
这项研究表明,年龄较大的儿童和男性在 疫苗接种 后往往有更好的 免疫应答,并且营养不良是低抗体滴度的重要危险因素。这项研究还表明,79.2%的儿童具有满意或高水平的抗-HBs抗体(≥10 mIU/mL)。然而,20.8%的儿童抗体滴度较低(<10 mIU/mL),表明免疫保护不足。
解释与创新
大多数按照现行 EPI 计划接种疫苗的儿童都会产生针对乙型肝炎的保护性免疫力;由于血清转化不足,一小部分人仍然容易受到感染。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的 疫苗接种 策略提供信息,并强调需要采取营养干预措施来改善儿童的 免疫应答。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计和潜在混杂因素的限制。证据是温和的,因为它是基于针对特定人群的单一研究。

Immune Response to Hepatitis B Vaccination According to National EPI Schedule among Children Below 2 Years

Authors: Kamrun Nahar, Aparup Kanti Das, Shanjana Islam, Asma Ferdousi, Mohammed Jabed Bin Amin Chowdhury, Syeda Humaida Hasan
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B virus infection is a public health burden globally. We incorporate Hep-B vaccine in our EPI schedule. There is no comprehensive data regarding the immune response of the HBV vaccine at one year in our country. The study was done to assess the immune response among HBV vaccinated children at this age group. Materials and methods: This cross-sectional study was conducted at EPI outdoor of Chittagong Medical College Hospital from January to December, 2022. Blood sample from 375 vaccinated children were tested for Anti-HBs titre by ELISA technique in between 9 months to 15 months. Statistical analyses were performed with Statistical Package for Social Sciences version 23.0. Results: This study shows that older children and male tend to have better immune responses after vaccination and malnutrition is a significant risk factor for low antibody titers. This study also shows that 79.2% of children had satisfactory or high levels of anti-HBs antibodies (≥10 mIU/mL). However, 20.8% of children had low antibody titers ( <10 mIU/mL) indicating inadequate immune protection. Conclusion: Most children vaccinated under the current EPI schedule develop protective immunity against hepatitis B; a small proportion remain vulnerable due to inadequate seroconversion. Chatt Maa Shi Hosp Med Coll J; Vol.25 (2); July 2026; Page 10-15
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus infection is a public health burden globally. There is no comprehensive data regarding the immune response of the HBV vaccine at one year in our country.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study was conducted at EPI outdoor of Chittagong Medical College Hospital from January to December, 2022. Blood sample from 375 vaccinated children were tested for Anti-HBs titre by ELISA technique in between 9 months to 15 months.
Core methods
Statistical analyses were performed with Statistical Package for Social Sciences version 23.0.
Main results
This study shows that older children and male tend to have better immune responses after vaccination and malnutrition is a significant risk factor for low antibody titers. This study also shows that 79.2% of children had satisfactory or high levels of anti-HBs antibodies (≥10 mIU/mL). However, 20.8% of children had low antibody titers ( <10 mIU/mL) indicating inadequate immune protection.
Interpretation and novelty
Most children vaccinated under the current EPI schedule develop protective immunity against hepatitis B; a small proportion remain vulnerable due to inadequate seroconversion.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted vaccination strategies and highlight the need for nutritional interventions to improve immune response in children.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and potential confounding factors. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single study with a specific population.

乙型肝炎病毒 基因型 C 菌株的内在复制优势独立于基础核心启动子 A1762T/G1764A 突变

Intrinsic replication advantage of a Hepatitis B Virus genotype C strain is independent of the basal core promoter A1762T/G1764A mutation
作者： Yihan Xiao, Xinjun Chen, Zongxin Li, Sheng Lei, Zhenbin Chen, Junqi Cai, Xiuji Cui
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在从同时感染基因型 B 和 C 的患者中分离和表征个体 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 菌株,并直接比较它们的内在复制能力。从 慢性乙型肝炎 患者身上分离出 HBV DNA。通过基因型特异性聚合酶链反应(PCR)确定基因型,并克隆全长基因组。选择代表亚基因型 B2 (c17) 和 C1 (c21) 的两个克隆,对其进行测序并转染至 HepG2 细胞中。通过Southern印迹和定量PCR评估病毒复制。通过定点诱变研究了常见的腺嘌呤 (A)1762 胸腺嘧啶 (T/) 鸟嘌呤 (G)1764A 突变 在 C1 克隆中的作用。结果,基因型C1克隆表现出比B2克隆显着更高的复制能力。与直觉相反,将 C1 克隆中的 A1762T/G1764A 突变 恢复为野生型进一步增强了其复制,表明该 突变 并不是其高复制适应性的主要驱动因素。总之,HBV 基因型 C1 具有比 B2 更大的固有复制能力,独立于经典的基础核心启动子 (BCP) 突变,为其与合并感染中严重疾病结果的关联提供了病毒学解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在从同时感染基因型 B 和 C 的患者中分离和表征个体 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 菌株,并直接比较它们的内在复制能力。
设计与对象
从 慢性乙型肝炎 患者身上分离出 HBV DNA。选择代表亚基因型 B2 (c17) 和 C1 (c21) 的两个克隆,对其进行测序并转染至 HepG2 细胞中。
核心方法
通过基因型特异性聚合酶链反应(PCR)确定基因型,并克隆全长基因组。通过Southern印迹和定量PCR评估病毒复制。通过定点诱变研究了常见 A1762T/G1764A 突变 在 C1 克隆中的作用。
主要结果
基因型C1克隆表现出比B2克隆显着更高的复制能力。与直觉相反,将 C1 克隆中的 A1762T/G1764A 突变 恢复为野生型进一步增强了其复制。
解释与创新
该研究为 HBV 基因型 C1 与合并感染中严重疾病结果的关联提供了病毒学解释,表明其复制优势独立于经典的基础核心启动子 突变。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解 HBV 基因型特异性复制动态,并可能有助于改进 监测 和基因型 C1 感染的靶向治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个患者的 HBV 毒株,并未考虑更广泛的人群变异性,但结果得到了直接复制测定和诱变实验的支持。

Intrinsic replication advantage of a Hepatitis B Virus genotype C strain is independent of the basal core promoter A1762T/G1764A mutation

Authors: Yihan Xiao, Xinjun Chen, Zongxin Li, Sheng Lei, Zhenbin Chen, Junqi Cai, Xiuji Cui
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to isolate and characterize individual hepatitis B virus (HBV) strains from a patient co-infected with genotype B and C, and to directly compare their intrinsic replication capacities. HBV DNA was isolated from a chronic hepatitis B patient. Genotypes were determined by genotype-specific polymerase chain reaction (PCR), and full-length genomes were cloned. Two clones representing subgenotype B2 (c17) and C1 (c21) were selected, sequenced and transfected into HepG2 cells. Viral replication was assessed via Southern blot and quantitative PCR. The role of the common Adenine (A)1762Thymine (T/)Guanine (G)1764A mutation in the C1 clone was investigated by site-directed mutagenesis. As a result, the genotype C1 clone exhibited significantly higher replication capacity than the B2 clone. Counterintuitively, reverting the A1762T/G1764A mutation in the C1 clone to wild-type further enhanced its replication, indicating this mutation was not the primary driver of its high replicative fitness. In conclusion, HBV genotype C1 possesses a greater inherent replication capacity than B2, independent of the classic basal core promoter (BCP) mutation, providing a virological explanation for its association with severe disease outcomes in co-infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to isolate and characterize individual hepatitis B virus (HBV) strains from a patient co-infected with genotype B and C, and to directly compare their intrinsic replication capacities.
Design and population
HBV DNA was isolated from a chronic hepatitis B patient. Two clones representing subgenotype B2 (c17) and C1 (c21) were selected, sequenced and transfected into HepG2 cells.
Core methods
Genotypes were determined by genotype-specific polymerase chain reaction (PCR), and full-length genomes were cloned. Viral replication was assessed via Southern blot and quantitative PCR. The role of the common A1762T/G1764A mutation in the C1 clone was investigated by site-directed mutagenesis.
Main results
The genotype C1 clone exhibited significantly higher replication capacity than the B2 clone. Counterintuitively, reverting the A1762T/G1764A mutation in the C1 clone to wild-type further enhanced its replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides a virological explanation for the association of HBV genotype C1 with severe disease outcomes in co-infection, showing that its replication advantage is independent of the classic basal core promoter mutation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the understanding of HBV genotype-specific replication dynamics and may contribute to improved surveillance and targeted therapeutic strategies for genotype C1 infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single patient's HBV strains and does not account for broader population variability, but the results are supported by direct replication assays and mutagenesis experiments.

自噬相关基因表达与 慢性乙型肝炎 和脂肪肝疾病的纤维化严重程度相关

Autophagy-related gene expression is associated with fibrosis severity in chronic hepatitis B and fatty liver disease
作者： Shuai Zhang, Jinghe Xie, Shoupei Liu, Haibin Wu, Chen Huang, Jing Yang, Yongjian Zhou, Na Yang, Yuyou Duan, Weili Gu
摘要中文翻译自噬调节肝细胞存活、星状细胞活化和炎症,但其在人类肝纤维化中的转录相关性尚不清楚。在 124 名 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的肝活检转录组中,20 个自噬相关基因在纤维化组织和非纤维化组织之间存在显着差异。共表达网络分析优先考虑 CCL2 、 MYC 、 CDKN1A 和 CAPN2,其表达与免疫细胞特征相关,并且在调整炎症等级、年龄和性别后与纤维化阶段呈正相关。探索性 15 基因神经网络分类器的受试者工作特征曲线 (AUC) 下的表观面积为 0.982;重新实施的开发程序的引导验证给出了 0.865 的乐观校正 AUC,并且分类器在来自 6 名健康个体和 6 名乙型肝炎相关肝硬化患者的全血中的 AUC 为 0.833(95% 置信区间,0.556-1.000)。在两个独立的非酒精性脂肪肝病 队列研究 中,晚期阶段 CCL2、CAPN2 和 CDKN1A 的表达高于轻度纤维化 (n = 72),而所有四种基因的表达在纤维化肝脏中均高于非纤维化肝脏 (n = 112),这主要是由于肝硬化样本。这些基因值得进一步研究作为纤维化严重程度的标志;它们的细胞来源和在纤维化中的作用尚不清楚。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究自噬在人类肝纤维化中的转录相关性,因为自噬在肝细胞存活、星状细胞活化和炎症中的作用已得到充分证实,但其与纤维化严重程度的关系仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 124 名 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的肝活检转录组,确定了 20 个自噬相关基因,这些基因在纤维化组织和非纤维化组织之间存在显着差异。它还检查了两名患有非酒精性脂肪肝的独立 队列研究。
核心方法
研究人员进行了共表达网络分析来对基因进行优先排序,并使用引导方法开发和验证了探索性 15 基因神经网络分类器。
主要结果
分类器的接收者操作特征曲线 (AUC) 下的表观面积为 0.982,但重新实施的开发程序的引导验证给出的乐观校正 AUC 为 0.865。在来自 6 名健康个体和 6 名乙型肝炎相关肝硬化患者的全血中,分类器的 AUC 为 0.833(95% 置信区间,0.556-1.000)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CCL2、CAPN2 和 CDKN1A 可能作为纤维化严重程度的潜在标志物,尽管它们的细胞来源和在纤维化中的作用仍不清楚。
科研与公卫意义
这些基因可以为未来纤维化严重程度标记物的研究提供信息,可能有助于开发与乙型肝炎相关的肝脏疾病的诊断工具。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于使用探索性分类器以及健康个体和肝硬化患者的样本量较小而受到限制,导致置信度评估适中。

Autophagy-related gene expression is associated with fibrosis severity in chronic hepatitis B and fatty liver disease

Authors: Shuai Zhang, Jinghe Xie, Shoupei Liu, Haibin Wu, Chen Huang, Jing Yang, Yongjian Zhou, Na Yang, Yuyou Duan, Weili Gu
Original abstract (English)
Autophagy regulates hepatocyte survival, stellate cell activation and inflammation, but its transcriptional correlates in human liver fibrosis are poorly defined. In liver biopsy transcriptomes from 124 patients with chronic hepatitis B, 20 autophagy-related genes differed nominally between fibrotic and non-fibrotic tissue. Co-expression network analysis prioritized CCL2 , MYC , CDKN1A and CAPN2 , whose expression correlated with immune cell signatures and was positively associated with fibrosis stage after adjustment for inflammation grade, age and sex. An exploratory 15-gene neural-network classifier had an apparent area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.982; bootstrap validation of a re-implemented development procedure gave an optimism-corrected AUC of 0.865, and the classifier had an AUC of 0.833 (95% confidence interval, 0.556–1.000) in whole blood from six healthy individuals and six with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis. In two independent cohorts with non-alcoholic fatty liver disease, expression of CCL2 , CAPN2 and CDKN1A , but not MYC , was higher in advanced than in mild fibrosis (n = 72), and that of all four genes was higher in fibrotic than in non-fibrotic liver (n = 112), largely owing to cirrhotic samples. These genes warrant further study as markers of fibrosis severity; their cellular sources and roles in fibrosis are unknown.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the transcriptional correlates of autophagy in human liver fibrosis, as autophagy's role in hepatocyte survival, stellate cell activation and inflammation is well established, yet its relationship with fibrosis severity remains poorly defined.
Design and population
The study analyzed liver biopsy transcriptomes from 124 patients with chronic hepatitis B, identifying 20 autophagy-related genes that differed nominally between fibrotic and non-fibrotic tissue. It also examined two independent cohorts with non-alcoholic fatty liver disease.
Core methods
The researchers performed co-expression network analysis to prioritize genes, and an exploratory 15-gene neural-network classifier was developed and validated using bootstrap methods.
Main results
The classifier had an apparent area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.982, but bootstrap validation of a re-implemented development procedure gave an optimism-corrected AUC of 0.865. In whole blood from six healthy individuals and six with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis, the classifier had an AUC of 0.833 (95% confidence interval, 0.556–1.000).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CCL2, CAPN2, and CDKN1A may serve as potential markers for fibrosis severity, although their cellular sources and roles in fibrosis remain unclear.
Scientific and public-health significance
These genes could inform future research on fibrosis severity markers, potentially aiding in the development of diagnostic tools for liver diseases associated with hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its use of an exploratory classifier and the small sample size of healthy individuals and those with cirrhosis, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染临床谱中的外周血自然杀伤细胞数量、表型和功能:范围界定综述

Peripheral Blood Natural Killer Cell Quantity, Phenotype, and Function Across the Clinical Spectrum of Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection: A Scoping Review
作者： Vania Petrina, Usman Hadi
摘要中文翻译本次范围审查旨在系统地绘制和严格审查关于 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染临床谱中外周血自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞的数量、表型和功能的现有证据,包括慢性 HBV 感染和肝炎、HBV 相关的肝纤维化和肝硬化以及 HBV 相关的肝细胞癌 (HCC)。尽管 NK 细胞有助于 抗病毒药物 免疫、免疫调节和肿瘤 监测,但有关其循环频率、表型特征和功能反应的研究结果仍然存在异质性。临床人群、疾病分类、抗病毒药物 治疗、血样处理、流式细胞术门控策略、测量分母和功能测定条件的差异可能会限制研究之间的直接比较。该审查将以 Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) 范围界定审查方法为指导,并根据 PRISMA 范围界定审查扩展 (PRISMA-ScR) 进行报告,同时额外关注使用 PRISMA-S 进行文献检索的透明报告。合格的证据主要包括原始人体研究,报告至少一种与慢性 HBV 感染或 HBV 相关肝病人群的数量、表型或功能相关的外周血 NK 细胞结果。只有在可以单独确定相关亚组结果的情况下,才会考虑涉及混合 HCC 病因学的研究进行 HBV 特异性合成。外周血检查结果将与肝内和肿瘤浸润 NK 细胞测量结果区分开来。该综述将绘制 NK 细胞频率和绝对计数、CD56bright 和 CD56dim 子集、激活和抑制受体谱以及功能测量(例如细胞因子产生、脱颗粒、细胞毒性和抗体依赖性反应)。将特别关注是否在可比人群、相同时间点和足够相似的实验条件下评估了定量和功能结果。注册前已对选定出版物进行了初步文献检索、书目数据收集、重复数据删除和有针对性的审查。注册将记录当前阶段的审查,并指定后续研究选择、数据图表、证据合成和方案修订的程序。早期的活动不会被视为前瞻性预注册。人工智能工具有助于初步的书目组织、方法规划和文献探索。它们的使用将被透明记录。有关研究资格、提取的证据、解释和最终结论的决定仍将接受人工审查和针对原始来源的验证。预期结果是一张证据图,确定了 HBV 相关临床状况的研究分布、NK 细胞数量、表型和功能的检查程度、方法异质性的主要来源以及证据有限或缺乏可比性的领域。这些发现旨在为未来的临床免疫学和肝病学研究以及 HBV 相关肝病中外周血 NK 细胞的更标准化研究的设计提供信息。

Peripheral Blood Natural Killer Cell Quantity, Phenotype, and Function Across the Clinical Spectrum of Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection: A Scoping Review

Authors: Vania Petrina, Usman Hadi
Original abstract (English)
This scoping review aims to systematically map and critically examine the available evidence on the quantity, phenotype, and function of peripheral blood natural killer (NK) cells across the clinical spectrum of chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, including chronic HBV infection and hepatitis, HBV-related liver fibrosis and cirrhosis, and HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Although NK cells contribute to antiviral immunity, immune regulation, and tumor surveillance, findings concerning their circulating frequency, phenotypic characteristics, and functional responses remain heterogeneous. Differences in clinical populations, disease classification, antiviral treatment, blood sample processing, flow cytometric gating strategies, measurement denominators, and functional assay conditions may limit direct comparisons between studies. The review will be guided by the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) methodology for scoping reviews and reported in accordance with the PRISMA Extension for Scoping Reviews (PRISMA-ScR), with additional attention to transparent reporting of literature searches using PRISMA-S. Eligible evidence will primarily comprise original human studies reporting at least one peripheral blood NK-cell outcome related to quantity, phenotype, or function in populations with chronic HBV infection or HBV-associated liver disease. Studies involving mixed HCC etiologies will be considered for HBV-specific synthesis only where relevant subgroup findings can be identified separately. Peripheral blood findings will be distinguished from intrahepatic and tumor-infiltrating NK-cell measurements. The review will map NK-cell frequencies and absolute counts, CD56bright and CD56dim subsets, activating and inhibitory receptor profiles, and functional measurements such as cytokine production, degranulation, cytotoxicity, and antibody-dependent responses. Particular attention will be given to whether quantitative and functional outcomes were assessed in comparable populations, at the same time points, and under sufficiently similar experimental conditions. Preliminary literature searching, bibliographic data collection, deduplication, and targeted examination of selected publications have already been undertaken before registration. The registration will document the current stage of the review and specify the procedures for subsequent study selection, data charting, evidence synthesis, and protocol amendments. Earlier activities will not be represented as prospectively preregistered. Artificial intelligence tools have assisted with preliminary bibliographic organization, methodological planning, and exploration of the literature. Their use will be documented transparently. Decisions concerning study eligibility, extracted evidence, interpretation, and final conclusions will remain subject to human review and verification against original sources. The anticipated outcome is an evidence map identifying the distribution of research across HBV-related clinical conditions, the extent to which NK-cell quantity, phenotype, and function have been examined, major sources of methodological heterogeneity, and areas where evidence is limited or insufficiently comparable. The findings are intended to inform future clinical immunology and hepatology research and the design of more standardized investigations of peripheral blood NK cells in HBV-associated liver disease.

The review will be guided by the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) methodology for scoping reviews and reported in accordance with the PRISMA Extension for Scoping Reviews (PRISMA-ScR), with additional attention to transparent reporting of literature searches using PRISMA-S.

Interpretation and novelty
Their use will be documented transparently.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

干扰素治疗可降低 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌高风险患者的肝细胞癌 发病率

Interferon therapy reduces incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with high risk of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Miao Hao, Chen-Rui Liu, Meng Zhang, Xia-Qing Zhou, Zi-Cheng Jiang, Xiao-Hong Gao, Xin Zhang, Wen-Jun Wang, Yi-Kai Wang, Xiao-Li Jia
摘要中文翻译背景我国HCC患者中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌(HCC)患者比例高达85.69%。目前的抗HBV药物包括核苷(酸)类似物(NAs)和干扰素(IFN)。目的 比较 NAs 和 IFN 治疗对 HBV 相关 HCC 高危患者 HCC 发生的影响。方法 我们纳入了 368 例 HBV 相关 HCC 高危患者,其中 120 例接受皮下注射 IFN(联合或不联合 NA),构成 IFN 组。其余 248 名患者被分配到 NAs 组,并接受单独 NAs 的 治疗 治疗。结果 对于 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者,经过 5 年 发病率 期后,IFN 组的 HCC 发病率 为 2.54%,而 NAs 组的 发病率 显着较高,为 25.74%(P 结论 与 NAs 相比,基于 IFN 的治疗显着降低了高风险患者的 HCC 风险)乙型肝炎病毒相关的肝癌。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决中国 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的高 发病率 问题,并评估干扰素 (IFN) 治疗与核苷类似物 (NA) 对高危患者 HCC 发生的影响。
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究对 368 名 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者进行,其中 120 名患者接受 IFN 治疗(联合或不联合 NA),248 名患者仅接受 NA 治疗。
核心方法
患者入组并随访 5 年 治疗 期。使用统计分析对 IFN 组和 NAs 组之间的 HCC 发病率 进行监测和比较以确定显着性。
主要结果
治疗治疗5年后,IFN组的HCC 发病率为2.54%,而NAs组的发病率显着较高,为25.74%(P < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明在高危 HBV 患者中,与单独使用 NA 相比,基于 IFN 的治疗可显着降低 HCC 风险,强调了一种新的治疗方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,基于 IFN 的治疗可能是减少 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者的 HCC 发病率 的更有效选择,可能为 治疗 指南和公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制,但由于 IFN 和 NA 组之间的清晰比较以及统计学上显着的结果,证据是强有力的。

Interferon therapy reduces incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with high risk of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Miao Hao, Chen-Rui Liu, Meng Zhang, Xia-Qing Zhou, Zi-Cheng Jiang, Xiao-Hong Gao, Xin Zhang, Wen-Jun Wang, Yi-Kai Wang, Xiao-Li Jia
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The proportion of patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is as high as 85.69% among HCC patients in China. Current anti-HBV drugs include nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) and interferon (IFN). AIM To compare the impact of NAs and IFN therapy on the occurrence of HCC in patients at high risk of HBV-related HCC. METHODS We enrolled 368 patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC, and 120 received subcutaneous injections of IFN, with or without NAs, constituting the IFN group. The remaining 248 patients were assigned to the NAs group, and received treatment with NAs alone. RESULTS For patients with a high risk of HBV-related HCC, after a 5-year treatment period, HCC incidence in the IFN group was 2.54%, while the NAs group showed a significantly higher incidence of 25.74% (P CONCLUSION IFN-based therapy, compared with NAs, significantly reduced the risk of HCC in patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the high incidence of HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in China and evaluate the impact of interferon (IFN) therapy versus nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) on HCC occurrence in high-risk patients.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on 368 patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC, with 120 patients receiving IFN therapy (with or without NAs) and 248 patients receiving NA therapy alone.
Core methods
Patients were enrolled and followed for a 5-year treatment period. HCC incidence was monitored and compared between the IFN group and the NAs group using statistical analysis to determine significance.
Main results
After 5 years of treatment, the HCC incidence in the IFN group was 2.54%, while the NAs group had a significantly higher incidence of 25.74% (P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that IFN-based therapy significantly reduces HCC risk compared to NAs alone in high-risk HBV patients, highlighting a novel therapeutic approach.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest that IFN-based therapy could be a more effective option for reducing HCC incidence in patients at high risk of HBV-related HCC, potentially informing treatment guidelines and public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the clear comparison between IFN and NAs groups and the statistically significant results.

可解释性驱动的特征工程可实现 慢性乙型肝炎 住院时紧凑且准确的多结果风险分层

Interpretability-driven feature engineering enables compact and accurate multi-outcome risk stratification at hospitalization for chronic hepatitis B
作者： Huiyi Zhang, Qingxian Song, Meng Jia, Keni Wu, Yong Zhao, Jun Yuan, Shu Su
摘要中文翻译临床人工智能 (AI) 通常会以预测性能与可解释性为代价。本研究开发了一种可解释性驱动的特征工程框架,用于 慢性乙型肝炎 住院时的多结果风险分层。共纳入 22,001 名患者的 36,024 条住院记录。集成基于 SHapley 加法解释 (SHAP) 的主效应和交互效应的框架用于构建紧凑的机器学习模型,用于对疾病无进展、肝硬化 (LC) 和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 进行分类。SHAP 主效应选择每个结果的前 15 个特征;TreeSHAP 交互选择了四个成对项。使用患者级数据分割、时间验证和独立外部验证以及校准和决策曲线分析来评估性能。全特征模型在对住院记录的疾病无进展、LC 和 HCC 进行分类方面分别实现了卓越的区分度(AUC:0.927、0.897 和 0.899)。相应的紧凑模型保持了很强的辨别力(AUC:0.911、0.882 和 0.890),特征减少了 80.8%。相互作用模型产生了微妙的 AUC 变化(AUC:0.912、0.881 和 0.891),同时表征了潜在的临床联合效应模式。时间验证产生的 AUC 分别为 0.863、0.827 和 0.847,而外部验证产生的 AUC 分别为 0.829、0.865 和 0.827。该框架为临床实践中的同期风险分层生成了紧凑而透明的模型,将预测准确性与多级可解释性无缝结合起来。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
临床人工智能 (AI) 通常会以预测性能与可解释性为代价。本研究开发了一种可解释性驱动的特征工程框架,用于 慢性乙型肝炎 住院时的多结果风险分层。
设计与对象
共纳入 22,001 名患者的 36,024 条住院记录。集成基于 SHapley 加法解释 (SHAP) 的主效应和交互效应的框架用于构建紧凑的机器学习模型,用于对疾病无进展、肝硬化 (LC) 和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 进行分类。
核心方法
SHAP 主效应选择每个结果的前 15 个特征;TreeSHAP 交互选择了四个成对项。
主要结果
全特征模型在对住院记录的疾病无进展、LC 和 HCC 进行分类方面分别实现了卓越的区分度(AUC:0.927、0.897 和 0.899)。相应的紧凑模型保持了很强的辨别力(AUC:0.911、0.882 和 0.890),特征减少了 80.8%。相互作用模型产生了微妙的 AUC 变化(AUC:0.912、0.881 和 0.891),同时表征了潜在的临床联合效应模式。
解释与创新
该框架为临床实践中的同期风险分层生成了紧凑而透明的模型,将预测准确性与多级可解释性无缝结合起来。
科研与公卫意义
该框架可以为开发用于 慢性乙型肝炎 患者风险分层的可解释人工智能工具提供信息,从而有可能改善临床决策和患者治疗结果。
局限与证据强度
使用患者级数据分割、时间验证和独立外部验证以及校准和决策曲线分析来评估性能。提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或数据收集相关的具体限制。

Interpretability-driven feature engineering enables compact and accurate multi-outcome risk stratification at hospitalization for chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Huiyi Zhang, Qingxian Song, Meng Jia, Keni Wu, Yong Zhao, Jun Yuan, Shu Su
Original abstract (English)
Clinical artificial intelligence (AI) often trades predictive performance against interpretability. This study develops an interpretability-driven feature engineering framework for multi-outcome risk stratification at hospitalization for chronic hepatitis B. A total of 36,024 hospitalization records from 22,001 patients were included. The framework integrating SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP)-based main and interaction effects was used to construct compact machine learning models for classifying disease non-progression, liver cirrhosis (LC), and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). SHAP main effects selected the top 15 features per outcome; TreeSHAP interactions selected four pairwise terms. Performance was evaluated using patient-level data splitting, temporal validation, and independent external validation, with calibration and decision-curve analyses. The full-feature models achieved superior discrimination (AUCs: 0.927, 0.897, and 0.899) for classifying hospitalization-recorded disease non-progression, LC, and HCC, respectively. The corresponding compact models maintained strong discrimination (AUCs: 0.911, 0.882, and 0.890) with 80.8% fewer features. Interaction models yielded subtle AUC shifts (AUCs: 0.912, 0.881, and 0.891) while characterizing potential clinical joint-effect patterns. Temporal validation yielded AUCs of 0.863, 0.827, and 0.847, while external validation yielded AUCs of 0.829, 0.865, and 0.827, respectively. The framework produced compact and transparent models for contemporaneous risk stratification in clinical practice, seamlessly uniting predictive accuracy with multi-level interpretability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Clinical artificial intelligence (AI) often trades predictive performance against interpretability. This study develops an interpretability-driven feature engineering framework for multi-outcome risk stratification at hospitalization for chronic hepatitis B.
Design and population
A total of 36,024 hospitalization records from 22,001 patients were included. The framework integrating SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP)-based main and interaction effects was used to construct compact machine learning models for classifying disease non-progression, liver cirrhosis (LC), and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).
Core methods
SHAP main effects selected the top 15 features per outcome; TreeSHAP interactions selected four pairwise terms.
Main results
The full-feature models achieved superior discrimination (AUCs: 0.927, 0.897, and 0.899) for classifying hospitalization-recorded disease non-progression, LC, and HCC, respectively. The corresponding compact models maintained strong discrimination (AUCs: 0.911, 0.882, and 0.890) with 80.8% fewer features. Interaction models yielded subtle AUC shifts (AUCs: 0.912, 0.881, and 0.891) while characterizing potential clinical joint-effect patterns.
Interpretation and novelty
The framework produced compact and transparent models for contemporaneous risk stratification in clinical practice, seamlessly uniting predictive accuracy with multi-level interpretability.
Scientific and public-health significance
The framework can inform the development of interpretable AI tools for risk stratification in chronic hepatitis B patients, potentially improving clinical decision-making and patient outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
Performance was evaluated using patient-level data splitting, temporal validation, and independent external validation, with calibration and decision-curve analyses. The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations related to the study design or data collection.

GAMBARAN HASIL PEMERIKSAAN 抗体 B 型肝炎 DAN 肝炎 C PADA IBU HAMIL DENGAN 方法免疫色谱法 DI PUSKESMAS SIDOARJO

GAMBARAN HASIL PEMERIKSAAN ANTIBODI HEPATITIS B DAN HEPATITIS C PADA IBU HAMIL DENGAN METODE IMUNOKROMATOGRAFI DI PUSKESMAS SIDOARJO
作者： Vernanda Amyarti Lailatul Syafitri, Evy Diah Woelansari, Museyaroh Museyaroh, Suahariyadi Suahariyadi
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎是传染病,可以在怀孕期间、分娩期间或产后由孕妇传播给婴儿。孕妇筛查检查对于预防垂直 传播 的早期发现工作非常重要。本研究旨在利用 Sidoarjo 社区健康中心的免疫层析方法确定孕妇的乙型肝炎抗体 (HBsAb) 和丙型肝炎抗体 (抗 HCV) 检测结果。本研究采用描述性研究设计。这项研究于2026年1月至2月在诗都阿佐社区健康中心的实验室进行,涉及40名接受产前护理(ANC)检查的孕妇。采用快速免疫层析法对受访者采集的血液样本进行检测,检测 HBsAb 和抗 HCV 抗体。结果显示,从年龄来看,20-35岁年龄段的受访者最多,占32人(80%)。根据孕龄,大多数受访者处于妊娠早期,共有 22 人(55%)。HBsAb检查结果显示,38名受访者(95%)无反应,2名受访者(5%)有反应。同时,抗HCV检查结果显示,所有40名受访者(100%)均无反应。作为预防措施,孕妇接受乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎筛查对于降低母婴 传播 风险非常重要。ABSTRAK 乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的治疗方法包括治疗乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎。请勿将其置于垂直方向。Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk mengetahui gambaran hasil pemeriksaan antibodi Hepatitis B (HBsAb) dan antibodi Hepatitis C (Anti-HCV) pada ibu hamil menggunakan metode imunokromatografi di Puskesmas Sidoarjo.杰尼斯·佩内利蒂安·杨·迪古纳坎·阿达拉桌面脚本。Penelitian dilakukan di Laboratorium Puskesmas Sidoarjo pada bulan Januari–Februari 2026 dengan jumlah responden sebanyak 40 ibu hamil yang melakukan pemeriksaan 产前护理 (ANC)。快速测试方法可检测 HBsAb 和抗 HCV 的反应。Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa berdasarkan usia,sebagian besar responden berada pada kelompok usia 20–35 tahun sebanyak 32 orang (80%)。Berdasarkan usia kehamilan,市长响应 berada pada 三个月 I sebanyak 22 orang (55%)。Hasil pemeriksaan HBsAb menunjukkan sebanyak 38 名回应者 (95%) 未回应,2 名回应者 (5%) 回应。Sementara itu,hasil pemeriksaan Anti-HCV menunjukkan seluruh responden 40 orang (100%) non reaktif.乙型肝炎可能是丙型肝炎的主要危险因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎是传染病,可以在怀孕期间、分娩期间或产后由孕妇传播给婴儿。孕妇筛查检查对于预防垂直 传播 的早期发现工作非常重要。
设计与对象
本研究采用描述性研究设计。这项研究于2026年1月至2月在诗都阿佐社区健康中心的实验室进行,涉及40名接受产前护理(ANC)检查的孕妇。
核心方法
采用快速免疫层析法对受访者采集的血液样本进行检测,检测 HBsAb 和抗 HCV 抗体。
主要结果
结果显示,从年龄来看,20-35岁年龄段的受访者最多,占32人(80%)。根据孕龄,大多数受访者处于妊娠早期,共有 22 人(55%)。HBsAb检查结果显示,38名受访者(95%)无反应,2名受访者(5%)有反应。同时,抗HCV检查结果显示,所有40名受访者(100%)均无反应。
解释与创新
该研究使用免疫层析方法提供了孕妇乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎抗体检测结果的概况,突出显示了两者的非反应性结果的 患病率。
科研与公卫意义
作为预防措施,孕妇接受乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎筛查对于降低母婴 传播 风险非常重要。

GAMBARAN HASIL PEMERIKSAAN ANTIBODI HEPATITIS B DAN HEPATITIS C PADA IBU HAMIL DENGAN METODE IMUNOKROMATOGRAFI DI PUSKESMAS SIDOARJO

Authors: Vernanda Amyarti Lailatul Syafitri, Evy Diah Woelansari, Museyaroh Museyaroh, Suahariyadi Suahariyadi
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C are infectious diseases that can be transmitted from pregnant women to their babies during pregnancy, childbirth, or after birth. Screening examinations in pregnant women are important as an early detection effort to prevent vertical transmission. This study aimed to determine the profile of Hepatitis B antibody (HBsAb) and Hepatitis C antibody (Anti-HCV) test results among pregnant women using the immunochromatographic method at the Sidoarjo Community Health Center. This study employed a descriptive research design. The research was conducted at the Laboratory of the Sidoarjo Community Health Center from January to February 2026, involving 40 pregnant women who attended antenatal care (ANC) examinations. Testing was performed using the rapid immunochromatographic method on blood samples collected from the respondents to detect HBsAb and Anti-HCV antibodies. The results showed that, based on age, most respondents were in the 20–35 years age group, accounting for 32 individuals (80%). Based on gestational age, the majority of respondents were in the first trimester, totaling 22 individuals (55%). The HBsAb examination results indicated that 38 respondents (95%) were non-reactive and 2 respondents (5%) were reactive. Meanwhile, the Anti-HCV examination results showed that all 40 respondents (100%) were non-reactive. It is important for pregnant women to undergo Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C screening as a preventive measure to reduce the risk of mother-to-child transmission. ABSTRAK Hepatitis B dan Hepatitis C merupakan penyakit infeksi yang beresiko menular dari ibu hamil kepada bayi selama kehamilan, persalinan, maupun setelah kelahiran. Pemeriksaan skrining pada ibu hamil penting dilakukan sebagai upaya deteksi dini untuk mencegah terjadinya penularan vertikal. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk mengetahui gambaran hasil pemeriksaan antibodi Hepatitis B (HBsAb) dan antibodi Hepatitis C (Anti-HCV) pada ibu hamil menggunakan metode imunokromatografi di Puskesmas Sidoarjo. Jenis penelitian yang digunakan adalah deskriptif. Penelitian dilakukan di Laboratorium Puskesmas Sidoarjo pada bulan Januari–Februari 2026 dengan jumlah responden sebanyak 40 ibu hamil yang melakukan pemeriksaan antenatal care (ANC). Pemeriksaan dilakukan menggunakan metode imunokromatografi rapid test terhadap sampel darah responden untuk mendeteksi HBsAb dan Anti-HCV. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa berdasarkan usia, sebagian besar responden berada pada kelompok usia 20–35 tahun sebanyak 32 orang (80%). Berdasarkan usia kehamilan, mayoritas responden berada pada trimester I sebanyak 22 orang (55%). Hasil pemeriksaan HBsAb menunjukkan sebanyak 38 responden (95%) non reaktif dan 2 responden (5%) reaktif. Sementara itu, hasil pemeriksaan Anti-HCV menunjukkan seluruh responden 40 orang (100%) non reaktif. Penting bagi ibu hamil untuk melakukan pemeriksaan Hepatitis B maupun Hepatitis C sebagai upaya pencegahan risiko penularan ibu hamil kepada bayi.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C are infectious diseases that can be transmitted from pregnant women to their babies during pregnancy, childbirth, or after birth. Screening examinations in pregnant women are important as an early detection effort to prevent vertical transmission.
Design and population
This study employed a descriptive research design. The research was conducted at the Laboratory of the Sidoarjo Community Health Center from January to February 2026, involving 40 pregnant women who attended antenatal care (ANC) examinations.
Core methods
Testing was performed using the rapid immunochromatographic method on blood samples collected from the respondents to detect HBsAb and Anti-HCV antibodies.
Main results
The results showed that, based on age, most respondents were in the 20–35 years age group, accounting for 32 individuals (80%). Based on gestational age, the majority of respondents were in the first trimester, totaling 22 individuals (55%). The HBsAb examination results indicated that 38 respondents (95%) were non-reactive and 2 respondents (5%) were reactive. Meanwhile, the Anti-HCV examination results showed that all 40 respondents (100%) were non-reactive.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides a profile of antibody test results for Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C among pregnant women using the immunochromatographic method, highlighting the prevalence of non-reactive results for both.
Scientific and public-health significance
It is important for pregnant women to undergo Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C screening as a preventive measure to reduce the risk of mother-to-child transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the limitations of the study or the strength of the evidence.

Journal: Journal of viral hepatitis  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/jvh.70245  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 33, 11, e70245
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

全球抗病毒药物疗法在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌中的应用不足及其生存结局:一项纳入78项研究和68,243例患者的系统综述和荟萃分析。

Global Underutilization of Antiviral Therapy and Survival Outcomes in Hepatitis B Virus-Related Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of 78 Studies and 68,243 Patients.
作者： Sahith Kudaravalli, Taotao Yan, Cheng-Hao Tseng, Yao-Chun Hsu, Xiaohe Li, Yuankai Wu, Bowu Chen, Xiangrong Gao, Sicheng Gao, Danfeng Ren
摘要中文翻译使用核苷(酸)类似物进行抗病毒药物治疗被推荐用于所有具有病毒血症的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝硬化的患者,无论其病毒载量或肝酶水平如何。然而,肝细胞癌(HCC)并不是美国HBV 治疗指南中的治疗标准。尽管之前的研究报告指出,在HBV相关HCC患者中,抗病毒药物治疗与改善生存率相关,但目前仍缺乏明确的指南。为了指导指南制定,我们估计了抗病毒药物治疗率及其与生存率和HCC复发率之间的关联。我们按照PRISMA指南,对2006年至2024年间发表的、报告了HBV相关HCC成人患者抗病毒药物治疗率、生存率和/或HCC复发率的研究进行了系统综述和荟萃分析。使用随机效应模型汇总研究估计值。初始共有4556项研究,其中178项进行了全文审查,最终分析了78项(共68,243名患者)。汇总的抗病毒药物治疗率(52项研究,41,345名患者)为53.1%。在报告了HCC前抗病毒药物治疗启动的研究中,17.2%的HBV相关HCC患者在HCC发生前接受了治疗。接受治疗(与未接受治疗相比)的患者3年和5年总生存率(OS)更高(80.4% vs. 46.8%,两者p < 0.01)。在非活动性HCC研究中,5年OS为78.0% vs. 56.4%(p < 0.01),而在活动性HCC研究中,5年OS为48.2% vs. 22.6%(p = 0.14)。在多变量荟萃回归分析中,抗病毒药物治疗仍与改善5年OS独立相关。HCC复发率没有显著差异。抗病毒药物治疗与改善长期生存率相关,但在HBV相关HCC中仍被低估使用。这些发现突显了护理中的关键差距,并强调了在HBV相关HCC患者中更早和更一致地启动抗病毒药物治疗的必要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 抗病毒药物 疗法在 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患者中的全球利用不足及其对生存结果的影响。核心问题是 抗病毒药物 疗法是否可以提高 HBV 相关 HCC 的生存率以及其在不同人群中的使用情况如何比较。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 2006 年至 2024 年间发表的涉及 68,243 名患者的 78 项研究进行的系统分析和荟萃分析。这些研究是根据 PRISMA 指南选择的,并使用随机效应模型来汇总研究估计值。该综述纳入了报告 抗病毒药物 治疗、患有 HBV 相关 HCC 的成人患者的生存率和/或 HCC 复发率的研究。
共识与结论
抗病毒药物 核苷(酸)类似物治疗可改善 HBV 相关 HCC 的长期生存率。与未经治疗的患者相比,接受治疗的患者具有更高的 3 年和 5 年总生存 (OS) 率。然而,抗病毒药物 疗法目前在美国还不是 HCC 的 治疗 标准,其在临床实践中的使用情况仍然报道不足。
争议与缺口
接受治疗和未接受治疗的患者之间的 HCC 复发率没有显着差异。此外,该审查强调了护理方面的一个关键差距,因为 抗病毒药物 疗法尽管具有潜在的益处,但尚未得到充分利用。证据还表明,抗病毒药物 治疗与生存之间的关联可能因 HCC 的活动性而异。
科研与实践启示
为了改善患者的治疗效果,HBV 相关 HCC 患者需要更早、更一致地开始 抗病毒药物。研究结果强调了修订现行 治疗 指南的重要性,将 抗病毒药物 疗法纳入治疗 HBV 相关 HCC 的标准,特别是在目前尚未充分利用的人群中。

Global Underutilization of Antiviral Therapy and Survival Outcomes in Hepatitis B Virus-Related Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of 78 Studies and 68,243 Patients.

Authors: Sahith Kudaravalli, Taotao Yan, Cheng-Hao Tseng, Yao-Chun Hsu, Xiaohe Li, Yuankai Wu, Bowu Chen, Xiangrong Gao, Sicheng Gao, Danfeng Ren
Original abstract (English)
Antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogues is recommended for patients with viremic hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related cirrhosis regardless of levels of viremia or liver enzymes. However, hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is not an antiviral treatment criterion in the U.S. HBV treatment guidelines, though prior studies have reported improved survival with antiviral treatment in HBV-related HCC. To inform guidelines, we estimated antiviral treatment rates and their association with survival and HCC recurrence rates in HBV-related HCC. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis of studies published between 2006 and 2024 that reported antiviral treatment, survival, and/or HCC recurrence rates in adults with HBV-related HCC, following PRISMA guidelines. Random-effects models pooled study estimates. Of the initial 4556 studies, 178 underwent full-text review, and 78 (68,243 patients) were analysed. The pooled antiviral treatment rate (52 studies, 41,345 patients) was 53.1%. In studies reporting pre-HCC antiviral initiation, 17.2% of HBV-related HCC patients received therapy before diagnosis. Treated (vs untreated) patients had higher 3- and 5-year OS (80.4% vs. 46.8%, both p < 0.01). Five-year OS was 78.0% vs. 56.4% in inactive HCC (p < 0.01) and 48.2% vs. 22.6% in active HCC (p = 0.14). Antiviral therapy remained independently associated with improved 5-year OS in multivariable meta-regression. There was no significant difference in HCC recurrence rates. Antiviral therapy is associated with improved long-term survival but remains underutilized in HBV-related HCC. These findings highlight a critical gap in care and underscore the need for earlier and more consistent antiviral initiation in patients with HBV-related HCC.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the global underutilization of antiviral therapy in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and its impact on survival outcomes. The central question is whether antiviral therapy improves survival in HBV-related HCC and how its utilization compares across different populations.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis and meta-analysis of 78 studies involving 68,243 patients, published between 2006 and 2024. The studies were selected following PRISMA guidelines, and random-effects models were used to pool study estimates. The review included studies that reported antiviral treatment, survival, and/or HCC recurrence rates in adults with HBV-related HCC.
Consensus and conclusions
Antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogues is associated with improved long-term survival in HBV-related HCC. Treated patients had higher 3- and 5-year overall survival (OS) rates compared to untreated patients. However, antiviral therapy is not currently a treatment criterion for HCC in the U.S., and its utilization remains underreported in clinical practice.
Controversies and gaps
There is no significant difference in HCC recurrence rates between treated and untreated patients. Additionally, the review highlights a critical gap in care, as antiviral therapy is underutilized despite its potential benefits. The evidence also suggests that the association between antiviral therapy and survival may vary depending on the activity of HCC.
Research and practice implications
To improve patient outcomes, there is a need for earlier and more consistent antiviral initiation in patients with HBV-related HCC. The findings emphasize the importance of revising current treatment guidelines to include antiviral therapy as a criterion for managing HBV-related HCC, particularly in populations where it is currently underutilized.

Journal: Clinical transplantation  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/ctr.70733  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 40, 11
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性或病毒血症肾脏移植:系统评价和荟萃分析。

Transplantation of Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Positive or Viremic Kidneys: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.
作者： Dhruv Ahuja, Beatrice P. Concepcion, Yuhong Yuan, Rachel C. Forbes, James Louis Rogers, Helen S. Te, Yvonne El Kassis, 袁育红
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述探讨了乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性供体的肾脏是否可以安全地移植到非病毒血症肾移植受者体内,以及与乙型肝炎病毒阴性供体肾脏相比,此类肾脏是否具有相似的患者和同种异体移植物存活率。核心问题涉及当前 抗病毒药物 预防背景下 HBV 阳性供体肾脏的安全性和生存能力。
证据基础与方法
该综述是一项系统综述和荟萃分析,检索了截至 2025 年 11 月的 PubMed 和 Embase。资格标准要求进行研究,对 HBV 阳性和 HBV 阴性肾脏的受者与报告的患者和同种异体移植物的存活率进行比较。系统评价纳入了 11 项研究,荟萃分析纳入了 6 项研究。荟萃分析汇总了移植后 1 年、3 年和 5 年患者和同种异体移植物存活的风险比,置信区间为 95%。
共识与结论
移植后 1、3 和 5 年,HBV 阳性和 HBV 阴性肾脏受者之间的同种异体移植存活风险比没有统计学差异,汇总估计值分别为 1 [0.98–1.01]、1.01 [0.98–1.04] 和 1.00 [0.91–1.09]。两组患者的生存率也相当。HBV 传播 的发生率较低,为 4.94%,特别是在使用预防措施以及受者在移植前进行免疫时。HBV 感染供体的肾脏与 HBV 阴性供体的肾脏具有相似的患者和同种异体移植存活率,可用于扩大供体库,特别是在适当预防的免疫 KTR 中。
争议与缺口
荟萃分析中的证据基础仅包括六项研究,这可能会限制普遍性。该综述没有报告按 HBV 基因型、供者病毒血症状态或具体预防方案进行的亚组分析,因此无法确定哪些供者或受者因素驱动任何残留的 传播 风险。没有报告五年以上长期结果的数据。
科研与实践启示
可以考虑将感染 HBV 的供体的肾脏移植到适当的受者体内,并进行 抗病毒药物 预防,从而扩大供体库。未来的研究应评估长期结果、HBV 基因型特异性效应和最佳预防策略,以进一步降低 传播 风险。

Transplantation of Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Positive or Viremic Kidneys: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.

Authors: Dhruv Ahuja, Beatrice P. Concepcion, Yuhong Yuan, Rachel C. Forbes, James Louis Rogers, Helen S. Te, Yvonne El Kassis, 袁育红
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: This systematic review and meta-analysis examines the evidence related to the use of HBV positive kidneys into non-viremic kidney transplant recipients (KTRs). METHODS: We searched PubMed and Embase through November 2025 and identified studies of recipients of hepatitis B surface antigen positive (HBsAg+) or viremic kidneys. Studies with a comparison group of KTRs of HBV negative kidneys that reported on patient and allograft survivals were included in the meta-analysis. The risk ratios (RR) for patient and allograft survivals were pooled to obtain the estimate and 95% confidence interval, at 1-, 3-, and 5-years post-transplant. Viral transmission including HBsAg development, HBV viremia, or hepatitis was also reviewed. RESULTS: Eleven studies were included in the systematic review and 6 in the meta-analysis. They included 638 KTRs of HBV positive kidneys and 2800 recipients of HBsAg negative non viremic kidneys. At 1, 3, and 5 years, the RR of allograft survival was not statistically different between the 2 groups and was 1 [0.98-1.01], 1.01 [0.98-1.04], and 1.00 [0.91-1.09] respectively. Similarly, the RR of patient survival was comparable between the 2 groups. The rate of HBV transmission was low (4.94%), especially when prophylaxis was used and when recipients were immune pre transplant. CONCLUSION: Kidneys from HBV infected donors confer similar patient and allograft survival than those from HBV negative donors. They may be used to expand the donor pool, in immune KTRs and with appropriate prophylaxis, in light of this emerging evidence of safety with very low reported rates of transmission.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review examines whether kidneys from hepatitis B surface antigen-positive donors can be safely transplanted into non-viremic kidney transplant recipients, and whether such kidneys confer similar patient and allograft survival compared to HBV-negative donor kidneys. The central question concerns the safety and viability of HBV-positive donor kidneys in the context of current antiviral prophylaxis.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic review and meta-analysis that searched PubMed and Embase through November 2025. Eligibility criteria required studies comparing recipients of HBV positive versus HBV negative kidneys with reported patient and allograft survivals. Eleven studies were included in the systematic review and six in the meta-analysis. The meta-analysis pooled risk ratios for patient and allograft survival at 1-, 3-, and 5-years post-transplant, with 95% confidence intervals.
Consensus and conclusions
At 1, 3, and 5 years post-transplant, the risk ratio for allograft survival was not statistically different between recipients of HBV positive and HBV negative kidneys, with pooled estimates of 1 [0.98–1.01], 1.01 [0.98–1.04], and 1.00 [0.91–1.09] respectively. Patient survival was also comparable between the two groups. The rate of HBV transmission was low at 4.94%, particularly when prophylaxis was used and when recipients were immune pre-transplant. Kidneys from HBV infected donors confer similar patient and allograft survival to those from HBV negative donors and may be used to expand the donor pool, especially in immune KTRs with appropriate prophylaxis.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence base includes only six studies in the meta-analysis, which may limit generalizability. The review did not report subgroup analyses by HBV genotype, donor viremia status, or specific prophylaxis regimens, leaving uncertainty about which donor or recipient factors drive any residual transmission risk. No data on long-term outcomes beyond five years were reported.
Research and practice implications
Kidneys from HBV infected donors can be considered for transplantation into appropriate recipients with antiviral prophylaxis, expanding the donor pool. Future research should evaluate longer-term outcomes, HBV genotype-specific effects, and optimal prophylaxis strategies to further reduce transmission risk.

HBeAg阴性慢性乙型肝炎中有限与无限核苷(酸)类似物治疗的对比:随机试验的荟萃分析

Finite Versus Indefinite Nucleos(t)ide Analogue Therapy in HBeAg‐Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Meta‐Analysis of Randomized Trials.
作者： Demver P Gomez, Wilmyr F Hababag
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究探讨有限疗程与连续疗程核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗在HBeAg阴性慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)成人中的疗效和安全性。核心问题是有限疗程是否能增加HBsAg丢失的可能性,同时维持病毒抑制。
证据基础与方法
本综述纳入了四项平行组随机对照试验(RCTs),比较非肝硬化、病毒抑制的HBeAg阴性成人中NA停药与继续治疗的效果。两名评审员独立筛选研究并使用Cochrane RoB 2评估偏倚风险。采用随机效应Mantel-Haenszel荟萃分析,结合Hartung-Knapp调整和Paule-Mandel估计方法,合并风险比(RRs)及其95%置信区间(CIs)。证据的确定性通过GRADE方法进行评估。
共识与结论
与连续治疗相比,有限疗程的NA治疗可能增加HBsAg丢失的概率,尽管证据确定性较低。研究发现,停药与HBsAg丢失的风险比为5.02(95% CI:0.47-53.47),相当于每1000名患者约有53例额外的HBsAg丢失。然而,有限疗程显著增加了病毒学复发(RR 23.09)和再次治疗(RR 21.94)的风险,同时降低了持续停药后的病毒学应答率(RR 0.46)。未报告肝功能失代偿或严重不良事件。
争议与缺口
国际指南对有限疗程与连续疗程NA治疗的推荐存在分歧。关于HBsAg丢失的证据确定性较低,且置信区间较宽,表明效果估计存在不确定性。此外,本研究未涉及长期结局或有限疗程对乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌或肝硬化的潜在影响。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究以明确有限疗程NA治疗的长期安全性和有效性,特别是其对HBV相关肝细胞癌和肝硬化的潜在影响。研究结果表明,有限疗程可能在专家中心作为实现功能性治愈的一种选择性策略,但需要更多数据支持其更广泛的应用。

Finite Versus Indefinite Nucleos(t)ide Analogue Therapy in HBeAg‐Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Meta‐Analysis of Randomized Trials.

Authors: Demver P Gomez, Wilmyr F Hababag
Original abstract (English)
Continuous nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) effectively maintains viral suppression but rarely results in functional cure. Finite NA therapy has been proposed to increase rates of HBsAg loss; however, international guidelines provide conflicting recommendations. This study compares the efficacy and safety of finite versus continuous NA therapy in HBeAg-negative adults using randomized controlled trials. We included parallel-group randomized controlled trials (RCTs) comparing nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) cessation versus continuation in non-cirrhotic, virally suppressed HBeAg-negative adults. Two reviewers independently screened studies and assessed risk of bias using Cochrane RoB 2. Random-effects Mantel-Haenszel meta-analyses with Hartung-Knapp adjustment and Paule-Mandel estimation were used to pool risk ratios (RRs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Certainty of evidence was assessed using the GRADE approach. Four RCTs (n = 357) met inclusion criteria. Discontinuation increased HBsAg loss vs. continuation (RR 5.02, 95% CI: 0.47-53.47; I 2 = 21.8%; low certainty), corresponding to ~53 additional losses per 1000 patients. However, finite therapy markedly increased virologic relapse (RR 23.09) and retreatment (RR 21.94) while reducing sustained off-therapy virologic response (RR 0.46). Post-cessation ALT flares were frequent but self-limited or resolved with retreatment. No hepatic decompensation or serious adverse events occurred. In monitored, non-cirrhotic adults, finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss despite predictable relapse and retreatment. With no evidence of severe harm, these findings support finite therapy as a selective, patient-centered strategy toward functional cure in expert centers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study investigates the efficacy and safety of finite versus continuous nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy in HBeAg-negative adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The central question is whether finite therapy increases the likelihood of HBsAg loss while maintaining viral suppression.
Evidence base and method
The review includes four parallel-group randomized controlled trials (RCTs) comparing NA cessation versus continuation in non-cirrhotic, virally suppressed HBeAg-negative adults. Two reviewers independently screened studies and assessed risk of bias using Cochrane RoB 2. Random-effects Mantel-Haenszel meta-analyses with Hartung-Knapp adjustment and Paule-Mandel estimation were used to pool risk ratios (RRs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Certainty of evidence was evaluated using the GRADE approach.
Consensus and conclusions
Finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss compared to continuous therapy, although the evidence is of low certainty. The study found that discontinuation of therapy was associated with a risk ratio of 5.02 (95% CI: 0.47-53.47) for HBsAg loss, corresponding to approximately 53 additional losses per 1000 patients. However, finite therapy significantly increased virologic relapse (RR 23.09) and retreatment (RR 21.94) while reducing sustained off-therapy virologic response (RR 0.46). No hepatic decompensation or serious adverse events were reported.
Controversies and gaps
International guidelines provide conflicting recommendations on finite versus continuous NA therapy. The evidence for HBsAg loss is of low certainty, and the wide confidence interval suggests uncertainty in the effect estimate. Additionally, the study does not address long-term outcomes or the impact of finite therapy on HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma or cirrhosis.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to clarify the long-term safety and efficacy of finite NA therapy, particularly in relation to HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma and cirrhosis. The findings suggest that finite therapy could be considered as a selective strategy in expert centers for achieving functional cure, but more data are required to support its broader implementation.

美国的 慢性乙型肝炎:当前形势、未满足的需求和未来方向

Chronic Hepatitis B in the United States: Current Landscape, Unmet Needs and Future Directions
作者： Chari A. Cohen, James Phillip Esteban, Shravani Bobde, Robert J. Wong
摘要中文翻译摘要 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染是美国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,与及时 诊断、治疗、监测和疾病管理相关的大量需求未得到满足。这篇叙述性综述探讨了美国慢性乙型肝炎的情况,包括患者护理方面的差距、不断发展的治疗前景、血清学生物标志物在改善乙型肝炎患者 (PLWHB) 患者的治疗结果和经历方面的临床效用。在美国,乙型肝炎的诊断和治疗仍然不足,目前的努力不足以实现世界卫生组织相关的 2030 年消除目标。美国肝病研究协会和美国传染病学会的最新指南代表了北美的观点,标志着向扩大 治疗 资格标准和共同决策的转变。核苷(酸)类似物 (NA) 是美国目前的护理标准,但单独使用 NA 很少能实现 功能性治愈。针对 功能性治愈 的新型有限疗法,例如反义寡核苷酸、小干扰 RNA、免疫调节剂以及组合或序贯疗法,正在研究中。由于慢性乙型肝炎的不可预测性和复杂性,定量乙型肝炎表面抗原等生物标志物可为 治疗 决策提供预后价值,并有助于指导个体化慢性乙型肝炎管理。PLWHB 经历心理影响和健康相关问题,包括对 传播 和 乙型肝炎病毒 相关并发症的恐惧。改善 CHB 管理的机会包括增加筛查和与护理的联系、开发更多针对 功能性治愈 的 治疗、采用血清学生物标志物来告知 治疗 决策以及优先考虑 PLWHB 的生活经历和偏好。

范围与问题
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染是美国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,与及时 诊断、治疗、监测和疾病管理相关的大量需求未得到满足。

这篇叙述性综述探讨了美国慢性乙型肝炎的情况,包括患者护理方面的差距、不断发展的治疗前景、血清学生物标志物在改善乙型肝炎患者 (PLWHB) 患者的治疗结果和经历方面的临床效用。

在美国,乙型肝炎的诊断和治疗仍然不足,目前的努力不足以实现世界卫生组织相关的 2030 年消除目标。

Chronic Hepatitis B in the United States: Current Landscape, Unmet Needs and Future Directions

Authors: Chari A. Cohen, James Phillip Esteban, Shravani Bobde, Robert J. Wong
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection represents a major public health challenge in the US, with substantial unmet needs related to timely diagnosis, treatment, monitoring and disease management. This narrative review explores the CHB landscape in the US, including gaps in patient care, the evolving therapeutic landscape, the clinical utility of serological biomarkers to improve outcomes and the experiences of people living with hepatitis B (PLWHB). Hepatitis B remains underdiagnosed and undertreated in the US, and current efforts are insufficient to meet the associated World Health Organization 2030 elimination targets. Latest guidelines from the American Association for the Study of Liver Diseases and the Infectious Diseases Society of America, representing a North American perspective, signify a shift towards expanded treatment eligibility criteria and shared decision‐making. Nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) are the current standard of care in the US, but functional cure is rarely achieved with NAs alone. Novel finite therapies targeting functional cure, such as antisense oligonucleotides, small interfering RNAs, immune modulators and combination or sequential regimens, are under investigation. Because of the unpredictable, complex nature of CHB, biomarkers such as quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen offer prognostic value for treatment decisions and could help guide individualised CHB management. PLWHB experience psychological impacts and health‐related concerns, including fear of transmission and hepatitis B virus‐related complications. Opportunities for improvement of CHB management include increased screening and linkage to care, development of more treatments targeting functional cure, adoption of serological biomarkers to inform treatment decisions and prioritising the lived experiences and preferences of PLWHB.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection represents a major public health challenge in the US, with substantial unmet needs related to timely diagnosis, treatment, monitoring and disease management.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review explores the CHB landscape in the US, including gaps in patient care, the evolving therapeutic landscape, the clinical utility of serological biomarkers to improve outcomes and the experiences of people living with hepatitis B (PLWHB).
Consensus and conclusions
Hepatitis B remains underdiagnosed and undertreated in the US, and current efforts are insufficient to meet the associated World Health Organization 2030 elimination targets.
Controversies and gaps
Opportunities for improvement of CHB management include increased screening and linkage to care, development of more treatments targeting functional cure, adoption of serological biomarkers to inform treatment decisions and prioritising the lived experiences and preferences of PLWHB.
Research and practice implications
Because of the unpredictable, complex nature of CHB, biomarkers such as quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen offer prognostic value for treatment decisions and could help guide individualised CHB management.
